## Cooperative Credit Associations Act {#cooperative-credit-associations-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1991, c. 48
:::

Assented to 1991-12-13

An Act to revise and amend the law governing cooperative credit
associations and to provide for related and consequential matters
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-96535 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Cooperative Credit Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01).

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-96538 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-96539 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In this Act,

[[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to an association,
    means the relationships among the association and its affiliates and
    the members, shareholders, directors and officers of the association
    and its affiliates, but does not include the business of the
    association or any of its affiliates; ([affaires
    internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is affiliated
    with another entity within the meaning of section
    6; ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Financial Consumer Agency
    of Canada established under section 3 of the [Financial Consumer
    Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[annual statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[annual statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the annual financial
    statement of an association within the meaning of paragraph
    292(1)(a); ([rapport annuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate referred
    to in section 14; ([association]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a firm of
    accountants; ([vérificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a security, means the
    person in possession of a security payable to bearer or endorsed in
    blank; ([porteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bearer form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bearer form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
    a security in bearer form as determined in accordance with
    subsection 90(2); ([titre au porteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes ownership
    through one or more trustees, legal representatives, agents or other
    intermediaries; ([véritable propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"} et [propriété effective]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an incorporated body
    wherever or however incorporated; ([personne
    morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an association, means
    an agency, the head office and any other office of the
    association; ([bureau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Canadian financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    financial institution that is incorporated or formed by or under an
    Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province; ([institution
    financière canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[central cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[central cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    cooperative credit society incorporated by or under an Act of the
    legislature of a province, one of whose principal purposes is to
    provide liquidity support to local cooperative credit societies, and

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose membership consists wholly or primarily of
      local cooperative credit societies, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose directors are wholly or primarily persons
      elected or appointed by local cooperative credit societies,

    but does not include a deposit protection agency; ([coopérative de
    crédit centrale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [centrale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[central securities register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[central securities register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[securities
    register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the register referred to in
    section 245; ([registre central des valeurs
    mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [registre des valeurs
    mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commissioner of the
    Financial Consumer Agency of Canada appointed under section 4 of the
    [Financial Consumer Agency of Canada
    Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
    individual, means a person who is cohabiting with the individual in
    a conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
    least one year; ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an association
    or any matter concerning an association, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member or former member of an association,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registered holder or beneficial owner, and a
      former registered holder or beneficial owner, of a security of an
      association or any of its affiliates,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer, or a former director or
      officer, of an association or any of its affiliates, or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other person who, in the discretion of a
      court, is a proper person to make an application under section
      318, 322 or 469; ([plaignant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[consumer provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[consumer provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a provision
    referred to in paragraph (b) or (b.1) of the definition [[consumer
    provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 2 of the [Financial
    Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([disposition
    visant les consommateurs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    cooperative corporation one of whose principal purposes is to
    provide financial services to its members; ([coopérative de
    crédit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cooperative corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cooperative corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body
    corporate organized and operated on cooperative
    principles; ([coopérative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Ontario, the Superior Court of
      Justice,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Quebec, the Superior Court of
      the Province,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of Nova Scotia, British
      Columbia, and Prince Edward Island, the Supreme Court of the
      Province,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of New Brunswick, Manitoba,
      Saskatchewan and Alberta, the Court of Queen's Bench for the
      Province,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Newfoundland and Labrador, the
      Trial Division of the Supreme Court of the Province, and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Supreme Court of Yukon, the Supreme Court of
      the Northwest Territories and the Nunavut Court of
      Justice; ([tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the court to which an
    appeal lies from a decision or order of a court; ([cour
    d'appel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond, debenture,
    note or other evidence of indebtedness of an entity, whether secured
    or unsecured; ([titre de créance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[delegate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delegate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person appointed or
    elected to represent a member of an association at a meeting of
    members; ([délégué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[deposit protection agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[deposit protection agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity
    established

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to provide or administer a stabilization or
      mutual aid fund for local cooperative credit societies,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to assist in the payment of any losses incurred
      by the members of a local cooperative credit society in the
      liquidation of the society, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to provide deposit insurance for members of local
      cooperative credit societies; ([agence
      d'assurance-dépôts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person occupying
    the position of director, by whatever name called, of a body
    corporate, and [[board of directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
    [[directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to the directors of a body
    corporate as a body; ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"},
    [conseil d'administration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [conseil]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate, trust,
    partnership, fund, an unincorporated organization, Her Majesty in
    right of Canada or of a province, an agency of Her Majesty in either
    of such rights and the government of a foreign country or any
    political subdivision thereof and any agency
    thereof; ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[federal credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01); ([coopérative de
    crédit fédérale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[federal league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cooperative
    corporation incorporated under an Act of Parliament whose membership
    consists wholly or primarily of federal credit unions and whose
    principal purpose is the provision of administrative, technical,
    research and consultative services, and goods related to those
    services, to any cooperative credit society or to persons intending
    to organize or operate such a society; ([confédération
    fédérale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person acting in a
    fiduciary capacity, and includes a personal representative of a
    deceased person; ([représentant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an association,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bank or an authorized foreign bank within the
      meaning of section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01),

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate to which the [Trust and Loan
      Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an insurance company or a fraternal benefit
      society incorporated or formed under the [Insurance Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8),

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trust, loan or insurance corporation
      incorporated by or under an Act of the legislature of a province,

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative credit society incorporated and
      regulated by or under an Act of the legislature of a province,

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed by or
      under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province and
      that is primarily engaged in dealing in securities, including
      portfolio management and investment counselling, and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a foreign institution; ([institution
      financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engaged in the business of banking, the trust,
      loan or insurance business, the business of a cooperative credit
      society or the business of dealing in securities or is otherwise
      engaged primarily in the business of providing financial services,
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} incorporated or formed otherwise than by or under
      an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a
      province; ([institution étrangère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Cooperative Credit
    Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), chapter C-41 of the Revised
    Statutes of Canada, 1985; ([loi antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[former-Act association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[former-Act association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian
    Co-operative Credit Society Limited, a corporation incorporated by
    chapter 58 of the Statutes of Canada, 1952-53; ([association
    antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[form of proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[form of proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a written or printed
    form that, when completed and executed by or on behalf of a
    shareholder, constitutes a proxy; ([formulaire de
    procuration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[guarantee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[guarantee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a letter of
    credit; ([garantie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[head office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[head office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the office required to be
    maintained by an association pursuant to section
    234; ([siège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a security certificate, the person
      in possession of the certificate issued or endorsed to that person
      or to bearer or in blank,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ownership of a membership
      share, the person referred to in section 7, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ownership of a share, the
      shareholder of the share within the meaning of section
      8; ([détenteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[holding body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[holding body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a holding body
    corporate within the meaning of section 4; ([société
    mère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, when used with reference to a
    body corporate that is incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament
    or of the legislature of a province, also refers to a body corporate
    that is continued by or under any such Act; ([constitué en personne
    morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [constitué]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[incorporating instrument]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporating instrument]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the special
    Act, letters patent, instrument of continuance or other constating
    instrument by which a body corporate was incorporated or continued
    and includes any amendment to or restatement of the constating
    instrument; ([acte constitutif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an association,
    means a person who applied for letters patent to incorporate the
    association; ([fondateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means the
    entity that issues or issued the
    security; ([émetteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2014, c. 39, s.
    278\]]{.Repealed}

[[letters patent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[letters patent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an instrument
    authorized to be issued under this Act, means letters patent in a
    form approved by the Superintendent; ([lettres
    patentes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[local cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[local cooperative credit society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    cooperative credit society incorporated by or under an Act of the
    legislature of a province

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose members consist substantially of
      individuals, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose principal purpose is to receive deposits
      from, and make loans to, its members; ([coopérative de crédit
      locale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [coopérative
      locale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an association, means
    a person who is a member of the association; ([Version anglaise
    seulement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[members register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[members register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the register referred
    to in section 49; ([registre des associés]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[membership share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[membership share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest in the
    equity of an association that confers the rights referred to in
    subsection 67(2); ([parts sociales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Finance; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[minor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[minor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    applicable provincial law and in the absence of any such law has the
    same meaning as the word "child" in the United Nations Convention on
    the Rights of the Child adopted in the United Nations General
    Assembly on November 20, 1989; ([mineur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a body corporate, a chief
      executive officer, president, vice-president, secretary,
      controller, treasurer and any other natural person designated as
      an officer of the body corporate by by-law or by resolution of the
      directors of the body corporate, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other entity, any natural
      person designated as an officer of the entity by by-law, by
      resolution of the members thereof or
      otherwise; ([dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[order form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[order form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
    a security in order form as determined in accordance with subsection
    90(3); ([titre à ordre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
    passed by a majority of the votes cast by or on behalf of the
    members who voted in respect of the resolution; ([résolution
    ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[patronage allocation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[patronage allocation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an amount that an
    association allocates among and credits or pays to its members or to
    its member and non-member patrons based on the business done by them
    with or through the association, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a patronage refund, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an allocation in proportion to
      borrowing; ([ristourne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person, an entity or
    a personal representative; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    stands in place of and represents another person and, without
    limiting the generality of the foregoing, includes, as the
    circumstances require, a trustee, an executor, an administrator, a
    committee, a guardian, a tutor, a curator, an assignee, a receiver,
    an agent or an attorney of any person, but does not include a
    delegate; ([représentant personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by
    regulation; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[provincial league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[provincial league]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cooperative
    corporation incorporated under an Act of the legislature of a
    province whose membership consists wholly or primarily of local
    cooperative credit societies and whose principal purpose is the
    provision of administrative, technical, research and consultative
    services, and goods related to those services, to any cooperative
    credit society or to persons intending to organize or operate such a
    society; ([confédération provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a completed and executed form of
    proxy by means of which a shareholder appoints a proxyholder to
    attend and act on the shareholder's behalf at a meeting of
    shareholders; ([procuration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[proxyholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[proxyholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person appointed by
    proxy to attend and act on behalf of a shareholder at a meeting of
    shareholders; ([fondé de pouvoir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[real property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[real property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a leasehold interest
    in real property; ([biens immeubles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recorded address]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recorded address]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a member of an association, the
      latest postal address of the member according to the members
      register of the association,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a shareholder of an association,
      the latest postal address of the person according to the central
      securities register of the association, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other person in respect of a
      retail association, the latest postal address of the person
      according to the records of the branch concerned; ([adresse
      enregistrée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security,
    means a security in registered form as determined in accordance with
    subsection 90(4); ([titre nominatif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regulatory capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regulatory capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
    association, has the meaning given to that expression by the
    regulations; ([capital réglementaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person who
    is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen ordinarily resident in Canada,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen not ordinarily resident in
      Canada who is a member of a prescribed class of persons, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a permanent resident within the meaning of
      subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
      Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and ordinarily resident in Canada, except a
      permanent resident who has been ordinarily resident in Canada for
      more than one year after the time at which the individual first
      became eligible to apply for Canadian citizenship; ([résident
      canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[residential property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[residential property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means real property
    consisting of buildings that are used, or are to be used, to the
    extent of at least one half of the floor space thereof, as one or
    more private dwellings; ([immeuble résidentiel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[retail association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for the purpose of any
    particular provision of this Act, means an association as defined in
    the regulations; ([association de détail]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[securities underwriter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[securities underwriter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who,
    as principal, agrees to purchase securities with a view to the
    distribution of the securities or who, as agent for a body corporate
    or other person, offers for sale or sells securities in connection
    with a distribution of the securities, and includes a person who
    participates, directly or indirectly, in a distribution of
    securities, other than a person whose interest in the distribution
    of securities is limited to receiving a distributor's or seller's
    commission payable by a securities underwriter; ([souscripteur à
    forfait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a body corporate, a share of any
      class of shares of the body corporate or a debt obligation of the
      body corporate, and includes a warrant of the body corporate, but
      does not include a deposit with a financial institution or any
      instrument evidencing such a deposit or, for greater certainty, a
      membership share, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other entity, any ownership
      interest in or debt obligation of the
      entity; ([titre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [valeur
      mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest in or
    charge on property by way of mortgage, lien, pledge or otherwise
    taken by a creditor or guarantor to secure the payment or
    performance of an obligation; ([sûreté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
    deliver; ([envoyer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of shares, means a
    division of a class of shares; ([série]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include a membership
    share; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[significant interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[significant interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a significant
    interest determined in accordance with section 9; ([intérêt
    substantiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution passed
    by a majority of not less than two thirds of the votes cast by or on
    behalf of the persons who voted in respect of that
    resolution; ([résolution extraordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[subordinated indebtedness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[subordinated indebtedness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    instrument evidencing an indebtedness of an association that by its
    terms provides that the indebtedness will, in the event of the
    insolvency or winding-up of the association, be subordinate in right
    of payment to all deposit liabilities of the association and all
    other liabilities of the association except those that, by their
    terms, rank equally with or are subordinate to such
    indebtedness; ([titre secondaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is a
    subsidiary of another entity within the meaning of section
    5; ([filiale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[substantial investment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[substantial investment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substantial
    investment determined in accordance with section 12; ([intérêt de
    groupe financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Superintendent of
    Financial Institutions appointed pursuant to the [Office of the
    Superintendent of Financial Institutions
    Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7); ([surintendant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, means any
    sale or disposition of securities for valuable
    consideration; ([opération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, includes
    a transmission by operation of law; ([transfert]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[voting share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[voting share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share of any class of
    shares of a body corporate carrying voting rights under all
    circumstances or by reason of an event that has occurred and is
    continuing or by reason of a condition that has been
    fulfilled. ([action avec droit de vote]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, ss. 2, 496, 497
- 1992, c. 51, s. 31
- 1998, c. 30, ss. 13(F), 15(E)
- 1999, c. 3, s. 24, c. 28, s. 115
- 2000, c. 12, s. 84
- 2001, c. 9, s. 248, c. 27, s. 234
- 2002, c. 7, s. 136(E)

- 2010, c. 12, s. 2114
- 2012, c. 5, s. 104
- 2014, c. 39, s. 278
- 2015, c. 3, s. 43
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-96670 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- distributing association

- **[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations respecting the determination
  of what constitutes a distributing association for the purposes of
  this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- association

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of an association, the
  Superintendent may determine that it is not or was not a distributing
  association if the Superintendent is satisfied that the determination
  would not prejudice any of its security holders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- class of associations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may determine that members of a
  class of associations are not or were not distributing associations if
  the Superintendent is satisfied that the determination would not
  prejudice any security holder of a member of the class.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 141
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person controls a body corporate if securities of
    the body corporate to which are attached more than 50 per cent of
    the votes that may be cast to elect directors of the body corporate
    are beneficially owned by the person and the votes attached to those
    securities are sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the
    directors of the body corporate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person controls an unincorporated entity, other
    than a limited partnership, if more than 50 per cent of the
    ownership interests, however designated, into which the entity is
    divided are beneficially owned by that person and the person is able
    to direct the business and affairs of the entity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the general partner of a limited partnership
    controls the limited partnership;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person controls a cooperative corporation if the
    person has the right to exercise more than 50 per cent of the votes
    that may be cast at an annual meeting or to appoint or elect the
    majority of the directors of the corporation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person controls an entity if the person has any
    direct or indirect influence that, if exercised, would result in
    control in fact of the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed control

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who controls an entity is deemed to control
  any entity that is controlled, or deemed to be controlled, by the
  entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed control

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is deemed to control, within the meaning of
  paragraph (1)(a), (b) or (d), an entity if the aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any securities of the entity that are beneficially
    owned by that person, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any securities of the entity that are beneficially
    owned by any entity controlled by that person

  is such that, if that person and all of the entities referred to in
  paragraph (b) that beneficially own securities of the entity were one
  person, that person would control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, for any purpose of any provision of
  this Act that refers to control within the meaning of paragraph
  (1)(e), make guidelines respecting what constitutes such control,
  including guidelines describing the policy objectives that the
  guidelines and the relevant provisions of the Act are intended to
  achieve and, if any such guidelines are made, the reference to
  paragraph (1)(e) in that provision shall be interpreted in accordance
  with the guidelines.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 3
- 2001, c. 9, s. 249
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding body corporate

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** A body corporate is the
holding body corporate of any entity that is its subsidiary.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 4
- 2001, c. 9, s. 250
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** An entity is a subsidiary
of another entity if it is controlled by the other entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 5
- 2001, c. 9, s. 250
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated entities

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One
  entity is affiliated with another entity if one of them is controlled
  by the other or both are controlled by the same person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 9, s. 251\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 6
- 2001, c. 9, s. 251
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, a person is a holder of a membership share of an
  association when, according to the members register of the
  association, the person is the owner of the membership share or is
  entitled to be entered in the members register or like record of the
  association as the owner of the membership share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holder of a membership share

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Act to the holding of a
  membership share by or in the name of a person is a reference to the
  fact that the person is registered or is entitled to be registered in
  the members register or like record of the association as the holder
  of that share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shareholder

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, a person is a shareholder of a body corporate
  when, according to the securities register of the body corporate, the
  person is the owner of one or more shares of the body corporate or is
  entitled to be entered in the securities register or like record of
  the body corporate as the owner of the share or shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holder of a share

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Act to the holding of a share by
  or in the name of any person is a reference to the fact that the
  person is registered or is entitled to be registered in the securities
  register or like record of the body corporate as the holder of that
  share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Significant interest

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person
  has a significant interest in a class of shares of an association
  where the aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any shares of that class beneficially owned by the
    person, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any shares of that class beneficially owned by
    entities controlled by the person

  exceeds 10 per cent of all of the outstanding shares of that class of
  shares of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing significant interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a significant interest in a class of
  shares of an association increases that significant interest in the
  class of shares where the person or any entity controlled by the
  person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional shares
    of that class, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns shares of that class,

  in such number as to increase the percentage of shares of that class
  that are beneficially owned by the person and by any entities
  controlled by the person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting in concert --- members' rights to
vote

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 52, where two or more members have agreed,
  pursuant to any agreement, commitment or understanding, whether formal
  or informal, verbal or written, to act jointly or in concert in
  respect of the exercise of any right to vote at a meeting of members,
  those members shall be deemed to be a single member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, members shall be
  presumed not to have agreed to act jointly or in concert solely by
  reason of the fact that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their membership voting rights are vested in the
    same delegate or delegates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they exercise their membership voting rights in the
    same manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where in the opinion of the Superintendent it is
  reasonable to conclude that an agreement, commitment or understanding
  referred to in subsection (1) exists by or among two or more members,
  the Superintendent may designate those members as members who have
  agreed to act jointly or in concert.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A member contravenes section 52 if the member agrees
  to act jointly or in concert with one or more other members in such a
  manner that a deemed single member contravenes that section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 10
- 2007, c. 6, s. 136
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting in concert --- shares

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of Part VIII, where two or more persons have agreed,
  pursuant to any agreement, commitment or understanding, whether formal
  or informal, verbal or written, to act jointly or in concert in
  respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares of an association that they beneficially
    own,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that they
    beneficially own of any entity that beneficially owns shares of an
    association, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that they
    beneficially own of any entity that controls any entity that
    beneficially owns shares of an association,

  those persons shall be deemed to be a single person who is acquiring
  beneficial ownership of the aggregate number of shares of the
  association or shares or ownership interests of the entity that are
  beneficially owned by them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without limiting the generality of subsection (1),
  any agreement, commitment or understanding by or between two or more
  persons who beneficially own shares of an association or shares or
  ownership interests of any entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or
  (c),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whereby any of them or their nominees may veto any
    proposal put before the board of directors of the association, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to which no proposal put before the board
    of directors of the association may be approved except with the
    consent of any of them or their nominees,

  shall be deemed to be an agreement, commitment or understanding
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, persons shall be
  presumed not to have agreed to act jointly or in concert solely by
  reason of the fact that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one is the proxyholder of one or more of the others
    in respect of shares or ownership interests referred to in
    subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they vote the voting rights attached to shares or
    ownership interests referred to in subsection (1) in the same
    manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where in the opinion of the Superintendent it is
  reasonable to conclude that an agreement, commitment or understanding
  referred to in subsections (1) and (2) exists by or among two or more
  persons, the Superintendent may designate those persons as persons who
  have agreed to act jointly or in concert.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person contravenes a provision of Part VIII if the
  person agrees to act jointly or in concert with one or more other
  persons in such a manner that a deemed single person contravenes the
  provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 11
- 2007, c. 6, s. 137
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substantial investment in body corporate

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person has a substantial investment in a body corporate where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the voting rights attached to the aggregate of any
    voting shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the person
    and by any entities controlled by the person exceed 10 per cent of
    the voting rights attached to all of the outstanding voting shares
    of the body corporate or, in the case of a cooperative corporation,
    the person, together with any entities controlled by the person, has
    the right to exercise more than 10 per cent of the votes that may be
    cast at an annual meeting; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of any shares of the body corporate
    beneficially owned by the person and by any entities controlled by
    the person represents ownership of greater than 25 per cent of the
    equity of the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing substantial investment in
  body corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in a body
  corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) increases that substantial
  investment when the person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional voting
    shares of the body corporate in such number as to increase the
    percentage of voting rights attached to the aggregate of the voting
    shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the person and by
    any entities controlled by the person or, in the case of a
    cooperative corporation, obtains the right to exercise more than 10
    per cent of the votes that may be cast at an annual meeting; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires, as the case may be,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} control of any entity that beneficially owns any
      voting shares of the body corporate in such number as to increase
      the percentage of voting rights attached to the aggregate of the
      voting shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the
      person and by any entities controlled by the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the body corporate is a cooperative
      corporation, control of any entity that has the right to exercise
      one or more votes that may be cast at an annual meeting of the
      cooperative corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in a body
  corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) increases that substantial
  investment when the person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional shares
    of the body corporate in such number as to increase the percentage
    of the equity of the body corporate represented by the aggregate of
    the shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the person
    and by any entities controlled by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns any shares of the body corporate in such number as to increase
    the percentage of the equity of the body corporate represented by
    the aggregate of the shares of the body corporate beneficially owned
    by the person and by any entities controlled by the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New substantial investment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where a person has a substantial investment in a
    body corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) and the person, or any
    entity controlled by the person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} purchases or otherwise acquires beneficial
      ownership of shares of the body corporate, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
      owns shares of the body corporate

    in such number as to cause the equity of the body corporate
    represented by the aggregate of the shares of the body corporate
    beneficially owned by the person and by any entities controlled by
    the person to exceed 25 per cent of the equity of the body
    corporate, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where a person has a substantial investment in a
    body corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) and the person or any
    entity controlled by the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} purchases or otherwise acquires beneficial
      ownership of voting shares of the body corporate or, where the
      body corporate is a cooperative corporation, purchases or
      otherwise acquires beneficial ownership of membership shares of
      the cooperative corporation, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
      owns voting shares of the body corporate or, where the body
      corporate is a cooperative corporation, acquires control of any
      entity that beneficially owns membership shares of the cooperative
      corporation,

    in such number as to cause the voting rights attached to the
    aggregate of the voting shares beneficially owned by the person and
    by any entities controlled by the person to exceed 10 per cent of
    the voting rights attached to all the outstanding voting shares of
    the body corporate or, where the body corporate is a cooperative
    corporation, in such number that the person, together with any
    entities controlled by the person, has the right to exercise more
    than 10 per cent of the votes that may be cast at an annual meeting
    of the cooperative corporation,

  the acquisition is deemed to cause the person to increase a
  substantial investment in the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substantial investment in
  unincorporated entity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person has a substantial investment in an
  unincorporated entity where the aggregate of any ownership interests,
  however designated, into which the entity is divided, beneficially
  owned by the person and by any entities controlled by the person
  exceeds 25 per cent of all of the ownership interests into which the
  entity is divided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing substantial investment in
  unincorporated entities

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in an
  unincorporated entity increases that substantial investment when the
  person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional
    ownership interests in the unincorporated entity in such number as
    to increase the percentage of ownership interests in the
    unincorporated entity beneficially owned by the person and by any
    entities controlled by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns ownership interests in the unincorporated entity in such number
    as to increase the percentage of ownership interests beneficially
    owned by the person and by any entities controlled by the person.

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2005, c. 54,
s. 142\]]{.Repealed}

[Previous
Version](section-13-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

39, s. 279\]]{.Repealed}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-96803 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** This Act applies to the
former-Act association, and to every body corporate incorporated or
formed by or under this Act, so long as it is not discontinued under
this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 14
- 2001, c. 9, s. 253
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflicting provisions

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** Where there is a conflict
or inconsistency between a provision of this Act and a provision of the
incorporating instrument of the former-Act association, the provision of
this Act prevails.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Status and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-96810 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate powers

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association has the capacity of a natural person and, subject to this
  Act, the rights, powers and privileges of a natural person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers restricted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall not carry on any business or
  exercise any power that it is restricted by this Act from carrying on
  or exercising, or exercise any of its powers in a manner contrary to
  this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business in Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association may carry on business throughout
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers outside Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, an association has the capacity
  to carry on its business, conduct its affairs and exercise its powers
  in any jurisdiction outside Canada to the extent and in the manner
  that the laws of that jurisdiction permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No invalidity

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** No act of an association,
including any transfer of property to or by an association, is invalid
by reason only that the act or transfer is contrary to the association's
incorporating instrument or this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-law not necessary

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** It is not necessary for
an association to pass a by-law in order to confer any particular power
on the association or its directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No personal liability

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** The members and
shareholders of an association are not, by reason only of holding
membership shares or shares of the association, liable for any
liability, act or default of the association except as otherwise
provided by this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No constructive notice

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** No person is affected by
or is deemed to have notice or knowledge of the contents of a document
concerning an association by reason only that the document has been
filed with the Superintendent or the Minister or is available for
inspection at an office of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority of directors and officers

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  association and no guarantor of an obligation of an association may
  assert against a person dealing with the association or against a
  person who has acquired rights from the association that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association's incorporating instrument or any
    by-laws of the association have not been complied with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons named as directors of the association
    in the most recent return sent to the Superintendent under section
    432 are not the directors of the association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place named in the incorporating instrument or
    by-laws of the association is not the place where the head office of
    the association is situated;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person held out by the association as a director,
    officer or representative of the association has not been duly
    appointed or has no authority to exercise the powers and perform the
    duties that are customary in the business of the association or
    usual for a director, officer or representative; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a document issued by any director, officer or
    representative of the association with actual or usual authority to
    issue the document is not valid or not genuine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- knowledge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  who has or ought to have knowledge of a situation described in that
  subsection by virtue of their relationship to the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 21
- 2005, c. 54, s. 143
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sunset provision

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  associations shall not carry on business after March 29, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order, extend by up
  to six months the time during which associations may continue to carry
  on business. No more than one order may be made under this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order not a regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The order is not a regulation for the purposes of the
  [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22). However, it shall be
  published in Part II of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- dissolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If Parliament dissolves on March 29, 2019 or on any
  day within the six-month period before that date or on any day within
  an extension ordered under subsection (2), associations may continue
  to carry on business until the end of the 180th day after the first
  day of the first session of the next Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 22
- 1997, c. 15, s. 116
- 2001, c. 9, s. 254
- 2006, c. 4, s. 200
- 2007, c. 6, s. 138
- 2012, c. 5, s. 105
- 2016, c. 7, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20120329.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Incorporation, Continuance and Discontinuance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-96851 .Part}

### [Formalities of Incorporation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-96852 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation of association

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** On the application of one
or more persons made in accordance with this Act, the Minister may,
subject to this Part, issue letters patent incorporating an association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 23
- 2001, c. 9, s. 256
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Who may apply for incorporation

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** An application for
incorporation of an association may only be made by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an association; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} persons who are entitled to be members of an
  association under Part IV that include at least

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} two central cooperative credit societies not all of
    which are incorporated under the laws of one province,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two local cooperative credit societies not all of
    which are incorporated under the laws of one province,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two or more provincial leagues not all of which
    are incorporated under the laws of one province, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a federal credit union or a federal league.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 24
- 2001, c. 9, s. 256
- 2007, c. 6, s. 139
- 2014, c. 39, s. 280
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for incorporation

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for letters patent to incorporate an association setting
  out the names of the first directors of the association shall be filed
  with the Superintendent, together with such other information,
  material and evidence as the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publishing notice of intent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before filing an application referred to in
  subsection (1), one of the applicants shall, at least once a week for
  a period of four consecutive weeks, publish, in a form satisfactory to
  the Superintendent, a notice of intention to make the application in
  the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in a newspaper in
  general circulation at or near the place where the head office of the
  association is to be situated.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objections to incorporation

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who objects to the proposed incorporation of an association
  may, within thirty days after the date of the last publication under
  subsection 25(2) in respect of the proposed association, submit the
  objection in writing to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister to be informed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an objection under subsection (1), the
  Superintendent shall inform the Minister of the objection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry into objection and report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an objection under subsection (1), and
  if the application for the issuance of the letters patent to which the
  objection relates has been received, the Superintendent shall, if
  satisfied that it is necessary and in the public interest to do so,
  hold or cause to be held a public inquiry into the objection as it
  relates to the application and, on completion of the inquiry, the
  Superintendent shall report the findings of the inquiry to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to be made available

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Within thirty days after receiving a report under
  subsection (3), the Minister shall make the report available to the
  public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules governing proceedings

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the Governor in Council,
  the Superintendent may make rules governing the proceedings at public
  inquiries held under this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** Before issuing letters
patent to incorporate an association, the Minister shall take into
account all matters that the Minister considers relevant to the
application, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature and sufficiency of the financial resources
  of the applicant or applicants as a source of continuing financial
  support for the association;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the soundness and feasibility of the plans of the
  applicant or applicants for the future conduct and development of the
  business of the association;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business record and experience of the applicant
  or applicants;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the character and integrity of the applicant or
  applicants or, if the applicant or any of the applicants is a body
  corporate, its reputation for being operated in a manner that is
  consistent with the standards of good character and integrity;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the association will be operated responsibly
  by persons with the competence and experience suitable for involvement
  in the operation of a financial institution;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the impact of any integration of the businesses and
  operations of the applicant or applicants with those of the
  association on the conduct of those businesses and operations;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} whether the association is to be operated in
  accordance with cooperative principles;

- [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} the predominance of associations, federal credit
  unions or federal leagues, or any combination of them, as members
  within the association; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in Canada
  and, in particular, the cooperative financial system in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 27
- 2001, c. 9, s. 257
- 2014, c. 39, s. 281
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of letters patent

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be set out in the letters patent incorporating an association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province in which the head office of the
    association is to be situated; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date that the association came, or is to come,
    into existence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions in letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may set out in the letters patent
  incorporating an association any provision not contrary to this Act
  that the Minister considers advisable in order to take into account
  the particular circumstances of the proposed association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may impose such terms and conditions in
  respect of the issuance of letters patent incorporating an association
  as the Minister considers necessary or appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 28
- 2005, c. 54, s. 144
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of issue of letters patent

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent shall
cause to be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
a notice of the issuance of letters patent incorporating an association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First directors

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The first directors of an
association are the directors named in the application for letters
patent to incorporate the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** An association comes into
existence on the date provided therefor in its letters patent.

### [Continuance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-96914 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal corporations

- **[[31.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  body corporate incorporated under the [Canada Business Corporations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-44) or any other Act of Parliament may apply to the
  Minister for letters patent continuing the body corporate as an
  association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other corporations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate incorporated otherwise than by or
  under an Act of Parliament may, if so authorized by the laws of the
  jurisdiction where it is incorporated, apply to the Minister for
  letters patent continuing the body corporate as an association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuance for the purpose of
  amalgamation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate incorporated or continued otherwise
  than under this Act may apply to the Minister for letters patent
  continuing the body corporate as an association if the body corporate
  proposes to be continued under this Act for the purpose of
  amalgamating with another body corporate in compliance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for continuance

- **[[31.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate applies for
  letters patent under section 31.1, sections 24 to 27 apply in respect
  of the application, with any modifications that the circumstances
  require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special resolution approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate applies for letters patent
  under section 31.1, the application must be duly authorized by a
  special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of special resolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A copy of the special resolution must be filed with
  the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue letters patent

- **[[31.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the application of a body corporate under subsection 31.1(1) or (2),
  the Minister may, subject to this Part, issue letters patent
  continuing the body corporate as an association if the body corporate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} satisfies the requirements for incorporation as an
    association or will, immediately after the letters patent are
    issued, satisfy those requirements;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is organized and operated and carries on its
    business on a cooperative basis or will, immediately after the
    letters patent are issued, be organized and operated and carry on
    its business on a cooperative basis; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a capital and corporate structure that, if set
    out in the letters patent and by-laws, would meet the requirements
    of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a body corporate under
  subsection 31.1(3), the Minister may, subject to this Part, issue
  letters patent continuing the body corporate as an association only if
  the Minister is of the opinion that the association that results from
  the amalgamation will

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} satisfy the requirements for incorporation as an
    association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be organized and operated and carry on its business
    on a cooperative basis; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have a capital and corporate structure that meet
    the requirements of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of letters patent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If letters patent are issued to a body corporate
  under subsection (1) or (2), section 27 applies in respect of the
  issue of letters patent, with any modifications that the circumstances
  require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

**[[31.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.4 .sectionLabel}** On the day set out in
the letters patent continuing a body corporate as an association under
section 31.3,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the body corporate becomes an association as if it
  had been incorporated under this Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the letters patent are deemed to be the incorporating
  instrument of the continued company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of letters patent

- **[[31.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate is continued
  as an association under section 31.3, the Superintendent shall without
  delay send a copy of the letters patent to the appropriate official or
  public body in the jurisdiction in which the body corporate was
  incorporated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of issuance of letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) a notice of the issuance
  of letters patent continuing a body corporate as an association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effects of continuance

- **[[31.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a body corporate is continued
  as an association,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the property of the body corporate continues to be
    the property of the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the association continues to be liable for the
    obligations of the body corporate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an existing cause of action or claim by or against
    the body corporate or any liability of the body corporate to
    prosecution is unaffected;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a civil, criminal or administrative action or
    proceeding pending by or against the body corporate may continue to
    be prosecuted by or against the association;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a conviction against, or any ruling, order or
    judgment in favour of or against the body corporate may be enforced
    by or against the association;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person who, on the day the body corporate becomes
    an association, was the holder of a security issued by the body
    corporate is not deprived of any right or privilege available to the
    person at that time in respect of the security or relieved of any
    liability in respect of the security, but any such right or
    privilege may be exercised only in accordance with this Act; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the by-laws of the body corporate, except those
    that are in conflict with this Act, continue as the by-laws of the
    association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership shares --- body corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate other than a federal credit
  union is continued as an association,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its common shares are deemed to be membership
    shares to which are attached the rights, privileges and restrictions
    set out in this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holders of the common shares of the body
    corporate are deemed to be the members of the association; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any agreement made before continuance under which
    the holders of any common shares of the body corporate have agreed
    to vote those shares in a manner provided in the agreement is of no
    effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership shares --- federal credit
  union

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When a federal credit union is continued as an
  association,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its membership shares are deemed to be membership
    shares of the association to which are attached the rights,
    privileges and restrictions set out in this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the members of the federal credit union are deemed
    to be the members of the association; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any agreement made before continuance under which
    the members of the federal credit union have agreed to vote in a
    manner provided in the agreement is of no effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
- 2007, c. 6, s. 140
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2115
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31.6-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 31.6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

- **[[31.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this
  Act or the regulations, the Minister may, on the recommendation of the
  Superintendent, by order, grant to an association in respect of which
  letters patent were issued under section 31.3 permission to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engage in a business activity specified in the
    order that an association is not otherwise permitted by this Act to
    engage in and that the body corporate continued as the association
    was engaging in at the time the application for the letters patent
    was made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} continue to have issued and outstanding debt
    obligations the issue of which is not authorized by this Act if the
    debt obligations were outstanding at the time the application for
    the letters patent was made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} hold assets that an association is not otherwise
    permitted by this Act to hold if the assets were held by the body
    corporate continued as the association at the time the application
    for the letters patent was made;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} acquire and hold assets that an association is not
    otherwise permitted by this Act to acquire or hold if the body
    corporate continued as the association was obliged, at the time the
    application for the letters patent was made, to acquire those
    assets; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} maintain outside Canada any records or registers
    required by this Act to be maintained in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The permission shall be expressed to be granted for a
  period specified in the order not exceeding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any activity described in paragraph
    (1)(a), thirty days after the date of issue of the letters patent
    or, if the activity is conducted under an agreement existing on the
    date of issue of the letters patent, the expiration of the
    agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in paragraph
    (1)(b), ten years; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in any of
    paragraphs (1)(c) to (e), two years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the Minister may, on the
  recommendation of the Superintendent, by order, renew a permission
  with respect to any matter described in paragraphs (1)(b) to (d) for
  any further period or periods that the Minister considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not grant to an association a
  permission

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters described in paragraph
    (1)(b), that purports to be effective more than ten years after the
    date of the approval for the association to commence and carry on
    business, unless the Minister is satisfied on the basis of evidence
    on oath provided by an officer of the association that the
    association will not be able at law to redeem at the end of the ten
    years the outstanding debt obligations to which the permission
    relates; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters referred to in paragraphs
    (1)(c) and (d), that purports to be effective more than ten years
    after the date of the approval for the association to commence and
    carry on business.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 258
- 2007, c. 6, s. 141
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31.7-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 31.7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Discontinuance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97004 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transferring to other federal Acts

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), for
    letters patent continuing the association as a bank or a bank
    holding company under that Act, or amalgamating and continuing the
    company as a bank or a bank holding company under that Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply, with the approval in writing of the
    Minister, under the [Canada Business Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-44) for a certificate of continuance as a
    corporation under that Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} apply, with the approval in writing of the
    Minister, under the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7) for a
    certificate of continuance, or a certificate of continuance and a
    certificate of amalgamation, as a cooperative under that Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Insurance Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8), for letters patent continuing the
    association as a company (other than a mutual company) or an
    insurance holding company under that Act, or amalgamating and
    continuing the association as a company (other than a mutual
    company) or an insurance holding company under that Act; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8), for letters patent continuing the
    association as a company under that Act, or amalgamating and
    continuing the association as a company under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The approval referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (c)
  may be given only if the Minister is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association has published, once a week for four
    consecutive weeks in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
    and in a newspaper in general circulation at or near the place where
    the head office of the association is situated, a notice of its
    intention to apply for the approval;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application has been authorized by a special
    resolution; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the association does not hold deposits, other than
    deposits that are made by a member, a person who controls the
    association or a person who has a significant interest in a class of
    shares of the association and that are not insured by the Canada
    Deposit Insurance Corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawing application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a special resolution authorizing the application
  for the certificate or letters patent so states, the directors of the
  association may, without further approval, withdraw the application
  before it is acted on.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on other transfers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An association may not apply to be continued, or to
  be amalgamated and continued, as the case may be, as a body corporate
  other than one referred to in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 32
- 1998, c. 1, s. 382
- 2001, c. 9, s. 259
- 2007, c. 6, s. 142
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Act ceases to apply

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** If an association applies
for a certificate or letters patent referred to in section 32 in
accordance with that section and the certificate is given or the letters
patent are issued, this Act ceases to apply to the association as of the
day the certificate or the letters patent take effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 33
- 1998, c. 1, s. 382
- 2001, c. 9, s. 259
- 2007, c. 6, s. 142
:::
