::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 6,
s. 142\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Corporate Name]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97029 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited names

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may not be incorporated under this Act with a name

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is prohibited by an Act of Parliament;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is, in the opinion of the Superintendent,
    deceptively misdescriptive;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is the same as or, in the opinion of the
    Superintendent, substantially the same as or confusingly similar to,
    any existing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} trademark or trade name, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} corporate name of a body corporate,

    except where the trademark or trade name is being changed or the
    body corporate is being dissolved or is changing its corporate name
    and consent to the use of the trademark, trade name or corporate
    name is signified to the Superintendent in such manner as the
    Superintendent may require;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is the same as or, in the opinion of the
    Superintendent, substantially the same as or confusingly similar to,
    the known name under or by which any entity carries on business or
    is identified; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is reserved under section 39 for another
    association or a proposed association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of "cooperative"

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the [Canada Cooperatives
  Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7), an association may use the word "cooperative"
  or "coopérative", or any abbreviation of that word, in its name.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 35
- 1996, c. 6, s. 49
- 1997, c. 15, s. 117
- 1998, c. 1, s. 383
- [2014, c. 20, s. 366(E)](#1034903-1145578){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1034903-1145578.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** The name of an
association, other than the former-Act association, shall include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the phrase "federal cooperative" or "[co­opérative
  fédérale]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", along with another word or
  expression indicating the financial nature of the association;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the phrase "federal central credit union", "federal
  credit union central" or "[fédération de caisses populaires
  fédérale]{.otherLang lang="fr"}";

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any combination or derivative of words and phrases
  referred to in paragraphs (a) and (b); or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} any word or phrase specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 36
- 2001, c. 9, s. 260
- 2007, c. 6, s. 143
- 2014, c. 39, s. 282
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 36"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated entity

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 35, an
association that is affiliated with another entity may, with the consent
of that entity, be incorporated with, or change its name to,
substantially the same name as that of the affiliated entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 37
- 1996, c. 6, s. 50
- 2001, c. 9, s. 261
- 2007, c. 6, s. 143
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}English or French form of name

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  name of an association may be set out in its letters patent in an
  English form, a French form, an English form and a French form or in a
  combined English and French form, and the association may use and be
  legally designated by any such form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association may identify itself outside Canada by
  its name in any language and the association may use and be legally
  designated by any such form of its name outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4) and section 250, an
  association may carry on business under or identify itself by a name
  other than its corporate name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where an association is carrying on business under or
  identifying itself by a name other than its corporate name, the
  Superintendent may, by order, direct the association not to use that
  other name if the Superintendent is of the opinion that that other
  name is a name referred to in any of paragraphs 35(1)(a) to (e).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 38
- 1996, c. 6, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reserved name

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
on request, reserve for ninety days a name for a proposed association or
for an association that intends to change its name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directing change of name

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  through inadvertence or otherwise an association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} comes into existence or is continued with a name,
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on an application to change its name, is granted a
    name

  that is prohibited by section 35, the Superintendent may, by order,
  direct the association to change its name and the association shall
  comply with that direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revoking name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an association has been directed under
  subsection (1) to change its name and has not, within sixty days after
  the service of the direction, changed its name to a name that is not
  prohibited by this Act, the Superintendent may revoke the name of the
  association and assign to it a name and, until changed in accordance
  with section 219 or 221, the name of the association is thereafter the
  name so assigned.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 40
- 1996, c. 6, s. 52
- 2001, c. 9, s. 262
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Membership]{.HTitleText1} {#h-97080 .Part}

### [Admittance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97081 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Only a
  person that is an association, a federal credit union, a central
  cooperative credit society, a local cooperative credit society, a
  cooperative corporation, a federal or provincial league or an
  unincorporated organization consisting wholly of any of those entities
  may be admitted to membership in an association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition for membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be admitted to membership in an
  association until the person's application for membership has been
  approved by the directors and the person has complied fully with any
  by-law governing admission of members.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 9, s. 264\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 50\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership not transferable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No transfer of a membership is valid for any purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 41
- 1994, c. 47, s. 50
- 2001, c. 9, ss. 263, 264
- 2014, c. 39, s. 283
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and the
  by-laws, each member of an association shall have one vote on all
  matters to be decided by the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The members of an association may, by by-law, provide
  that the voting rights of a member shall be vested in one or more
  delegates to be elected or appointed by the member in such manner as
  may be provided for in the by-laws, and the delegates so elected or
  appointed may exercise all or any of those rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, in this Act, reference is made to a meeting of
  members of an association and the association has passed a by-law
  under subsection (2), a reference in this Act to a meeting of members
  shall be construed as a reference to a meeting of delegates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes of members

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** The members of an
association may, by by-law, establish one or more classes of members,
and such a by-law may provide for such matters as are necessary,
including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the qualifications for and the terms and conditions
  applying to membership in each such class;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of delegates to represent each class and
  the manner of electing or appointing such delegates; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the election, appointment or removal of its directors
  by the members or delegates of particular classes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of membership shares

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** No transfer of membership
shares in an association is valid unless the transfer is approved by
resolution of the directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws binding

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Act,
every by-law of an association binds the association and its members to
the same extent as if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} each member had duly approved the by-law; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} there were in the by-law a covenant under seal on the
  part of each member, and the successors and assigns of the member, to
  conform thereto.

### [Withdrawal and Expulsion]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97111 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of withdrawal

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** A member may withdraw
from membership in an association by giving six months notice of its
intention to the directors or such shorter notice as the directors may
fix.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expulsion

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member may be expelled from membership in an association by special
  resolution passed by the directors at a meeting called for that
  purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for resolution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution passed under subsection (1) is
  not valid unless prior written notice is given to the member setting
  forth the grounds on which the association is seeking to expel it and
  an opportunity is given to the member to appear, by an agent or
  counsel, to make submissions at the meeting of the directors called to
  consider the resolution to expel it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution passed under subsection (1)
  takes effect on the date on which the member is notified of the
  resolution or, if later, the date specified in the resolution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where withdrawal or expulsion

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** Where a member withdraws
or is expelled pursuant to section 46 or 47, the association shall
permit the withdrawal of the member's deposits with the association and
shall, in accordance with any by-law passed pursuant to subsection
67(4), redeem the membership shares held by the member, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are reasonable grounds for believing that the
  association is, or the payment to redeem the membership shares would
  cause the association to be, in contravention of any regulation
  referred to in 409(1) and (2) or any direction referred to in 409(3);
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph 79(1)(b), the redemption of the
  membership shares has not been approved by the Superintendent.

### [Members Register]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97125 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members register

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall maintain a members register in which it shall record

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names, alphabetically arranged, and latest
    known addresses of the members and former members of the
    association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of membership shares held by each
    member; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date and particulars of the issue and transfer
    of each membership share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former-Act and amalgamated associations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), "members
  register" includes similar registers required by law to be maintained
  by the former-Act association, or by a body corporate amalgamated and
  continued as an association under this Act, before the coming into
  force of this section or the amalgamation, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 236(4) and sections 240 to 242 and 246 to
  249 apply, with such modifications as the circumstances require, in
  respect of a members register.

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97136 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum membership

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  membership in a retail association and the former-Act association must
  include at least

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} two central cooperative credit societies not all of
    which are incorporated under the laws of one province;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two local cooperative credit societies not all of
    which are incorporated under the laws of one province;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} two provincial leagues not all of which are
    incorporated under the laws of one province; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a federal credit union or a federal league.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Predominately federal membership

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The membership of an association that is not a
  retail association or the former-Act association must be predominated
  by associations other than retail associations, by federal credit
  unions, by federal leagues or by any combination of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If minimum not attained

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, the membership in an association is
  not in accordance with subsection (1) or (1.1), as the case may be,
  the association shall without delay take the steps that are necessary
  to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply for a certificate of continuation or letters
    patent referred to in subsection 32(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} liquidate and dissolve the association under Part
    VII.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 50
- 1998, c. 1, s. 384
- 2001, c. 9, s. 265
- 2007, c. 6, s. 144
- 2014, c. 39, s. 284
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lien

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** Unless the by-laws
otherwise provide, where a member is indebted to an association, the
association has a lien to the extent of the debt on the membership
shares and deposits recorded in the name of, and any dividends payable
to, the member.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No control

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** No person other than an
association may control an association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 52
- 2001, c. 9, s. 266
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Organization and Commencement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-97159 .Part}

### [Organization Meetings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97160 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First directors' meeting

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  letters patent incorporating an association are issued, a meeting of
  the directors of the association shall be held at which the directors
  may, subject to this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adopt forms of certificates for membership shares
    and shares and of corporate records;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorize the issue of membership shares of the
    association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} admit persons to membership in the association;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} appoint officers;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} make banking arrangements; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} deal with any other matters necessary to organize
    the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling directors' meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An incorporator or a director named in the
  application for letters patent may call the meeting referred to in
  subsection (1) by giving, subject to subsection 186(2), no fewer than
  five days notice of the purpose, time and place of the meeting to each
  director of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling members' meeting

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  the meeting referred to in subsection 53(1), the directors of the
  association shall forthwith call a meeting of the members of the
  association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting of members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The members of an association shall, at the meeting
  called pursuant to subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make by-laws;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} elect or appoint directors to hold office for a
    term expiring not later than the close of the third annual meeting
    of the association following the election; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} appoint an auditor to hold office until the close
    of the first annual meeting of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of first directors

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** A director named in the
application for letters patent to incorporate an association holds
office until the close of the meeting called pursuant to subsection
54(1).

### [Commencement and Carrying on of Business]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97182 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to commence and carry on business

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall not carry on any business until the Superintendent
  has, by order, approved the commencement and carrying on of business
  by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former-Act association

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A certificate or any other authorization that was
  given to the former-Act association and that is in effect immediately
  before the coming into force of this Part is deemed to be an order of
  the Superintendent under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continued association

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except in respect of a body corporate that is
  continued as an association under this Act for the purpose of
  immediately amalgamating with one or more bodies corporate and
  continuing as an association under this Act, if letters patent
  continuing a body corporate as an association under this Act are
  issued, the Superintendent shall make an order approving the
  commencement and carrying on of business by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamated association

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If letters patent amalgamating and continuing two or
  more bodies corporate as an association under this Act are issued, the
  Superintendent shall make an order approving the commencement and
  carrying on of business by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}S. 57(2) and s. 60 do not apply

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, subsection 57(2) and section
  60 do not apply in respect of an association referred to in
  subsection (3) or (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 56
- 2005, c. 54, s. 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to make order

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application by an association, the Superintendent may make an order
  approving the commencement and carrying on of business by the
  association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of payments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application by an association for an order under
  subsection (1) must contain a statement setting out the amounts paid
  or to be paid by the association in connection with its incorporation
  and organization.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No payments before order

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** Until an order approving
the commencement and carrying on of business is made for an association,
the association shall not make any payment on account of incorporation
or organization expenses out of the paid-in capital of the association
and interest thereon, except reasonable sums

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the remuneration of not more than two officers;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the payment of costs related to the issue of
  membership shares and shares of the association; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the payment of clerical assistance, legal
  services, accounting services, office accommodation at one location,
  office expenses, advertising, stationery, postage and travel expenses.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits and investments before order

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** Where an association
comes into existence but no order approving the commencement and
carrying on of business is made for the association, the association may
only

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deposit, in Canada, paid-in capital of the
  association in another deposit-taking Canadian financial institution;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} invest paid-in capital of the association in
  unencumbered securities of the Government of Canada or the government
  of any province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for order

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent shall not make an order approving the commencement and
  carrying on of business by an association until it has been shown to
  the satisfaction of the Superintendent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the meeting of members of the association referred
    to in subsection 54(1) has been duly held;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the association has paid-in capital of at least
    five million dollars or such greater amount as the Minister may
    specify;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the expenses of incorporation and organization to
    be borne by the association are reasonable; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all other relevant requirements of this Act have
    been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall not make an order approving
  the commencement and carrying on of business by an association more
  than one year after the day on which the association comes into
  existence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 60
- 2001, c. 9, s. 267
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of order

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** An order approving the
commencement and carrying on of business by an association may contain
any conditions or limitations that the Superintendent considers
appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 61
- 2001, c. 9, s. 268
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variations

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of the order approving the commencement and carrying on of
  business by an association, the Superintendent may at any time, by
  further order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make the order subject to such conditions or
    limitations that are consistent with this Act and that relate to the
    business of the association as the Superintendent deems expedient
    and necessary, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amend or revoke any authorization contained in the
    order or any condition or limitation to which the order is subject,

  but before making any such further order the Superintendent shall
  provide the association with an opportunity to make representations
  regarding that further order.

- [(2) to (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1996, c. 6, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 62
- 1996, c. 6, s. 53
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public notice

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the
  making of an order approving the commencement and carrying on of
  business by an association, the association shall publish a notice of
  the making of the order in a newspaper in general circulation at or
  near the place where the head office of the association is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice in [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) a notice of the making of
  an order approving the commencement and carrying on of business by an
  association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of existence

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** Except for the sole
purpose of winding up the association's affairs, an association ceases
to exist one year after the day on which its incorporating instrument
became effective if it does not obtain an order approving the
commencement and carrying on of business within that year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allowed disbursements

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  an order approving the commencement and carrying on of business is not
  made for an association, no part of the moneys of the association
  shall be used for the payment of incorporation and organization
  expenses, other than remuneration and costs referred to in section 58,
  unless the payment has been approved by a special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court to settle
  disbursements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the amount allowed by a special resolution for the
  payment of any incorporation and organization expenses referred to in
  subsection (1) is considered insufficient by the directors or if no
  special resolution for the payment of such expenses is passed, the
  directors may apply to any court having jurisdiction in the place
  where the head office of the association is situated to settle and
  determine the amounts to be paid out of any moneys of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors shall, at least twenty-one days prior
  to the date fixed for the hearing of the application referred to in
  subsection (2), send to the members or incorporators, as the case may
  be, a notice of the application, which notice shall contain a
  statement of the amounts that are proposed to be settled and
  determined by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ratio payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In order that the amounts paid and payable under this
  section may be equitably borne by the members or incorporators, as the
  case may be, the directors shall, after the amounts of the payments
  have been approved by special resolution or settled and determined by
  a court, fix the proportionate part thereof chargeable to each member
  or incorporator as the ratio of the amount paid in by the member or
  incorporator to the aggregate of all the amounts paid in by the
  members or incorporators.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of excess

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After the amounts referred to in this section have
  been paid, the directors shall pay, with any interest earned thereon,
  to the members or incorporators, the respective balances of the moneys
  paid in by them, less the amount chargeable to each member or
  incorporator under subsection (4).

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Capital Structure]{.HTitleText1} {#h-97252 .Part}

### [Share Capital]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97253 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue membership shares and
shares

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act and the
  incorporating instrument and by-laws of the association,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} membership shares of an association may be issued
    at such times and to such members and for such consideration as the
    directors of the association may determine; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shares of an association may be issued at such
    times and to such persons and for such consideration as the
    directors of the association may determine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Shares of an association shall be in registered form
  and membership shares and shares shall be without nominal or par
  value.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding membership shares

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Members shall hold the minimum
  number of membership shares prescribed by the by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The membership shares of an association confer on the
  holders thereof equal rights, including equal rights to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receive dividends declared on the membership
    shares; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} receive the remaining property of the association
    on dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No association shall designate a class of its shares
  as "membership shares" or any variation thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redemption

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of an association shall set the terms and
  conditions under which membership shares may be redeemed, including
  the manner and time of payment for the redeemed membership shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates not mandatory

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of an association may provide that no
  membership share certificates need be issued but if this is the case,
  the association shall issue to each member who so requests, a
  statement of the number of membership shares held by the member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an association issues membership share
  certificates, there shall be stated on the face of each certificate
  for a membership share issued by the association after the coming into
  force of this section

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the association is subject to the
    [Cooperative Credit Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom it is issued;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the certificate represents
    membership shares in the association, and the number of such
    membership shares;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the certificate is not
    transferable without the approval of the Board of Directors of the
    association; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that there may be a lien on the membership shares
    represented by the certificate in favour of the association for
    indebtedness to the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 67
- 2007, c. 6, s. 145(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No automatic rights

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  association shall not issue any share that confers on the holder
  thereof the right

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to vote at meetings of the association otherwise
    than in accordance with this Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to receive any of the remaining property of the
    association on dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association may issue a share that confers on the
  holder thereof the right to vote at an election of directors by reason
  of an event that has occurred and is continuing or by a reason of a
  condition that has been fulfilled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of contravening provision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any provision in a by-law that purports to confer a
  right referred to in subsection (1) is of no force and effect.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares of former-Act association

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Membership shares and shares with
  nominal or par value of the former-Act association are deemed to be
  shares without nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed share conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where any right of a holder of a membership share or
  share with nominal or par value of the former-Act association was
  stated or expressed in terms of the nominal or par value of the share
  immediately before the coming into force of this Part, that right is
  thereafter deemed to be the same right stated or expressed without
  reference to the nominal or par value of the share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes of shares

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  members of an association may, by by-law, provide for one or more
  classes of shares and, if they so provide, shall set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attaching to the shares of each class; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum number, if any, of shares of any class
    that the association is authorized to issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A by-law referred to in subsection (1) must be
  approved by special resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 70
- 2001, c. 9, s. 269
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares issued in series

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of an association may, subject to any limitations set out in
  them, authorize the issue of any class of shares in one or more series
  and may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fix the maximum number of shares in each series and
    determine the designation, rights, privileges, restrictions and
    conditions attaching to them; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorize the directors to do anything referred to
    in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Series participation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any cumulative dividend or amounts payable on
  return of capital in respect of a series of shares are not paid in
  full, the shares of all series of the same class participate rateably
  in respect of accumulated dividends and return of capital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting rights

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where voting rights are attached to any series of a
  class of shares, the shares of every other series of that class shall
  have the same voting rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on series

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No rights, privileges, restrictions or conditions
  attached to a series of shares authorized under this section confer on
  the series a priority in respect of dividends or return of capital
  over any other series of shares of the same class that are then
  outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Material to Superintendent

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the directors exercise their authority under
  paragraph (1)(b), the directors shall, before the issue of shares of
  the series, send to the Superintendent particulars of the series of
  shares and a copy of the by-law that granted the authority to the
  directors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 71
- 2005, c. 54, s. 146
- 2007, c. 6, s. 146(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One share, one vote

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** Where voting rights are
attached to a share of an association issued pursuant to a by-law passed
under section 70, the by-law may confer only one vote in respect of that
share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership shares and shares
non-assessable

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** Membership shares and
shares issued by an association after the coming into force of this
section are non-assessable and the members and shareholders are not
liable to the association or to its creditors in respect thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  membership share and no share of any class of shares of an association
  shall be issued until it is fully paid for in money or, with the
  approval of the Superintendent, in property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other currencies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When issuing membership shares or shares, an
  association may provide that any aspect of the membership shares or
  shares relating to money or involving the payment of or the liability
  to pay money be in a currency other than the currency of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When approval not necessary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of an association may, with the approval
  of the Superintendent, provide for a formula or procedure for valuing
  a member of the association or any of its assets or liabilities when
  the member, or the asset or liability, is proposed to be acquired by
  the association in exchange for membership shares or shares of the
  association. The approval of the Superintendent under subsection (1)
  is not necessary when such shares are issued in accordance with such a
  by-law.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 74
- 2001, c. 9, s. 270
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital account

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall maintain a separate stated capital account for the
  membership shares and for each class and series of shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition to stated capital account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall record in the appropriate stated
  capital account the full amount of any consideration it receives for
  any membership shares or shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), an association may, subject
  to subsection (2.2), record in the stated capital account maintained
  for the shares of a class or series any part of the consideration it
  receives in an exchange if it issues shares

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in exchange for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who immediately before the
      exchange did not deal with the association at arm's length within
      the meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) or property of any prescribed person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shares of or another interest in a body
      corporate that immediately before the exchange or because of it
      did not deal with the association at arm's length within the
      meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) or shares of or another interest in any
      prescribed entity, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who immediately before the
      exchange dealt with the association at arm's length within the
      meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if the person, the association and all of
      the holders of shares in the class or series of shares so issued
      consent to the exchange;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} under an agreement referred to in subsection
    227(1); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to shareholders of an amalgamating body corporate
    who receive the shares in addition to or instead of securities of
    the amalgamated association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on addition to a stated capital
  account

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} On the issuance of a share, an association shall
  not add to the stated capital account in respect of the share an
  amount greater than the amount of the consideration it receives for
  the share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constraint on addition to a stated
  capital account

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} Where an association that has issued any
  outstanding shares of more than one class or series proposes to add to
  a stated capital account that it maintains in respect of a class or
  series of shares an amount that was not received by the association as
  consideration for the issue of shares, the addition must be approved
  by special resolution unless all the issued and outstanding shares are
  of not more than two classes of convertible shares referred to in
  subsection 84(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital of former-Act
  association

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the coming into force of this Part, the former-Act
  association shall record in the stated capital account maintained for
  membership shares then outstanding an amount that is equal to the
  aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate amount paid up on the membership
    shares immediately before the coming into force of this Part, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the contributed surplus of the
    association that is attributable to those membership shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contributed surplus entry

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of any contributed surplus recorded in the
  stated capital account pursuant to paragraph (3)(b) shall be deducted
  from the contributed surplus account of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Share issued before coming into force

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any amount unpaid in respect of a membership share
  issued by the former-Act association before the coming into force of
  this Part and paid after the coming into force of this Part shall be
  recorded in the stated capital account maintained by the association
  for the membership shares.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 75
- 1997, c. 15, s. 118
- 2001, c. 9, s. 271
- 2005, c. 54, s. 147
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-emptive right: shareholders

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the by-laws of an association so provide, no shares of any class shall
  be issued unless the shares have first been offered to the persons
  holding shares of that class, and those persons have a pre-emptive
  right to acquire the offered shares in proportion to their holdings of
  the shares of that class, at such price and on such terms as those
  shares are to be offered to others.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the existence of a pre-emptive right,
  a shareholder of an association has no pre-emptive right in respect of
  shares that are to be issued

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a consideration other than money;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a share dividend; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to the exercise of conversion privileges,
    options or rights previously granted by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the existence of a pre-emptive right,
  a shareholder of an association has no pre-emptive right in respect of
  shares to be issued

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the issue of shares to the shareholder is
    prohibited by this Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where, to the knowledge of the directors of the
    association, the offer of shares to a shareholder whose recorded
    address is in a country other than Canada ought not to be made
    unless the appropriate authority in that country is provided with
    information in addition to that submitted to the members at the last
    annual meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conversion privileges

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may issue conversion privileges, options or rights to
  acquire securities of the association, and shall set out the
  conditions thereof

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the documents that evidence the conversion
    privileges, options or rights; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the securities to which the conversion
    privileges, options or rights are attached.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transferable rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Conversion privileges, options and rights to acquire
  securities of an association may be made transferable or
  non-transferable, and options and rights to acquire such securities
  may be made separable or inseparable from any securities to which they
  are attached.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reserved shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an association has granted privileges to
  convert any securities issued by the association into shares, or into
  shares of another class or series, or has issued or granted options or
  rights to acquire shares, if the by-laws limit the number of
  authorized shares, the association shall reserve and continue to
  reserve sufficient authorized shares to meet the exercise of such
  conversion privileges, options and rights.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding of own shares

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** Except as provided in
sections 79 to 81, or unless permitted by the regulations, an
association shall not

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} hold membership shares or shares of the association;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} permit any of its subsidiaries to hold membership
  shares greater than the minimum number of membership shares of the
  association prescribed by a by-law made under subsection 67(1) or any
  shares of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchase and redemption of membership
shares and shares

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and to its
  by-laws, an association may,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with the consent of the Superintendent, purchase,
    for the purpose of cancellation, any membership shares or shares
    issued by it, or redeem any redeemable shares issued by it at a
    price not exceeding the redemption price thereof calculated
    according to a formula stated in its by-laws or, in the case of
    shares, according to the conditions attaching to the shares; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} purchase in any calendar year, for the purpose of
    cancellation, not more than one per cent of the membership shares
    outstanding at the beginning of the year, at a price not exceeding
    the redemption price of the membership shares calculated according
    to a formula stated in its by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions on purchase and redemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall not make any payment to purchase
  or redeem any membership shares or shares issued by it if there are
  reasonable grounds for believing that the association is, or the
  payment would cause the association to be, in contravention of any
  regulation referred to in subsection 409(1) or (2) or any direction
  made pursuant to subsection 409(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Donations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association may accept any membership share or
  share of the association surrendered to it as a gift, but may not
  extinguish or reduce a liability in respect of an amount unpaid on any
  such share except in accordance with section 82.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding as personal representative

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may, and may permit its subsidiaries to, hold, in the
  capacity of a personal representative, membership shares and shares of
  the association, but only where the association or the subsidiary does
  not have a beneficial interest in the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 51, an association may, and may
  permit its subsidiaries to, hold membership shares and shares of the
  association by way of a security interest where the security interest
  is nominal or immaterial when measured by criteria established by the
  association that have been approved in writing by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (2) precludes the former-Act
  association or any of its subsidiaries from holding any security
  interest held immediately prior to the coming into force of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 80
- 2005, c. 54, s. 148(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- conditions before
acquisition

- **[[80.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may permit any of its subsidiaries to acquire shares of
  the association through the issuance of those shares by the
  association to the subsidiary if the conditions prescribed for the
  purposes of this subsection are met before the subsidiary acquires the
  shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions after acquisition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After a subsidiary has acquired shares under the
  purported authority of subsection (1), the conditions prescribed for
  the purposes of this subsection must be met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliance with conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an association permits any of its subsidiaries to
  acquire shares of the association under the purported authority of
  subsection (1) and one or more of the conditions prescribed for the
  purposes of subsections (1) and (2) were not met, are not met or cease
  to be met, as the case may be, then, despite section 17 and subsection
  75(2), the association must comply with the prescribed requirements.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 147
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of membership shares and
shares

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where an
  association purchases membership shares, or shares of the association
  or fractions thereof, or redeems or otherwise acquires membership
  shares or shares of the association, the association shall cancel
  those membership shares or shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of shares

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an association or any of its subsidiaries,
  through the realization of security, acquires any membership shares or
  shares of the association, the association may, or may cause its
  subsidiary to, as the case may be, within six months after the day of
  the realization, sell or otherwise dispose of the membership shares or
  shares.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction of capital

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  stated capital of an association may be reduced by special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall not reduce its stated capital by
  special resolution if there are reasonable grounds for believing that
  the association is, or the reduction would cause the association to
  be, in contravention of any regulation referred to in subsection
  409(1) or (2) or any direction made pursuant to subsection 409(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of special resolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution to reduce the stated capital of
  an association shall specify the stated capital account or accounts
  from which the reduction of stated capital effected by the special
  resolution will be deducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Superintendent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution to reduce the stated capital of
  an association has no effect until it is approved in writing by the
  Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (4) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reduction in the stated capital is made solely
    as a result of changes made to the accounting principles referred to
    in subsection 292(4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is to be no return of capital to members or
    shareholders as a result of the reduction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No approval to reduce the stated capital of an
  association may be given by the Superintendent unless application
  therefor is made within three months after the time of the passing of
  the special resolution and a copy of the special resolution, together
  with a notice of intention to apply for approval, has been published
  in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statements to be submitted

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In addition to evidence of the passing of a special
  resolution to reduce the stated capital of an association and of the
  publication thereof, statements showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of the association's membership shares
    and shares issued and outstanding,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of the voting by members of the
    association or by class of shares,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the association's assets and liabilities, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reason why the association seeks the reduction
    of capital

  shall be submitted to the Superintendent at the time of the
  application for approval of the special resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 82
- 2007, c. 6, s. 148
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-82-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery by action

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any money or property was paid or distributed to a member, shareholder
  or other person as a consequence of a reduction of capital made
  contrary to section 82, a creditor of the association may apply to a
  court for an order compelling the member, shareholder or other person
  to pay the money or deliver the property to the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares held by personal representative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person holding membership shares or shares in the
  capacity of a personal representative and registered on the records of
  the association as a member or shareholder and therein described as
  the personal representative of a named person is personally liable
  under subsection (1), but the named person is subject to all the
  liabilities imposed by that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An action to enforce a liability imposed by
  subsection (1) may not be commenced more than two years after the date
  of the act complained of.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remedy preserved

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not affect any liability that
  arises under section 211.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment of stated capital account

- **[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On a
  purchase, redemption or other acquisition by an association of
  membership shares, or shares, or fractions thereof issued by it, other
  than membership shares or shares acquired pursuant to section 80 or
  acquired through the realization of security and sold pursuant to
  subsection 81(2), the association shall deduct from the stated capital
  account maintained for the membership shares, or for the class or
  series of shares, so purchased, redeemed or otherwise acquired an
  amount equal to the result obtained by multiplying the stated capital
  in respect of the membership shares or the shares of that class or
  series by the number of membership shares or shares of that class or
  series so purchased, redeemed or otherwise acquired and dividing by
  the number of membership shares or shares of that class or series
  outstanding immediately before the purchase, redemption or other
  acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall adjust its stated capital
  account or accounts in accordance with any special resolution referred
  to in section 82.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares converted to another class

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On a conversion of outstanding shares of an
  association into shares of another class or series, or on a change of
  outstanding shares of the association into shares of another class or
  series, the association shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deduct from the stated capital account maintained
    for the class or series of shares converted or changed an amount
    equal to the result obtained by multiplying the stated capital of
    the shares of that class or series by the number of shares of that
    class or series converted or changed, and dividing by the number of
    outstanding shares of that class or series immediately before the
    conversion or change; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} record the result obtained under paragraph (a) and
    any additional consideration received pursuant to the conversion or
    change in the stated capital account maintained or to be maintained
    for the class or series of shares into which the shares have been
    converted or changed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital of convertible shares

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3) and subject to the
  association's by-laws, where an association issues two classes of
  shares and there is attached to each class a right to convert a share
  of one class into a share of the other class and a share is so
  converted, the amount of stated capital attributable to a share in
  either class is the aggregate of the stated capital of both classes
  divided by the number of outstanding shares of both classes
  immediately before the conversion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conversion or change of shares

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Shares issued by an association and converted into
  shares of another class or series, or changed under subsection 221(1)
  into shares of another class or series, become issued shares of the
  class or series of shares into which the shares have been converted or
  changed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition to stated capital account

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** On a conversion of any
debt obligation of an association into membership shares, or into shares
of a class or series of shares, the association shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deduct from the liabilities of the association the
  nominal value of the debt obligation being converted; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} record the result obtained under paragraph (a) and
  any additional consideration received for the conversion in the stated
  capital account maintained or to be maintained for the membership
  shares or for the class or series of shares into which the debt
  obligation has been converted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of dividend

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of an association may declare and an association may pay a
  dividend by issuing fully paid membership shares or options or rights
  to acquire membership shares to members or fully paid shares or
  options or rights to acquire fully paid shares to members or
  shareholders and, subject to subsection (4), the directors of an
  association may declare and an association may pay a dividend in money
  or property, and if a dividend is to be paid in money, the dividend
  may be paid in a currency other than the currency of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors of an association shall notify the
  Superintendent of the declaration of a dividend at least 15 days
  before the day fixed for its payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dividend

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If membership shares or shares of an association are
  issued in payment of a dividend, the association shall record in the
  stated capital account maintained or to be maintained for the
  membership shares or shares of the class or series issued in payment
  of the dividend the declared amount of the dividend stated as an
  amount of money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When dividend not to be declared

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The directors of an association shall not declare and
  an association shall not pay a dividend if there are reasonable
  grounds for believing that the association is, or the payment would
  cause the association to be, in contravention of any regulation
  referred to in subsection 409(1) or (2) or any direction made pursuant
  to subsection 409(3).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 6, s. 149\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 86
- 2001, c. 9, s. 272
- 2007, c. 6, s. 149
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-86-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 86"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Subordinated Indebtedness]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97481 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on subordinated indebtedness

- **[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall not issue subordinated indebtedness unless the
  subordinated indebtedness is fully paid for in money or, with the
  approval of the Superintendent, in property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to subordinated indebtedness

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not in any prospectus, advertisement,
  correspondence or literature relating to any subordinated indebtedness
  issued or to be issued by an association refer to the subordinated
  indebtedness otherwise than as subordinated indebtedness.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed not to be a deposit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subordinated indebtedness issued by an association is
  deemed not to be a deposit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other currencies

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} When issuing subordinated indebtedness, an
  association may provide that any aspect of the subordinated
  indebtedness relating to money or involving the payment of or the
  liability to pay money in relation thereto be in a currency other than
  that of Canada including, without restricting the generality of the
  foregoing, the payment of any interest thereon.

### [Security Certificates and Transfers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-97491 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** In this section and
sections 89 to 142,

[[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a claim that a
    transfer was or would be wrongful or that a particular adverse
    person is the owner of or has an interest in a
    security; ([opposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[[bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[[bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"}
    purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a purchaser for value in good
    faith and without notice of any adverse claim who takes delivery of
    a security in bearer form or order form or of a security in
    registered form issued to the purchaser or endorsed to the purchaser
    or endorsed in blank; ([acheteur de bonne foi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[clearing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[clearing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person designated as
    a recognized clearing agency by the Superintendent; ([agence de
    compensation et de dépôt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means voluntary transfer of
    possession; ([livraison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [remise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, means
    securities of which any unit is, by nature or usage of trade, the
    equivalent of any other like unit; ([fongibles]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means free of forgery or
    counterfeit; ([authentique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means honesty in fact in the
    conduct of the transaction concerned; ([bonne foi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[over-issue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[over-issue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the issue of securities in
    excess of any maximum number of securities that the issuer is
    authorized to issue; ([émission excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who takes an
    interest in a security by sale, mortgage, pledge, issue, reissue,
    gift or any other voluntary
    transaction; ([acquéreur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[securities broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[securities broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is
    engaged for all or part of the person's time in the business of
    buying and selling securities and who, in the transaction concerned,
    acts for, or buys a security from, or sells a security to, a
    customer; ([courtier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[security
    certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an instrument issued by an
    association that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in bearer, order or registered form,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of a type commonly dealt in on securities
      exchanges or markets or commonly recognized in any area in which
      it is issued or dealt in as a medium for investment,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one of a class or series or by its terms
      divisible into a class or series of instruments, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence of a share, participation or other
      interest in or obligation of an association,

    but does not include an instrument evidencing a deposit or a
    membership share; ([valeur mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"},
    [titre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [certificat de valeur
    mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning given that
    expression by section 278; ([acte de fiducie]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[unauthorized]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[unauthorized]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a signature or
    an endorsement, means a signature or an endorsement made without
    actual, implied or apparent authority, and includes a forgery; ([non
    autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[uncertificated security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[uncertificated security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a security,
    not evidenced by a security certificate, the issue and any transfer
    of which is registered or recorded in records maintained for that
    purpose by or on behalf of an association; ([valeur mobilière sans
    certificat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means issued in accordance with the
    applicable law or validated under section
    104. ([valide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions governing transfers of
securities

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** The transfer of a
security is governed by sections 90 to 142.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security a negotiable instrument

- **[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security is a negotiable instrument but, in the case of any
  inconsistency between the provisions of the [Bills of Exchange
  Act](/eng/acts/B-4) and this Act, this Act prevails to the extent of
  the inconsistency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bearer form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security is in bearer form if it is payable to
  bearer according to its terms and not by reason of any endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order form

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A security is in order form where the security is not
  a share and, by its terms, it is payable to the order or assigns of
  any person therein specified with reasonable certainty or to the
  person or the person's order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A security is in registered form if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it specifies a person entitled to the security or
    to the rights it evidences, and its transfer is capable of being
    recorded in a securities register; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it bears a statement that it is in registered form.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of guarantor

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** A guarantor for an issuer
of a security is deemed to be an issuer to the extent of the guarantee,
whether or not the guarantor's obligation is noted on the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of holder

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to Part VIII, every security
  holder is entitled at the holder's option to a security certificate
  that complies with this Act or to a non-transferable written
  acknowledgement of the holder's right to obtain a security certificate
  that complies with this Act from an association in respect of the
  securities of that association held by the security holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee for security certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association may charge a fee, not exceeding a
  prescribed amount, for a security certificate issued in respect of a
  transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint holders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association is not required to issue more than one
  security certificate in respect of securities held jointly by several
  persons, and delivery of a security certificate to one of several
  joint holders is sufficient delivery to all joint holders of the
  security.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 92
- 1999, c. 31, s. 54
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security certificate shall be signed by or bear the printed or
  otherwise mechanically reproduced signature of at least one of the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registrar or transfer agent of the association or
    a branch transfer agent or a natural person on their behalf; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trustee who certifies it in accordance with a
    trust indenture.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of validity of signature

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a security certificate contains a person's printed
  or mechanically reproduced signature, the association may issue the
  security certificate even if the person has ceased to be a director or
  officer of the association. The security certificate is as valid as if
  the person were a director or officer at the date of its issue.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 93
- 2005, c. 54, s. 150
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-93-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 93"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of share certificate

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** There shall be stated on
the face of each share certificate issued by an association after the
coming into force of this section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the association;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the association is subject to the
  Canadian Cooperative Credit Associations Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom the share certificate
  is issued; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number and class of shares and the designation of
  any series that the certificate represents.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions and charges

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  charge in favour of an association and no restriction on transfer
  other than a constraint under Part VIII is effective against a
  transferee of a security issued by the association if the transferee
  has no actual knowledge of the charge or restriction unless it or a
  reference to it is noted conspicuously on the security certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any of the issued shares of a distributing
  association remain outstanding and are held by more than one person,
  the association may not restrict the transfer or ownership of its
  shares except by way of a constraint under Part VIII.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 95
- 2005, c. 54, s. 151
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-95-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 95"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Particulars of class

- **[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be stated legibly on a share certificate issued after the coming
  into force of this section by an association that is authorized to
  issue shares of more than one class or series

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attached to the shares of each class and series existing when the
    share certificate is issued; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the class or series of shares that the
    certificate represents has rights, privileges, restrictions or
    conditions attached thereto and that the association will furnish a
    shareholder, on demand and without charge, with a full copy of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the text of the rights, privileges, restrictions
      and conditions attached to each class authorized to be issued and
      to each series in so far as those rights, privileges, restrictions
      and conditions have been fixed by the directors, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the text of the authority of the directors, if
      the directors are so authorized, to fix the rights, privileges,
      restrictions and conditions of subsequent series of shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a share certificate issued by an association
  contains the statement mentioned in paragraph (1)(b), the association
  shall provide a shareholder, on demand and without charge, with a full
  copy of the texts referred to in subparagraphs (1)(b)(i) and (ii).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fractional share

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** An association may issue
a certificate for a fractional share or may issue in place thereof a
scrip certificate in bearer form that entitles the holder to receive a
certificate for a full share by exchanging scrip certificates
aggregating a full share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scrip certificates

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** The directors of an
association may attach conditions to any scrip certificate issued by the
association, including conditions that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the scrip certificate becomes void if not exchanged
  for a share certificate representing a full share before a specified
  date; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any shares for which the scrip certificate is
  exchangeable may, notwithstanding any pre-emptive right, be issued by
  the association to any person and the proceeds thereof may be
  distributed rateably to the holders of all the scrip certificates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of fractional shares

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a fractional share issued by an association is not entitled
  to exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect of the
  fractional share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of scrip certificates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a scrip certificate is not entitled to
  exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect of the
  scrip certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dealings with registered owner

- **[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association or a trustee within the meaning of section 278 may,
  subject to sections 145 to 149 and 154, treat the registered owner of
  a security as the person exclusively entitled to vote, to receive
  notices, to receive any interest, dividend or other payment in respect
  of the security and to exercise all of the rights and powers of an
  owner of the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive registered holder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), an association may
  treat a person as a registered security holder entitled to exercise
  all of the rights of the security holder that the person represents,
  if that person provides the association with evidence as described in
  subsection 134(4) that the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the heir or personal representative of a deceased
    security holder or the personal representative of the heirs of the
    deceased security holder;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the personal representative of a registered
    security holder who is a minor, an incompetent person or a missing
    person; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a liquidator of, or a trustee in bankruptcy for, a
    registered security holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permissible registered holder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person on whom the ownership of a security of an
  association devolves by operation of law, other than a person
  described in subsection (2), provides proof of that person's authority
  to exercise rights or privileges in respect of a security of the
  association that is not registered in the person's name, the
  association shall, subject to this Act, treat that person as entitled
  to exercise those rights or privileges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity of association

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An association is not required to inquire into the
  existence of, or see to the performance or observance of, any duty
  owed to a third person by a registered holder of any of its securities
  or by anyone whom it treats, as permitted or required by this Part, as
  the owner or registered holder thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 100
- 2005, c. 54, s. 152
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-100-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 100"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minors

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** If a minor exercises
any rights of ownership in the securities of an association, no
subsequent repudiation or avoidance is effective against the
association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 101
- 2005, c. 54, s. 153(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-101-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 101"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint shareholders

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** An association may
treat as owners of a security the survivors of persons to whom the
security was issued as joint holders, if the association receives proof
satisfactory to it of the death of any of the joint holders.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission of securities

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the provisions of Part
  VIII and any applicable law relating to the collection of taxes, a
  person referred to in paragraph 100(2)(a) is entitled to become
  registered as the owner of a security, or to designate another person
  to be registered as the owner of a security, if the person referred to
  in paragraph 100(2)(a) delivers to the association or its transfer
  agent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the original grant of probate or of letters of
    administration, or a copy thereof certified to be a true copy by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the court that granted the probate or letters of
      administration,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a trust company incorporated under the [Trust
      and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) or under the laws of a
      province, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a lawyer or notary acting on behalf of the
      person referred to in paragraph 100(2)(a), or
