    unissued shares into series and fix the maximum number of shares, if
    any, in each series and the rights, privileges, restrictions and
    conditions attached thereto;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} authorize the directors to change the rights,
    privileges, restrictions and conditions attached to unissued shares
    of any series;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} revoke, diminish or enlarge any authority conferred
    under paragraphs (f) and (g);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} increase or decrease the number of directors or the
    minimum or maximum number of directors, subject to subsection
    169(1);

  - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} change the name of the association; or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} change the province in which the head office of the
    association is situated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of by-law

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A by-law respecting a change to the name of the
  association is not effective until it is approved by the
  Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Letters patent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the name of an association or the province in
  Canada in which the head office of the association is situated is
  changed under this section, the Superintendent may issue letters
  patent to amend the association's incorporating instrument
  accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Letters patent issued under subsection (3) become
  effective on the day stated in the letters patent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 221
- 2001, c. 9, s. 283
- 2005, c. 54, s. 183
- 2007, c. 6, s. 151
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-221-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 221"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class vote

- **[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holders of shares of a class or, subject to subsection (2), of a
  series are, unless the by-laws otherwise provide in the case of an
  amendment to the by-laws referred to in paragraph (a), (b) or (e),
  entitled to vote separately as a class or series on a proposal to
  amend the by-laws to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} increase or decrease any maximum number of
    authorized shares of that class, or increase any maximum number of
    authorized shares of a class having rights or privileges equal or
    superior to the shares of that class;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} effect an exchange, reclassification or
    cancellation of all or part of the shares of a class;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} add, change or remove the rights, privileges,
    restrictions or conditions attached to the shares of a class and,
    without limiting the generality of the foregoing,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} remove or change prejudicially rights to accrued
      dividends or rights to cumulative dividends,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} add, remove or change prejudicially redemption
      rights,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} reduce or remove a dividend preference or a
      liquidation preference, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} add, remove or change prejudicially conversion
      privileges, options, voting, transfer or pre-emptive rights, or
      rights to acquire securities of the association, or sinking fund
      provisions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} increase the rights or privileges of any class of
    shares having rights or privileges equal or superior to the shares
    of that class;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} create a new class of shares equal or superior to
    the shares of that class;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} make any class of shares having rights or
    privileges inferior to the shares of that class equal or superior to
    the shares of that class; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} effect an exchange or create a right of exchange of
    all or part of the shares of another class into the shares of that
    class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right limited

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holders of a series of shares of a class are
  entitled to vote separately as a series under subsection (1) if that
  series is affected by an addition or amendment to the by-laws in a
  manner different from other shares of the same class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) apply whether or not the
  shares of a class otherwise carry the right to vote.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate resolutions

**[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** A proposed addition or
amendment to the by-laws referred to in subsection 222(1) is adopted
when the holders of the shares of each class or series entitled to vote
separately thereon as a class or series have approved the addition or
amendment by a special resolution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposal to amend

- **[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a member
  may, in accordance with sections 152 and 153, make a proposal to make
  an application referred to in section 219 or to make, amend or repeal
  the by-laws referred to in subsection 221(1) of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of amendment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a meeting at which a proposal to amend the
  incorporating instrument or to make, amend or repeal the by-laws of an
  association is to be considered must set out the proposal.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 224
- 2001, c. 9, s. 284
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights preserved

**[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** No amendment to the
incorporating instrument or by-laws of an association affects an
existing cause of action or claim or liability to prosecution in favour
of or against the association or its directors or officers, or any
civil, criminal or administrative action or proceeding to which the
association or any of its directors or officers are a party.

#### [Amalgamation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-98808 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to amalgamate

**[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** On the joint
application of two or more bodies corporate incorporated by or under an
Act of Parliament, including associations, the Minister may issue
letters patent amalgamating and continuing the applicants as one
association if the proposed capital and corporate structure of the
amalgamated association meets the requirements for an association to be
incorporated under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 226
- 2001, c. 9, s. 285
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamation agreement

- **[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  applicant proposing to amalgamate shall enter into an amalgamation
  agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every amalgamation agreement shall set out the terms
  and means of effecting the amalgamation and, in particular,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the amalgamated association and the
    province in which its head office is to be situated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and place of ordinary residence of each
    proposed director of the amalgamated association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the membership shares of each
    association are to be converted into membership shares in the
    amalgamated association;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the shares of each applicant
    are to be converted into shares or other securities of the
    amalgamated association;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if any membership shares of an applicant are not to
    be converted into membership shares of the amalgamated association
    the amount of money or securities that the members are to receive in
    addition to or in lieu of membership shares of the amalgamated
    association;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if any shares of an applicant are not to be
    converted into shares or other securities of the amalgamated
    association, the amount of money or securities that the holders of
    those shares are to receive in addition to or in lieu of shares or
    other securities of the amalgamated association;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the manner of payment of money in lieu of the issue
    of fractional shares of the amalgamated association or of any other
    body corporate that are to be issued in the amalgamation;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the proposed by-laws of the amalgamated
    association;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} details of any other matter necessary to perfect
    the amalgamation and to provide for the subsequent management and
    operation of the amalgamated association; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the proposed effective date of the amalgamation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cross ownership of shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If membership shares or shares of one of the
  applicants are held by or on behalf of another of the applicants,
  other than membership shares or shares held in the capacity of a
  personal representative or by way of security, the amalgamation
  agreement must provide for the cancellation of those membership shares
  or shares when the amalgamation becomes effective without any
  repayment of capital in respect thereof, and no provision shall be
  made in the agreement for the conversion of those membership shares or
  shares into membership shares or shares of the amalgamated
  association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 227
- 2005, c. 54, s. 184
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-227-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 227"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of agreement by Superintendent

**[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** An amalgamation
agreement must be submitted to the Superintendent for approval and any
approval of the agreement under subsection 229(4) by the members and the
holders of any class or series of shares of an applicant is invalid
unless, before the date of the approval, the Superintendent has approved
the agreement in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 228
- 2007, c. 6, s. 152
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-228-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 228"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of each applicant shall submit an amalgamation agreement for
  approval to a meeting of the members or shareholders of the applicant
  association or subsidiary of which they are directors and, subject to
  subsection (3), to the holders of each class or series of such shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each share of an applicant carries the right to vote
  in respect of an amalgamation agreement whether or not it otherwise
  carries the right to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate vote for class or series

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holders of shares of a class or series of shares
  of each applicant are entitled to vote separately as a class or series
  in respect of an amalgamation agreement if the agreement contains a
  provision that, if it were contained in a proposed amendment to the
  by-laws or incorporating instrument of the applicant, would entitle
  those holders to vote separately as a class or series.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special resolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), an amalgamation agreement
  is approved when the members or shareholders of each applicant
  association or subsidiary have approved the amalgamation by special
  resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An amalgamation agreement may provide that, at any
  time before the issue of letters patent of amalgamation, the agreement
  may be terminated by the directors of an applicant notwithstanding
  that the agreement has been approved by the members or shareholders of
  all or any of the applicant associations or subsidiaries.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 229
- 2005, c. 54, s. 185
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-229-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 229"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vertical short-form amalgamation

- **[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may, without complying with sections 227 to 229,
  amalgamate with one or more bodies corporate that are incorporated by
  or under an Act of Parliament if the body or bodies corporate, as the
  case may be, are wholly-owned subsidiaries of the association and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation is approved by a resolution of the
    directors of the association and of each amalgamating subsidiary;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resolutions provide that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shares of each amalgamating subsidiary will
      be cancelled without any repayment of capital in respect thereof,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the letters patent of amalgamation and the
      by-laws of the amalgamated association will be the same as the
      incorporating instrument and the by-laws of the amalgamating
      association that is the holding body corporate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} no securities will be issued by the amalgamated
      association in connection with the amalgamation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Horizontal short-form amalgamation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Two or more bodies corporate incorporated by or under
  an Act of Parliament may amalgamate and continue as an association
  without complying with sections 227 to 229 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least one of the applicants is an association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicants are all wholly-owned subsidiaries of
    the same holding body corporate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation is approved by a resolution of the
    directors of each of the applicants; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the resolutions provide that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shares of all applicants, except those of one
      of the applicants that is an association, will be cancelled
      without any repayment of capital in respect of those shares,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the letters patent of amalgamation and the
      by-laws of the amalgamated association will be the same as the
      incorporating instrument and the by-laws of the amalgamating
      association whose shares are not cancelled, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the stated capital of the amalgamating
      association and bodies corporate whose shares are cancelled will
      be added to the stated capital of the amalgamating association
      whose shares are not cancelled.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 230
- 2001, c. 9, s. 286
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint application to Minister

- **[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), unless an
  amalgamation agreement is terminated in accordance with subsection
  229(5), the applicants shall, within three months after the approval
  of the agreement in accordance with subsection 229(4) or the approval
  of the directors in accordance with section 230, jointly apply to the
  Minister for letters patent of amalgamation continuing the applicants
  as one association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions precedent to application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No application for the issue of letters patent under
  subsection (1) may be made unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notice of intention to make such an application has
    been published at least once a week for a period of four consecutive
    weeks in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in a
    newspaper in general circulation at or near the place where the head
    office of each applicant is situated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application is supported by satisfactory
    evidence that the applicants have complied with the requirements of
    this Part relating to amalgamations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of sections 24 to 26

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If two or more bodies corporate, none of which is an
  association, apply for letters patent under subsection (1), sections
  24 to 26 apply in respect of the application with any modifications
  that the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing letters patent of amalgamation
  continuing the applicants as one association, the Minister shall take
  into account all matters that the Minister considers relevant to the
  application, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sources of continuing financial support for the
    amalgamated association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the soundness and feasibility of the plans of the
    applicants for the future conduct and development of the business of
    the amalgamated association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business record and experience of the
    applicants;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reputation of the applicants for being operated
    in a manner that is consistent with the standards of good character
    and integrity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the amalgamated association will be
    operated responsibly by persons with the competence and experience
    suitable for involvement in the operation of a financial
    institution;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the impact of any integration of the operations and
    businesses of the applicants on the conduct of those operations and
    businesses;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} whether the association is to be operated in
    accordance with cooperative principles; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in
    Canada and, in particular, the cooperative financial system in
    Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 231
- 2001, c. 9, s. 287
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of letters patent

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an application has been made
  to the Minister in accordance with section 231, the Minister may issue
  letters patent of amalgamation continuing the applicants as one
  association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where letters patent are issued pursuant to this
  section, section 28 applies with such modifications as the
  circumstances require in respect of the issue of the letters patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) notice of the issuance of
  letters patent pursuant to subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court enforcement

- **[[232.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an association or any director,
  officer, employee or agent of an association is contravening or has
  failed to comply with any term or condition made in respect of the
  issuance of letters patent of amalgamation, the Minister may, in
  addition to any other action that may be taken under this Act, apply
  to a court for an order directing the association or the director,
  officer, employee or agent to comply with the term or condition, cease
  the contravention or do any thing that is required to be done, and on
  the application the court may so order and make any other order it
  thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal from an order of a court under this section
  lies in the same manner as, and to the same court to which, an appeal
  may be taken from any other order of the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 288
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

- **[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the day provided for in the letters patent issued under section 232,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation of the applicants and their
    continuance as one association becomes effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the property of each applicant continues to be the
    property of the amalgamated association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamated association continues to be liable
    for the obligations of each applicant;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any existing cause of action, claim or liability to
    prosecution is unaffected;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any civil, criminal or administrative action or
    proceeding pending by or against an applicant may be continued to be
    prosecuted by or against the amalgamated association;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any conviction against, or ruling, order or
    judgment in favour of or against, an applicant may be enforced by or
    against the amalgamated association;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if any director or officer of an applicant
    continues as a director or officer of the amalgamated association,
    any disclosure by that director or officer of a material interest in
    any contract made to the applicant shall be deemed to be disclosure
    to the amalgamated association; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the letters patent of amalgamation are the
    incorporating instrument of the amalgamated association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minutes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any deemed disclosure under paragraph (1)(g) shall be
  recorded in the minutes of the first meeting of directors of the
  amalgamated association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in this Act or the
  regulations, the Governor in Council may, by order, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, grant to an association in respect of
  which the letters patent were issued pursuant to subsection 232(1)
  permission to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engage in a business activity specified in the
    order that an association is not otherwise permitted by this Act to
    engage in and that one or more of the amalgamating bodies corporate
    was engaging in at the time application for the letters patent was
    made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} continue to have issued and outstanding debt
    obligations the issue of which is not authorized by this Act if the
    debt obligations were outstanding at the time the application for
    the letters patent was made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 52\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} hold assets that an association is not otherwise
    permitted by this Act to hold if the assets were held by one or more
    of the amalgamating bodies corporate at the time the application for
    the letters patent was made;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} acquire and hold assets that an association is not
    otherwise permitted by this Act to acquire or hold if one or more of
    the amalgamating bodies corporate were obliged, at the time the
    application for the letters patent was made, to acquire those
    assets; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} maintain outside Canada any records or registers
    required by this Act to be maintained in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permission granted under any of paragraphs (3)(a)
  to (f) shall be expressed to be granted for a period specified in the
  order not exceeding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in paragraph
    (3)(a), thirty days after the date of issue of the letters patent
    or, where the activity is conducted pursuant to an agreement
    existing on the date of issue of the letters patent, the expiration
    of the agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in paragraph
    (3)(b), ten years; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in any of
    paragraphs (3)(d) to (f), two years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), the Governor in Council
  may, by order, renew a permission granted by order under
  subsection (3) with respect to any matter described in any of
  paragraphs (3)(b) to (e) for such further period or periods as the
  Governor in Council deems necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council shall not grant to an
  association any permission

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters described in paragraph
    (3)(b) that purports to be effective more than ten years after the
    date of the approval for the association to commence and carry on
    business unless the Governor in Council is satisfied on the basis of
    evidence on oath provided by an officer of the association that the
    association will not be able at law to redeem at the end of the ten
    years the outstanding debt obligations to which the permission
    relates; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters described in paragraphs
    (3)(d) and (e) that purports to be effective more than ten years
    after the date of issue of the letters patent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 233
- 1994, c. 47, s. 52
- 2007, c. 6, s. 153
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-233-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 233"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Transfer of Business]{.HTitleText3} {#h-98937 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale by association

- **[[233.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An association may sell all or
  substantially all of its assets to a financial institution
  incorporated under an Act of Parliament, a bank holding company or an
  authorized foreign bank in respect of its business in Canada if the
  purchasing financial institution, bank holding company or authorized
  foreign bank assumes all or substantially all of the liabilities of
  the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An agreement of purchase and sale (in subsection (3),
  section 233.2, subsections 233.3(1) and (4) and section 233.5 referred
  to as a "sale agreement") must set out the terms of, and means of
  effecting, the sale of assets referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Act, the consideration for a
  sale referred to in subsection (1) may be cash or fully paid
  securities of the purchasing financial institution, bank holding
  company or authorized foreign bank or in part cash and in part fully
  paid securities of the purchasing financial institution, bank holding
  company or authorized foreign bank or any other consideration that is
  provided for in the sale agreement.

- Meaning of [[authorized foreign bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[bank
  holding company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[authorized foreign
  bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[bank holding
  company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the meaning assigned to those
  expressions by section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 289
- 2014, c. 39, s. 285
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-233.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 233.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement to Superintendent

**[[233.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233.2 .sectionLabel}** A sale agreement
must be sent to the Superintendent before it is submitted to members and
shareholders of the selling association under subsection 233.3(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 289
- 2007, c. 6, s. 154
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-233.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 233.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[233.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a selling
  association shall submit a sale agreement for approval to a meeting of
  the members, and to a meeting of shareholders, of the association and,
  subject to subsection (3), to the holders of each class or series of
  shares of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each share of a selling association carries the right
  to vote in respect of a sale referred to in subsection 233.1(1)
  whether or not the share otherwise carries the right to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class vote

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holders of shares of a class or series of shares
  of a selling association are entitled to vote separately as a class or
  series in respect of a sale referred to in subsection 233.1(1) only if
  the shares of the class or series are affected by the sale in a manner
  different from the shares of another class or series.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special resolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A sale agreement is approved when the members and
  shareholders, and the holders of each class or series of shares
  entitled to vote separately as a class or series under subsection (3),
  of the selling association have approved the sale by special
  resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 289
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Abandoning sale

**[[233.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233.4 .sectionLabel}** If a special
resolution approving a sale under subsection 233.3(4) so states, the
directors of a selling association may, subject to the rights of third
parties, abandon the sale without further approval of the members and
shareholders.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 289
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to Minister

- **[[233.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), unless a
  sale agreement is abandoned in accordance with section 233.4, the
  selling association shall, within three months after the approval of
  the sale agreement in accordance with subsection 233.3(4), apply to
  the Minister for approval of the sale agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions precedent to application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No application for approval under subsection (1) may
  be made unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice of intention to make the application has
    been published at least once a week for a period of four consecutive
    weeks in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in a
    newspaper in general circulation at or near the place where the head
    office of the selling association is situated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application is supported by satisfactory
    evidence that the selling association has complied with the
    requirements of sections 233.1 to 233.4 and this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A sale agreement has no force or effect until it has
  been approved by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an application has been made to the Minister in
  accordance with subsections (1) and (2), the Minister may approve the
  sale agreement to which the application relates.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 289
:::

### [Corporate Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-98981 .Subheading}

#### [Head Office and Corporate Records]{.HTitleText3} {#h-98982 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head office

- **[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall at all times have a head office in the province
  specified in its incorporating instrument or by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of head office

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors of an association may change the
  address of the head office within the province specified in the
  incorporating instrument or by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of address

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association shall send to the Superintendent,
  within fifteen days after any change of address of its head office, a
  notice of the change of address.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 234
- 2005, c. 54, s. 186
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-234-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 234"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

- **[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall prepare and maintain records containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its incorporating instrument and the by-laws of the
    association and all amendments thereto;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} minutes of meetings and resolutions of members and
    shareholders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs 432(1)(a)
    and (c) to (g) contained in all returns provided to the
    Superintendent pursuant to section 432;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} particulars of any authorizations, conditions and
    limitations established by the Superintendent under section 61 or
    subsection 62(1) that are from time to time applicable to the
    association; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} written approvals of the Minister and the
    Superintendent relating to special resolutions of members and
    shareholders and changes in the by-laws of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the records described in subsection
  (1), an association shall prepare and maintain adequate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} corporate accounting records;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} records containing minutes of meetings and
    resolutions of the directors and any committee thereof; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} records showing, for each member and customer of
    the association, on a daily basis, particulars of the transactions
    between the association and that member and customer and the balance
    owing to or by the association in respect of that member or
    customer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamated and former-Act associations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b) and subsection
  (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a body corporate amalgamated and
    continued as an association under this Act, "records" includes
    similar records required by law to be maintained by the body
    corporate before it was so amalgamated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the former-Act association,
    "records" includes similar records required by law to be maintained
    by the association before the coming into force of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 235
- 2007, c. 6, s. 155(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-235-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 235"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of records

- **[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  records described in section 235 shall be kept at the head office of
  the association or at such other place in Canada as the directors
  think fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of place of records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where any of the records described in section 235 are
  not kept at the head office of an association, the association shall
  notify the Superintendent of the place where the records are kept.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The records described in section 235, other than
  those described in paragraph 235(2)(c), shall at all reasonable times
  be open to inspection by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to association records

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Members, shareholders and creditors of an association
  and their personal representatives may examine the records referred to
  in subsection 235(1) during the usual business hours of the
  association and may take extracts from them free of charge or have
  copies of them made on payment of a reasonable fee. If the association
  is a distributing association, any other person may on payment of a
  reasonable fee examine those records during the usual business hours
  of the association and take extracts from them or have copies of them
  made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic access

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} An association may make the information contained
  in records referred to in subsection 235(1) available to persons by
  any system of mechanical or electronic data processing or any other
  information storage device that is capable of reproducing the records
  in intelligible written form within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of by-laws

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every member and every shareholder of an association
  is entitled, on request made not more frequently than once in each
  calendar year, to receive, free of charge, one copy of the by-laws of
  the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 236
- 2001, c. 9, s. 290
- 2005, c. 54, s. 187
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-236-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 236"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lists

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is entitled to a basic list of members or shareholders of
  an association (in this section referred to as the "applicant") may
  request the association to furnish the applicant with a basic list
  within ten days after receipt by the association of the affidavit
  referred to in subsection (2) and, on payment of a reasonable fee by
  the applicant, the association shall comply with the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affidavit and contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request under subsection (1) must be accompanied by
  an affidavit containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the applicant,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address for service of the entity, if
    the applicant is an entity, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking that the basic list and any
    supplemental lists obtained pursuant to subsections (5) and (6) will
    not be used except as permitted under section 239,

  and, if the applicant is an entity, the affidavit shall be made by a
  director or an officer of the entity, or any person acting in a
  similar capacity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to list

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A member, shareholder or creditor of an association
  or their personal representative --- or if the association is a
  distributing association, any person --- is entitled to a basic list
  of members or shareholders of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Basic list

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A basic list of members or shareholders of an
  association consists of a list of members or shareholders that is made
  up to a date not more than ten days before the receipt of the
  affidavit referred to in subsection (2) and that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names of the members or shareholders of the
    association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the voting rights of each member or the number of
    shares owned by each shareholder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of each member or shareholder as shown
    in the records of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supplemental lists

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person requiring an association to supply a basic
  list of members or shareholders may, if the person states in the
  accompanying affidavit that supplemental lists are required, request
  the association or its agent, on payment of a reasonable fee, to
  provide supplemental lists of members or shareholders setting out any
  changes from the basic list in the names and addresses of the members
  or shareholders and the voting rights of each member or the number of
  shares owned by each shareholder for each business day following the
  date to which the basic list is made up.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When supplemental lists to be furnished

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An association or its agent shall provide a
  supplemental list of members or shareholders required under subsection
  (5)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within ten days following the date the basic list
    is provided, where the information relates to changes that took
    place prior to that date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within ten days following the day to which the
    supplemental list relates, where the information relates to changes
    that took place on or after the date the basic list was provided.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 237
- 2005, c. 54, s. 188
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-237-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 237"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Option holders

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** A person requiring an
association to supply a basic list or a supplemental list of members or
shareholders may also require the association to include in that list
the name and address of any known holder of an option or right to
acquire membership shares or shares of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of list

**[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239 .sectionLabel}** A list of members or
shareholders obtained under section 237 shall not be used by any person
except in connection with

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an effort to influence the voting of members or
  shareholders of the association;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offer to acquire shares of the association; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other matter relating to the affairs of the
  association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of records

- **[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  register or other record required or authorized by this Act to be
  prepared and maintained by an association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may be in a bound or loose-leaf form or in a
    photographic film form; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may be entered or recorded by any system of
    mechanical or electronic data processing or any other information
    storage device that is capable of reproducing any required
    information in intelligible written form within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conversion of records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Registers and records maintained in one form may be
  converted to any other form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of converted records

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 243, an association may
  destroy any register or other record referred to in subsection (1) at
  any time after the register or other record has been converted to
  another form.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of records

**[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** An association and its
agents shall take reasonable precautions to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prevent loss or destruction of,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent falsification of entries in,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} facilitate detection and correction of inaccuracies
  in, and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that unauthorized persons do not have access
  to or use of information in

the registers and records required or authorized by this Act to be
prepared and maintained.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to maintain copies and
process information in Canada

- **[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Superintendent is of the opinion that it is incompatible with the
  fulfilment of the Superintendent's responsibilities under this Act for
  an association to maintain, in another country, copies of records
  referred to in section 235 or of its central securities register or
  for an association to process, in another country, information or data
  relating to the preparation and maintenance of those records or of its
  central securities register --- or if the Superintendent is advised by
  the Minister that, in the opinion of the Minister, it is not in the
  national interest for an association to do any of those activities in
  another country --- the Superintendent shall direct the association to
  not maintain those copies, or to not process the information or data,
  as the case may be, in that other country or to maintain those copies
  or to process the information or data only in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Association to comply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall without delay comply with any
  direction issued under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 242
- 2001, c. 9, s. 291
- 2005, c. 54, s. 189
- 2007, c. 6, s. 156
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-242-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 242"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of records

**[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243 .sectionLabel}** An association shall
retain

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the members register;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the records of the association referred to in
  subsection 235(1);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any record of the association referred to in
  paragraph 235(2)(a) or (b); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the central securities register.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations respecting the records, papers and documents to be
retained by an association and the length of time those records, papers
and documents are to be retained.

#### [Securities Registers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99088 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Central securities register

- **[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall maintain a central securities register in which it
  shall record the securities, within the meaning of section 88, issued
  by it in registered form, showing in respect of each class or series
  of securities

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names, alphabetically arranged, and latest
    known addresses of the persons who are security holders, and the
    names and latest known addresses of the persons who have been
    security holders;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of securities held by each security
    holder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date and particulars of the issue and transfer
    of each security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former-Act and amalgamated associations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), "central
  securities register" includes similar registers required by law to be
  maintained by the former-Act association, or by a body corporate
  amalgamated and continued as an association under this Act, before the
  coming into force of this section or the amalgamation, as the case may
  be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to central securities register

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Members, shareholders and creditors of an association
  and their personal representatives may examine the central securities
  register during the usual business hours of the association and may
  take extracts from it free of charge or have copies of it made on
  payment of a reasonable fee. If the association is a distributing
  association, any other person may on payment of a reasonable fee
  examine the central securities register during the usual business
  hours of the association and take extracts from it or have copies of
  it made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The association may make the information contained in
  the central securities register available by any mechanical or
  electronic data processing system or other information storage device
  that is capable of reproducing it in intelligible written form within
  a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affidavit and undertaking

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person who wishes to examine the central securities
  register, take extracts from it or have copies of it made shall
  provide the association with an affidavit containing their name and
  address --- or if they are an entity, the name and address for service
  of the entity --- and with an undertaking that the information
  contained in the register will not be used except in the same way as a
  list of members or shareholders may be used under section 239. In the
  case of an entity, the affidavit is to be sworn by a director or
  officer of the entity or a person acting in a similar capacity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supplementary information

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person who wishes to examine a central securities
  register, take extracts from it or have copies of it made may on
  payment of a reasonable fee, if they state in the accompanying
  affidavit that supplementary information is required, request the
  association or its agent to provide supplementary information setting
  out any changes made to the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When supplementary information to be
  provided

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An association or its agent shall provide the
  supplementary information within

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 10 days after the day on which the central
    securities register is examined if the changes take place before
    that day; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 10 days after the day to which the supplementary
    information relates if the changes take place on or after the day on
    which the central securities register is examined.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 245
- 2001, c. 9, s. 292
- 2005, c. 54, s. 190
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-245-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 245"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agents

**[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** An association may
appoint an agent to maintain its central securities register.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of central securities register

**[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** The central securities
register of an association shall be maintained by the association at its
head office or at any other place in Canada designated by the directors
of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of registration

**[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** Registration of the
issue or transfer of a security in the central securities register is
complete and valid registration for all purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of certificates

**[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** An association, its
agent or a trustee within the meaning of section 278 is not required to
produce

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cancelled security certificate in registered form
  or an instrument referred to in subsection 77(1) that is cancelled or
  a like cancelled instrument in registered form after six years from
  the date of its cancellation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cancelled security certificate in bearer form or an
  instrument referred to in subsection 77(1) that is cancelled or a like
  cancelled instrument in bearer form after the date of its
  cancellation; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an instrument referred to in subsection 77(1) or a
  like instrument, irrespective of its form, after the date of its
  expiration.

#### [Corporate Name and Seal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99122 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of name

**[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** An association shall
set out its name in legible characters in all contracts, invoices,
negotiable instruments and other documents evidencing rights or
obligations with respect to other parties that are issued or made by or
on behalf of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate seal

- **[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association may adopt a corporate seal and change one that it adopted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of unsealed documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document executed on behalf of an association is
  not invalid merely because a corporate seal is not affixed to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 251
- 2005, c. 54, s. 191
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 251"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[252 to 259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252_to_259
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 15, s. 130\]]{.Repealed}

### [Insiders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-99133 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99134 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and sections 261 to 267,

  [[business combination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business combination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an acquisition
      of all or substantially all of the assets of one body corporate by
      another, an amalgamation of two or more bodies corporate or any
      similar reorganization between two or more bodies
      corporate; ([regroupement d'entreprises]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option, transferable by
      delivery, to demand delivery of a specified number or amount of
      shares at a fixed price within a specified time but does not
      include an option or right to acquire shares of the body corporate
      that granted the option or right to acquire; ([option
      d'achat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributing association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributing association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      2005, c. 54, s. 192\]]{.Repealed}

  [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2005, c. 54, s.
      192\]]{.Repealed}

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an association,
      means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an officer as defined in paragraph (a) of the
        definition "officer" in section 2, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any natural person who performs functions for
        the association similar to those performed by a person referred
        to in paragraph (a) of the definition "officer" in section
        2; ([dirigeant d'une association]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option, transferable by
      delivery, to deliver a specified number or amount of shares at a
      fixed price within a specified time; ([option de
      vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a voting share and includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security currently convertible into a voting
        share, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a currently exercisable option or a right to
        acquire a voting share or a security referred to in paragraph
        (a),

      but does not include a membership
      share. ([action]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and sections 261 to
  267, a person controls a body corporate when the person controls the
  body corporate within the meaning of section 3, determined without
  regard to paragraph 3(1)(e).

- [(3) and (4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2005, c. 54, s.
  192\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 260
- 2005, c. 54, s. 192
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-260-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 260"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Insider Reporting]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99155 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insider report

**[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** An insider shall submit
an insider report in accordance with the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 261
- 1997, c. 15, s. 131
- 2005, c. 54, s. 193
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 261"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption by Superintendent

**[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** On application by an
insider, the Superintendent may in writing and on any terms that the
Superintendent thinks fit exempt the insider from any of the
requirements of section 261. The exemption may be given retroactive
effect and the Superintendent shall publish the particulars of the
exemption and the reasons for it in a periodical available to the
public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 262
- 2005, c. 54, s. 193
:::

[Previous
Version](section-262-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 262"){.PITLink}

c. 54, s. 193\]]{.Repealed}

Version](section-262.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 262.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations for carrying out the purposes of sections 261 and
262, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining "insider" for the purposes of sections 261
  and 262;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the form and content of an insider report;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the submission or publication of an
  insider report.

::: HistoricalNote

- 2005, c. 54, s. 193

54, s. 193\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Insider Trading]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99173 .Subheading}

Meaning of [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means with respect to a
  distributing association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of a subsidiary of the
    association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of a body corporate that
    enters into a business combination with the association; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person employed or retained by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- short sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No insider may knowingly sell, directly or
  indirectly, a security of a distributing association or of any of the
  distributing association's affiliates if the insider does not own or
  has not fully paid for the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), an insider may sell a
  security that they do not own if they own another security that is
  convertible into the security that was sold or they own an option or
  right to acquire the security that was sold, and if within 10 days
  after the sale they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exercise the conversion privilege, option or right
    and deliver the security so acquired to the purchaser; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} transfer the convertible security, option or right
    to the purchaser.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- calls and puts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No insider may knowingly, directly or indirectly, buy
  or sell a call or put in respect of a security of an association or of
  any of the association's affiliates.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 265
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-265-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 265"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Civil remedies]{.HTitleText3} {#h-99191 .Subheading}

Extended meaning of [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and sections 266.1 and 267,
  [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} with respect to an association means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an affiliate of the association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of the association or of any
    person described in paragraph (b), (f) or (h);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a member who holds more than one per cent of the
    membership shares of the association;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a central cooperative credit society that is a
    member of the association and any other member of the association
    designated by the Superintendent;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person who beneficially owns directly or
    indirectly, or who exercises control or direction over or has a
    combination of ownership, control and direction in respect of,
    shares of the association carrying more than the prescribed
    percentage of the voting rights attached to all of the association's
    outstanding shares not including shares held by the person as
    underwriter while those shares are in the course of a distribution
    to the public;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person, other than a person described in
    paragraph (h), who is employed or retained by the association or by
    a person described in paragraph (h);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person who engages in or proposes to engage in
    any business or professional activity with or on behalf of the
    association;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person who received material confidential
    information concerning the association while they were a person
    described in any of paragraphs (a) to (h);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a person who receives material confidential
    information from a person who is and who they know or ought
    reasonably to have known is a person described in this subsection,
    including in this paragraph, or subsection (3) or (4); or

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person.

- Extended meaning of [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, each of the
  following is deemed to be a security of an association:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a put, call, option or other right or obligation to
    purchase or sell a security of the association; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a security of another entity, the market price of
    which varies materially with the market price of the securities of
    the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed insider --- take-over bid or
  business combination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and subsection
  266.1(1), a person who proposes to make a take-over bid as defined in
  the regulations for securities of an association or to enter into a
  business combination with an association is an insider of the
  association with respect to material confidential information obtained
  from the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed insider --- affiliate or
  associate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An insider of a person referred to in subsection (3),
  or the person's affiliate or associate, is an insider of the
  association referred to in that subsection. Paragraphs (1)(b) to (k)
  apply in making this determination except that references to
  "association" are to be read as references to "person described in
  subsection (3)".

- Meaning of [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (4), [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means with respect to a person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate that the person directly or
    indirectly controls, determined without regard to paragraph 3(1)(d),
    or of which they beneficially own shares or securities currently
    convertible into shares carrying more than 10% of the voting rights
    under all circumstances or by reason of the occurrence of an event
    that has occurred and is continuing or a currently exercisable
    option or right to purchase the shares or convertible securities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a partner of the person acting on behalf of the
    partnership of which they are partners;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trust or estate in which the person has a
    substantial beneficial interest or in respect of which they serve as
    a trustee or a liquidator of the succession or in a similar
    capacity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a spouse or common-law partner of the person;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a child of the person or of their spouse or
    common-law partner; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if that relative has the same residence as the
    person, a relative of the person or of their spouse or common-law
    partner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insider trading --- compensation to
  sellers and purchasers

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An insider of an association who purchases or sells a
  security of the association with knowledge of confidential information
  that if it were generally known might reasonably be expected to
  materially affect the value of any of the securities of the
  association is liable to compensate the seller or purchaser of the
  security, as the case may be, for any loss suffered by them as a
  result of the purchase or sale unless the insider establishes that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the insider reasonably believed that the
    information had been generally disclosed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information was known or ought reasonably to
    have been known by the seller or purchaser; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the purchase or sale of the security took place in
    the prescribed circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insider trading --- compensation to
  association

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The insider is accountable to the association for any
  benefit or advantage received or receivable by the insider as a result
  of a purchase or sale described in subsection (6) unless they
  establish the circumstances described in paragraph (6)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 266
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-266-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 266"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tipping --- compensation to sellers and
purchasers

- **[[266.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An insider of an association who
  discloses confidential information with respect to the association
  that has not been generally disclosed and that if it were generally
  known might reasonably be expected to materially affect the value of
  any of the securities of the association is liable to compensate any
  person who subsequently sells securities of the association to or
  purchases them from any person who received the information unless the
  insider establishes that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the insider reasonably believed that the
    information had been generally disclosed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information was known or ought reasonably to
    have been known by the person who alleges that they suffered the
    loss;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the insider is not a person described in
    subsection 266(3) or (4), the disclosure of the information was
    necessary in the course of their business; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the insider is a person described in subsection
    266(3) or (4), the disclosure of the information was necessary to
    effect the take-over bid or business combination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tipping --- compensation to association

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The insider is accountable to the association for any
  benefit or advantage received or receivable by them as a result of a
  disclosure of information as described in subsection (1) unless they
  establish the circumstances described in paragraph (1)(a), (c) or (d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measure of damages

- **[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  court may assess damages under subsection 266(6) or 266.1(1) in
  accord­ance with any measure of damages that it considers relevant in
  the circumstances. However, in assessing damages in respect of a
  security of a distributing association, the court shall consider the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the plaintiff is a purchaser, the price that
    they paid for the security less the average market price of the
    security over the 20 trading days immediately following general
    disclosure of the information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the plaintiff is a seller, the average market
    price of the security over the 20 trading days immediately following
    general disclosure of the information, less the price that they
    received for the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability --- more than one insider

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If more than one insider is liable under subsection
  266(6) or 266.1(1) with respect to the same transaction or series of
  transactions, their liability is joint and several, or solidary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An action to enforce a right created by subsection
  266(6) or (7) or section 266.1 may be commenced only within two years
  after discovery of the facts that gave rise to the cause of action.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 267
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-267-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 267"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prospectus]{.HTitleText2} {#h-99253 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution

- **[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person including an association shall distribute securities of an
  association except in accordance with the regulations made under
  subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the distribution of securities of an association, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information that is to be disclosed
    by an association before the distribution of any of its securities,
    including the information that is to be included in a prospectus;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of disclosure and the form of
    the information that is to be disclosed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exempting any class of distribution of securities
    from the application of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 268
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-268-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 268"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of exemption

- **[[269]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-269 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application by an association or any person proposing to make a
  distribution, the Superintendent may, by order, exempt that
  distribution from the application of any regulations made under
  subsection 268(2) if the Superintendent is satisfied that the
  association has disclosed or is about to disclose, in compliance with
  the laws of the relevant jurisdiction, information relating to the
  distribution that in form and content substantially complies with the
  requirements of those regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order under subsection (1) may contain any
  conditions or limitations that the Superintendent deems appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 269
- 2005, c. 54, s. 194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-269-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 269"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Trust Indentures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-99279 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[278]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-278 .sectionLabel}** In this section and
sections 279 to 290,

[[event of default]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[event of default]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a
    trust indenture, an event specified in the trust indenture on the
    occurrence of which the principal, interest and other moneys payable
    thereunder become or may be declared to be payable before maturity,
    but the event is not an event of default until all the conditions
    set out in the trust indenture in connection with the giving of
    notice of the event have been satisfied or the period of time for
    giving the notice has elapsed; ([cas de défaut]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an association that has issued,
    is about to issue or is in the process of issuing subordinated
    indebtedness; ([émetteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trustee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person appointed as
    trustee under the terms of a trust indenture to which an association
    is a party, and includes any successor
    trustee; ([fiduciaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any deed, indenture or
    other instrument, including any supplement or amendment thereto,
    made by an association under which the association issues
    subordinated indebtedness and in which a person is appointed as
    trustee for the holders of the subordinated indebtedness issued
    thereunder. ([acte de fiducie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[279]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-279 .sectionLabel}** Sections 280 to 290
apply in respect of a trust indenture if the subordinated indebtedness
issued or to be issued under the trust indenture is part of a
distribution to the public.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

**[[280]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-280 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
in writing, exempt a trust indenture from the application of sections
281 to 290 if, in the Superintendent's opinion, the trust indenture and
the subordinated indebtedness are subject to a law of a province or
other jurisdiction, other than Canada, that is substantially equivalent
to the provisions of this Act relating to trust indentures.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest

- **[[281]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-281 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall be appointed as trustee if at the time of the appointment
  there is a material conflict of interest between the person's role as
  trustee and any other role of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eliminating conflict of interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A trustee shall, within ninety days after the trustee
  becomes aware that a material conflict of interest exists,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} eliminate the conflict of interest; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} resign from office.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity despite conflict

**[[282]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-282 .sectionLabel}** A trust indenture and
any subordinated indebtedness issued thereunder are valid
notwithstanding a material conflict of interest of the trustee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of trustee

**[[283]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-283 .sectionLabel}** If a trustee is
appointed in contravention of subsection 281(1) or if a trustee
contravenes subsection 281(2), any interested person may apply to a
court for an order that the trustee be replaced, and the court may make
an order on such terms as it thinks fit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee qualifications

**[[284]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-284 .sectionLabel}** A trustee, or at least
one of the trustees if more than one is appointed, must be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2) of the
  [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate that is incorporated under the laws
  of a province and authorized to carry on business as a trustee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, ss. 284, 496
- 2007, c. 6, s. 157
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-284-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 284"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of security holders

- **[[285]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-285 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of subordinated indebtedness issued under a trust indenture
  may, on payment to the trustee of a reasonable fee and on delivery of
  a statutory declaration to the trustee, require the trustee to
  provide, within fifteen days after the delivery to the trustee of the
  statutory declaration, a list setting out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of the registered holders
    of the outstanding subordinated indebtedness,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the principal amount of outstanding subordinated
    indebtedness owned by each such holder, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate principal amount of subordinated
    indebtedness outstanding

  as shown on the records maintained by the trustee on the day the
  statutory declaration is delivered to that trustee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of issuer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the demand of a trustee, the issuer of
  subordinated indebtedness shall provide the trustee with the
  information required to enable the trustee to comply with subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where applicant is entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the person requiring the trustee to provide a
  list under subsection (1) is an entity, the statutory declaration
  required under that subsection shall be made by a director or an
  officer of the entity or a person acting in a similar capacity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of statutory declaration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The statutory declaration required under
  subsection (1) must state

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person requiring the
    trustee to provide the list and, if the person is an entity, the
    address for service thereof; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the list will not be used except as permitted
    by subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of list

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use a list obtained under this
  section except in connection with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an effort to influence the voting of the holders of
    subordinated indebtedness;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offer to acquire subordinated indebtedness; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other matter relating to the subordinated
    indebtedness or the affairs of the issuer or guarantor thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with trust indentures

- **[[286]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  issuer or a guarantor of subordinated indebtedness issued or to be
  issued under a trust indenture shall, before undertaking

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issue, certification and delivery of
    subordinated indebtedness under the trust indenture, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the satisfaction and discharge of the trust
    indenture,

  provide the trustee with evidence of compliance with the conditions in
  the trust indenture in respect thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance by issuer or guarantor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the demand of a trustee, the issuer or guarantor
  of subordinated indebtedness issued or to be issued under a trust
  indenture shall provide the trustee with evidence of compliance with
  the conditions in the trust indenture by the issuer or guarantor in
  respect of any act to be done by the trustee at the request of the
  issuer or guarantor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of compliance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following documents constitute evidence of
  compliance for the purposes of subsections (1) and (2):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statutory declaration or certificate made by a
    director or an officer of the issuer or guarantor stating that the
    conditions referred to in subsections (1) and (2) have been complied
    with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an opinion of legal counsel that the conditions of
    the trust indenture requiring review by legal counsel have been
    complied with, if the trust indenture requires compliance with
    conditions that are subject to review by legal counsel; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an opinion or report of the auditors of the issuer
    or guarantor, or such other accountant as the trustee selects, that
    the conditions of the trust indenture have been complied with, if
    the trust indenture requires compliance with conditions that are
    subject to review by auditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further evidence of compliance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The evidence of compliance referred to in
  subsection (3) shall include a statement by the person giving the
  evidence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} declaring that the person has read and understands
    the conditions of the trust indenture referred to in subsections (1)
    and (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} describing the nature and scope of the examination
    or investigation on which the person based the certificate,
    statement or opinion; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} declaring that the person has made such examination
    or investigation as the person believes necessary to enable the
    statements to be made or the opinions contained or expressed therein
    to be given.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee may require evidence

- **[[287]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-287 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the request of a trustee, the issuer or guarantor of subordinated
  indebtedness issued under a trust indenture shall provide the trustee
  with evidence in such form as the trustee requires of compliance with
  any condition thereof relating to any action required or permitted to
