  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition would cause the person to have a
    significant interest in any class of shares of the association; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the person has a significant interest in a
    class of shares of the association, the acquisition would increase
    the significant interest of the person in that class of shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamation, etc., constitutes
  acquisition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the entity that would result from an amalgamation,
  a merger or a reorganization would have a significant interest in a
  class of shares of an association, the entity is deemed to be
  acquiring a significant interest in that class of shares of the
  association through an acquisition for which the approval of the
  Minister is required under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On application by an association, the Superintendent
  may exempt from the application of this section and section 355 any
  class of shares of the association that do not amount to more than 30%
  of the equity of the association.

- Definition of [[equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an association, means
  its equity as determined in accordance with the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 354
- 1993, c. 34, s. 54
- 2001, c. 9, s. 297
- 2007, c. 6, s. 158
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-354-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 354"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No acquisition of control without
approval

- **[[354.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall acquire control,
  within the meaning of paragraph 3(1)(e), of an association without the
  approval of the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamation, etc., constitutes
  acquisition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the entity that would result from an amalgamation,
  a merger or a reorganization would control, within the meaning of
  paragraph 3(1)(e), an association, the entity is deemed to be
  acquiring control, within the meaning of that paragraph, of the
  association through an acquisition for which the approval of the
  Minister is required under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 298
- 2007, c. 6, s. 159
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-354.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 354.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constraining registration

**[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** No association shall,
unless the acquisition of the share has been approved by the Minister,
record in its securities register a transfer or issue of any share of
the association to any person or to any entity controlled by a person if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transfer or issue of the share would cause the
  person to have a significant interest in any class of shares of the
  association; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the person has a significant interest in a
  class of shares of the association, the transfer or issue of the share
  would increase the significant interest of the person in that class of
  shares of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for small holdings

**[[355.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355.1 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding
section 355, where, as a result of a transfer or issue of shares of a
class of shares of an association to a person, the total number of
shares of that class registered in the securities register of the
association in the name of that person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} would not exceed five thousand, and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} would not exceed 0.1 per cent of the outstanding
  shares of that class,

the association is entitled to assume that no person is acquiring or
increasing a significant interest in that class of shares of the
association as a result of that issue or transfer of shares.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 47, s. 53
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where approval not required

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 354(1)
  and (2) and section 355, approval of the Minister is not required
  where a person with a significant interest in a class of shares of an
  association or an entity controlled by a person with a significant
  interest in a class of shares of an association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchases or otherwise acquires shares of that
    class, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that holds any share
    of that class,

  and the number of shares of that class purchased or otherwise
  acquired, or the acquisition of control of the entity, as the case may
  be, would not increase the significant interest of the person in that
  class of shares of the association to a percentage that is greater
  than the percentage referred to in subsection (2) or (3), whichever is
  applicable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percentage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), for the purposes of
  subsection (1), the percentage is 5 percentage points in excess of the
  significant interest of the person in that class of shares of the
  association on the later of the day this Part comes into force and the
  day of the most recent purchase or other acquisition by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any entity controlled by the person, other than the
    entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b),

  of shares of that class of shares of the association, or of control of
  an entity that held shares of that class of shares of the association,
  for which approval was given by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a person has a significant interest in a class
  of shares of an association and the person's percentage of that class
  has decreased after the date of the most recent purchase or other
  acquisition by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any entity controlled by the person, other than the
    entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b),

  of shares of that class of shares of the association, or of control of
  an entity that held shares of that class of shares of the association,
  for which approval was given by the Minister, the percentage for the
  purposes of subsection (1) is the percentage that is the lesser of

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 5 percentage points in excess of the significant
    interest of the person in that class of shares of the association on
    the later of the day this Part comes into force and the day of the
    most recent purchase or other acquisition by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any entity controlled by the person, other than
      the entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b),

    of shares of that class of shares of the association, or of control
    of an entity that held shares of that class of shares of the
    association, for which approval was given by the Minister, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 10 percentage points in excess of the lowest
    significant interest of the person in that class of shares of the
    association at any time after the later of the day this Part comes
    into force and the day of the most recent purchase or other
    acquisition by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any entity controlled by the person, other than
      the entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b),

    of shares of that class of shares of the association, or of control
    of an entity that held shares of that class of shares of the
    association, for which approval was given by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the purchase or
  other acquisition of shares or the acquisition of control referred to
  in that subsection would

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} result in the acquisition of a significant interest
    in a class of shares of the association by an entity controlled by
    the person and the acquisition of that investment is not exempted by
    the regulations; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} result in an increase in a significant interest in
    a class of shares of the association by an entity controlled by the
    person by a percentage that is greater than the percentage referred
    to in subsection (2) or (3), whichever applies, and the increase is
    not exempted by the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exempting from the application of paragraph (4)(a)
    the acquisition of a significant interest in a class of shares of
    the association by an entity controlled by the person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} exempting from the application of paragraph (4)(b)
    an increase in a significant interest in a class of shares of the
    association by an entity controlled by the person by a percentage
    that is greater than the percentage referred to in subsection (2) or
    (3), whichever applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 356
- 1997, c. 15, s. 136
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When approval not required

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections 354(1) and (2)
  and section 355, the approval of the Minister is not required if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Superintendent has, by order, directed the
    association to increase its capital and shares of the association
    are issued and acquired in accordance with the terms and conditions,
    if any, that may be specified in the order; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who controls, within the meaning of
    paragraph 3(1)(d), the association acquires additional shares of the
    association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections 354(1) and (2) and
  section 355, the Minister may approve

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchase or other acquisition of such number or
    percentage of shares of an association as may be required in a
    particular transaction or series of transactions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purchase or other acquisition of up to a
    specified number or percentage of shares of an association within a
    specified period.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 357
- 2001, c. 9, s. 299
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for approval

- **[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for an approval of the Minister required under this Part
  must be filed with the Superintendent and contain the information,
  material and evidence that the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, with respect to any particular transaction, this
  Part applies to more than one person, any one of those persons may
  make the application to the Minister for approval on behalf of all of
  those persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 358
- 2001, c. 9, s. 300
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

**[[358.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358.1 .sectionLabel}** If an application
for an approval under subsection 354(1) is made, the Minister, in
determining whether or not to approve the transaction, shall take into
account all matters that the Minister considers relevant to the
application, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature and sufficiency of the financial resources
  of the applicant or applicants as a source of continuing financial
  support for the association;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the soundness and feasibility of the plans of the
  applicant or applicants for the future conduct and development of the
  business of the association;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business record and experience of the applicant
  or applicants;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the character and integrity of the applicant or
  applicants or, if the applicant or any of the applicants is a body
  corporate, its reputation for being operated in a manner that is
  consistent with the standards of good character and integrity;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the association will be operated responsibly
  by persons with the competence and experience suitable for involvement
  in the operation of a financial institution;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the impact of any integration of the businesses and
  operations of the applicant or applicants with those of the
  association on the conduct of those businesses and operations;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} whether the association is to be operated in
  accordance with cooperative principles; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in Canada
  and, in particular, the cooperative financial system in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 300
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

**[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may impose
any terms and conditions in respect of an approval given under this Part
that the Minister considers necessary to ensure compliance with any
provision of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 359
- 2001, c. 9, s. 300
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certifying receipt of application

- **[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the opinion of the
  Superintendent, an application filed with the Superintendent under
  subsection 358(1) contains all the required information, the
  Superintendent shall forthwith refer the application to the Minister
  and send a receipt to the applicant certifying the date on which the
  completed application was received by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incomplete application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the opinion of the Superintendent, an
  application filed under subsection 358(1) is incomplete, the
  Superintendent shall send a notice to the applicant specifying the
  information required by the Superintendent to complete the
  application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision to applicant

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3)
  and section 362, the Minister shall, within a period of thirty days
  after the certified date referred to in subsection 360(1), send to the
  applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the transaction to which the
    application relates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister is not satisfied that the
    transaction to which the application relates should be approved, a
    notice to that effect, advising the applicant of the right to make
    representations to the Minister in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) and 362(2), if an
  application involves the acquisition of control of an association, the
  Minister shall, within a period of forty-five days after the certified
  date referred to in subsection 360(1), send to the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the transaction to which the
    application relates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister is not satisfied that the
    transaction to which the application relates should be approved, a
    notice to that effect, advising the applicant of the right to make
    representations to the Minister in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period for notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is unable to complete the
  consideration of an application within the period referred to in
  subsection (1), the Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within that period, send a notice to that effect to
    the applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within a further period of thirty days after the
    date of the sending of the notice referred to in paragraph (a) or
    within any other further period that may be agreed on by the
    applicant and the Minister, send a notice referred to in paragraph
    (1)(a) or (b) to the applicant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further extensions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister considers it appropriate to do so,
  the Minister may extend the period referred to in subsection (2) for
  one or more periods of forty-five days.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 361
- 2001, c. 9, s. 301
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable opportunity to make
representations

- **[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, after receipt of the notice
  referred to in paragraph 361(1)(b), the applicant advises the Minister
  that the applicant wishes to make representations, the Minister shall
  provide the applicant with a reasonable opportunity within a period of
  thirty days after the date of the notice, or within such further
  period as may be agreed on by the applicant and the Minister, to make
  representations in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable opportunity to make
  representations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If after receipt of the notice referred to in
  paragraph 361(2)(b) the applicant advises the Minister that the
  applicant wishes to make representations, the Minister must provide
  the applicant with a reasonable opportunity within a period of
  forty-five days after the date of the notice, or within any further
  period that may be agreed on by the applicant and the Minister, to
  make representations in respect of the matter.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 362
- 2001, c. 9, s. 302
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

- **[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within a period of thirty days after
  the expiration of the period for making representations referred to in
  subsection 362(1), the Minister shall, in the light of any such
  representations and having regard to the matters to be taken into
  account, send a notice to the applicant indicating whether or not the
  Minister approves the transaction to which the application relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within a period of forty-five days after the
  expiration of the period for making representations referred to in
  subsection 362(2), the Minister shall, in the light of any such
  representations and having regard to the matters to be taken into
  account, send a notice to the applicant indicating whether or not the
  Minister approves the transaction to which the application relates.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 363
- 2001, c. 9, s. 303
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister does
not send a notice under subsection 361(1) or (3) or 363(1) within the
period provided for in those subsections, the Minister is deemed to have
approved the transaction to which the application relates.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 364
- 2001, c. 9, s. 303
:::

### [DIVISION II]{.HLabel2}[\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 54\]]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100033 .Subheading}

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100034 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of shareholdings

- **[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  with respect to any association, a person contravenes section 354 or
  354.1 or fails to comply with any terms and conditions imposed under
  section 359, the Minister may, if the Minister deems it in the public
  interest to do so, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct that person and any person controlled by
    that person not to exercise any voting rights attached to shares of
    the association beneficially owned by any of those persons as the
    Minister deems appropriate and specifies in the order; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct that person and any person controlled by
    that person to dispose of such number of shares of the association
    beneficially owned by any of those persons as the Minister specifies
    in the order, within such time as the Minister specifies therein and
    in such proportion, if any, as between the person and the persons
    controlled by that person as is specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No direction shall be made under subsection (1)
  unless the Minister has provided each person to whom the direction
  relates and the association concerned with a reasonable opportunity to
  make representations in respect of the subject-matter of the
  direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any person with respect to whom a direction has been
  made under subsection (1) may, within thirty days after the date of
  the direction, appeal the matter in accordance with section 462.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No stay

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A direction made under paragraph (1)(a) is not stayed
  by an appeal under subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 368
- 2001, c. 9, s. 304
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

- **[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a person fails to comply with
  a direction made under subsection 368(1), an application on behalf of
  the Minister may be made to a court for an order to enforce the
  direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A court may, on an application under subsection (1),
  make such order as the circumstances require to give effect to the
  terms of the direction and may, without limiting the generality of the
  foregoing, require the association concerned to sell the shares that
  are the subject-matter of the direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An appeal from an order of a court under this section
  lies in the same manner as, and to the same court to which, an appeal
  may be taken from any other order of the court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest of securities underwriter

**[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to a securities underwriter in respect of shares of a body
corporate or ownership interests in an unincorporated entity that are
acquired by the underwriter in the course of a distribution to the
public of those shares or ownership interests and that are held by the
underwriter for a period of not more than six months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arrangements to effect compliance

- **[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of an association may make such arrangements as they deem
  necessary to carry out the intent of this Part and, in particular, but
  without limiting the generality of the foregoing, may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} require any person in whose name a share of the
    association is held to submit a declaration setting out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the beneficial ownership of the share, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} such other information as the directors deem
      relevant for the purposes of this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require any person who wishes to have a transfer of
    a share registered in the name of, or to have a share issued to,
    that person to submit a declaration referred to in paragraph (a) as
    though the person were the holder of that share; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determine the circumstances in which a declaration
    referred to in paragraph (a) is to be required, the form of the
    declaration and the times at which it is to be submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, direct an
  association to obtain from any person in whose name a share of the
  association is held a declaration setting out the name of every entity
  controlled by that person and containing information concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ownership or beneficial ownership of the share;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} such other related matters as are specified by the
    Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} As soon as possible after receipt by an association
  of a direction under subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association shall comply with the direction;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every person who is requested by the association to
    provide a declaration containing information referred to in
    subsection (1) or (2) shall comply with the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outstanding declaration: effect

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to this section, a declaration is
  required to be submitted by a shareholder or other person in respect
  of the issue or transfer of any share, an association may refuse to
  issue the share or register the transfer unless the required
  declaration is submitted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on information

**[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** An association and any
person who is a director or an officer, employee or agent of the
association may rely on any information contained in a declaration
required by the directors pursuant to section 371 or on any information
otherwise acquired in respect of any matter that might be the subject of
such a declaration, and no action lies against the association or any
such person for anything done or omitted to be done in good faith in
reliance on any such information.

**[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1994, c.
47, s. 55\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption regulations

**[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, by regulation, exempt from any of the provisions of this Part any
share transaction or any class of share transactions involving the
transfer of shares on the death of the beneficial owner thereof, or any
arrangement made in contemplation of the death of the beneficial owner,
to one or more members of the beneficial owner's family, or to one or
more trustees on their behalf.

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Business and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-100080 .Part}

### [General Business]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100081 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main business

- **[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, an association
  shall not engage in or carry on any business other than such business
  as generally appertains to the business of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} providing financial services to one or more of the
    following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the association,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an entity in which an association has a
      substantial investment as allowed by section 390,

    - [(ii.1)]{.lawlabel} another association,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative credit society,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative corporation, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an entity controlled by an entity or group of
      entities described by any of subparagraphs (i) to (iv); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} providing administrative, educational, promotional,
    technical, research and consultative services and related goods to
    any cooperative credit society, or any person intending to organize
    or operate such a society, in support of the financial services
    provided or to be provided by the society.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, an association may act as a
  financial agent for, and provide investment counselling and portfolio
  management services to, any person referred to in paragraph (1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any order that may be made by the
  Superintendent under section 61 or 62, an association shall not
  receive money on deposit from a federal credit union, local
  cooperative credit society or cooperative corporation that is not a
  member of the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 375
- 1997, c. 15, s. 137
- 2001, c. 9, s. 306
- 2014, c. 39, s. 286
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-375-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 375"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional businesses

- **[[375.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to engaging in or
  carrying on any business that an association is permitted to engage in
  or carry on under subsection 375(1), an association may, with the
  approval of the Minister and subject to any order of the
  Superintendent that may be made under section 61 or 62,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide financial services to persons or entities
    that are not persons or entities referred to in any of subparagraphs
    375(1)(a)(i) to (v); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide clearing, settlement and payment services
    to members of the Canadian Payments Association and engage in or
    carry on ancillary services related to those clearing, settlement
    and payment services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may impose any terms and conditions in
  respect of the provision of financial services provided by a retail
  association as the Minister considers necessary or appropriate. The
  Minister may also vary or revoke any of those terms and conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting what an association may or may not do
    with respect to the provision of services and products referred to
    in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of the
    provision of services and products referred to in paragraphs (1)(a)
    and (b).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 307
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional activities

- **[[376]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition, an association may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} hold, manage and otherwise deal with real property;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} act as a custodian of property on behalf of any
    entity referred to in paragraph 375(1)(a) or, if the association is
    a retail association, on behalf of any person to whom the
    association may provide financial services;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} receive money on deposit, on such terms as to
    interest and time and mode of repayment as may be agreed on, from

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the government of Canada, a province or a
      municipality in Canada, or any agency thereof, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a deposit protection agency;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} make loans to and investments in entities that are
    not members of the association;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} make loans to officers and employees of the
    association;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} provide management, investment, administrative,
    advisory, educational, promotional, technical, research and
    consultative services to the entities described in paragraph
    375(1)(a);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} outside Canada, or with the prior written approval
    of the Minister, in Canada, provide the following services to
    entities described in paragraph 375(1)(a), to members of the
    Canadian Payments Association to which that Association may provide
    clearing, settlement and payment services under paragraph
    375.1(1)(b) or, if the association is a retail association, to any
    person:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} collecting, manipulating and transmitting

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} information that is primarily financial or
        economic in nature,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} information that relates to the business of a
        permitted entity, as defined in subsection 386(1), or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} any other information that the Minister may, by
        order, specify,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} providing advisory or other services in the
      design, development or implementation of information management
      systems,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} designing, developing or marketing computer
      software, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} designing, developing, manufacturing or selling,
      as an ancillary activity to any activity referred to in any of
      subparagraphs (i) to (iii) that the association is engaging in,
      computer equipment integral to the provision of information
      services related to the business of financial institutions or to
      the provision of financial services;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} with the prior written approval of the Minister,
    develop, design, hold, manage, manufacture, sell or otherwise deal
    with data transmission systems, information sites, communication
    devices or information platforms or portals that are used

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to provide information that is primarily
      financial or economic in nature,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to provide information that relates to the
      business of a permitted entity, as defined in subsection 386(1),
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for a prescribed purpose or in prescribed
      circumstances; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if it is a retail association,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} act as a financial agent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} provide investment counselling services and
      portfolio management services,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} issue payment, credit or charge cards and, in
      cooperation with others including other financial institutions,
      operate a payment, credit or charge card plan,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} promote merchandise and services to the holders
      of any payment, credit or charge card issued by the association,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} engage in the sale of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} tickets, including lottery tickets, on a
        non-profit public service basis in connection with special,
        temporary and infrequent non-commercial celebrations or projects
        that are of local, municipal, provincial or national interest,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} urban transit tickets, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} tickets in respect of a lottery sponsored by
        the federal government or a provincial or municipal government
        or an agency of any such government or governments, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} act as receiver, liquidator or sequestrator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specialized business management or
  advisory services

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association may engage, under prescribed
  terms and conditions, if any are prescribed, in specialized business
  management or advisory services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as authorized by or under this Act, an
  association shall not deal in goods or engage in any trade or
  business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting what an association may or may not do
    with respect to the provision of services and products referred to
    in paragraphs (1)(g) and (h) and subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of the
    provision of investment counselling and portfolio management
    services and the provision of services and products referred to in
    paragraphs (1)(g) and (h) and subsection (2); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which associations
    may be exempted from the requirement to obtain the approval of the
    Minister before carrying on a particular activity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(g) or (h).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 376
- 1997, c. 15, s. 138
- 2001, c. 9, s. 307
- 2009, c. 2, s. 276(F)
- 2012, c. 5, s. 106
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-376-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 376"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Networking

**[[377]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-377 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 381,
an association may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} act as agent for any entity referred to in paragraph
  375(1)(a), any member of a cooperative credit society or, if the
  association is a retail association, any other person in respect of
  the provision of any service that is provided by a financial
  institution, a permitted entity as defined in subsection 386(1) or a
  prescribed entity and may enter into an arrangement with any person in
  respect of the provision of that service; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} refer any person to any such financial institution or
  entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 377
- 2001, c. 9, s. 308
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on fiduciary activities

**[[378]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378 .sectionLabel}** No association shall
act in Canada as

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an executor, administrator or official guardian or a
  guardian, tutor, curator, judicial adviser or committee of a mentally
  incompetent person; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trustee for a trust.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on deposit taking

- **[[378.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not
  accept deposits in Canada unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is a member institution as defined in section 2
    of the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it has been authorized under subsection 26.03(1) of
    that Act to accept deposits without being a member institution, as
    defined in section 2 of that Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the order approving the commencement and carrying
    on of business by the retail association authorizes it to accept
    deposits solely in accordance with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits that fall below \$150,000

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association referred to in paragraph (1)(b)
  or (c) shall ensure that, on each day that is at least 30 days after
  it receives the authorization referred to in that paragraph,

  ::: {#100174 .Subsection}
  A/B ≤ 0.01

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of all amounts each of which is the sum of all the
      deposits held by it at the end of a day in the preceding 30 days
      each of which deposits is less than \$150,000 and payable in
      Canada; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of all amounts each of which is the sum of all deposits
      held by it at the end of a day in those preceding 30 days and
      payable in Canada.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange rate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2), the rate of
  exchange to be applied on any day in determining the amount in
  Canadian dollars of a deposit in a currency of a country other than
  Canada is to be determined in accordance with rules prescribed under
  subsection 26.03(2) of the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-3).

- Definition of [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2),
  [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning that would be given to
  it by the schedule to the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-3) for the purposes of deposit insurance if that
  schedule were read without reference to subsections 2(2), (5) and (6)
  of that schedule, but does not include prescribed deposits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the deposits referred to in subsection
    (4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing terms and conditions with respect to
    the acceptance of those deposits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 309
- 2007, c. 6, s. 160
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-378.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 378.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice before opening account or
providing prescribed product

- **[[378.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before a retail association referred
  to in paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) opens a deposit account in Canada
  or provides in Canada any prescribed product that relates to a
  deposit, the retail association shall, in the prescribed manner, give
  the person requesting the opening of the account or the provision of
  the product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice in writing that deposits to the deposit
    account, or that the deposit that relates to the prescribed product,
    as the case may be, will not be insured by the Canada Deposit
    Insurance Corporation or, if the request is made by telephone, a
    verbal notice to that effect; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other information that may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association referred to in paragraph
  378.1(1)(b) or (c) shall, in accordance with any regulations that may
  be made,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} post notices at all of its branches, and at
    prescribed points of service, in Canada where deposits are accepted,
    and on all of its websites at which deposits are accepted in Canada,
    to inform the public that deposits with the association are not
    insured by the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include in its advertisements notices to inform the
    public that deposits with the retail association are not insured by
    the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which notices referred to
    in subsection (1) are to be given and the additional information to
    be contained in the notices; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting notices for the purpose of subsection
    (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 160
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits less than \$150,000

- **[[378.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a retail
  association referred to in paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) may not, in
  respect of its business in Canada, act as agent for any person in the
  taking of a deposit that is less than \$150,000 and payable in Canada.

- Meaning of [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
  the meaning assigned to that term by subsection 378.1(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the circumstances in which, and the conditions under which,
  a retail association referred to in subsection (1) may act as agent
  for any person in the taking of a deposit that is less than \$150,000
  and payable in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 160
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shared premises

- **[[378.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, no
  retail association referred to in paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) shall
  carry on business in Canada on premises that are shared with those of
  a member institution, within the meaning of section 2 of the [Canada
  Deposit Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3), that is affiliated
  with the retail association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) only applies in respect of premises or
  any portion of premises on which both the retail association and the
  member institution carry on business with the public and to which the
  public has access.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjacent premises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, no retail association
  referred to in paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) shall carry on business in
  Canada on premises that are adjacent to a branch or office of a member
  institution, within the meaning of section 2 of the [Canada Deposit
  Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3), that is affiliated with the
  retail association, unless the retail association clearly indicates to
  its customers that its business and the premises on which it is
  carried on are separate and distinct from the business and premises of
  the affiliated member institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which, and the
    conditions under which, a retail association referred to in
    paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) may carry on business in Canada on
    premises that are shared with those of a member institution referred
    to in subsection (1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which, and the
    conditions under which, a retail association referred to in
    paragraph 378.1(1)(b) or (c) may carry on business in Canada on
    premises that are adjacent to a branch or office of a member
    institution referred to in subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 160
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on guarantees

- **[[379]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-379 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall not guarantee on behalf of any person the payment or
  repayment of any sum of money unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sum of money is a fixed sum of money with or
    without interest thereon; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person on whose behalf the association has
    undertaken to guarantee the payment or repayment has an unqualified
    obligation to reimburse the association for the full amount of the
    payment or repayment to be guaranteed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply where the person on
  whose behalf the association has undertaken to guarantee a payment or
  repayment is a subsidiary of the association.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2014, c. 39, s. 287\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations imposing
  terms and conditions in respect of guarantees permitted by this
  section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 379
- 1997, c. 15, s. 139
- 2001, c. 9, s. 310
- 2014, c. 39, s. 287
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-379-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 379"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- liquidity support

**[[379.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-379.1 .sectionLabel}** An association
shall not provide any prescribed form of liquidity support for central
cooperative credit societies or local cooperative credit societies
except in accordance with prescribed terms and conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 39, s. 288
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on securities activities

**[[380]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-380 .sectionLabel}** An association shall
not deal in Canada in securities to the extent prohibited or restricted
by such regulations as the Governor in Council may make for the purposes
of this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

- **[[380.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-380.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for an association
  to issue a debt obligation in relation to which the amounts of
  principal and interest owing are guaranteed to be paid from loans or
  other assets held by an entity that is created and organized for the
  principal purpose of holding those loans or other assets and with the
  intention of legally isolating those loans or other assets from the
  association, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the debt obligation is a covered bond as defined in
    section 21.5 of the [National Housing Act](/eng/acts/N-11);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the association is a registered issuer as defined
    in section 21.5 of that Act other than one whose right to issue
    covered bonds has been suspended; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the debt obligation is issued under a registered
    program as defined in section 21.5 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  exempting any type of debt obligation from the application of
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 364
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on insurance business

- **[[381]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-381 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  association shall not undertake the business of insurance except to
  the extent permitted by this Act or the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on acting as agent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall not act in Canada as agent for
  any person in the placing of insurance and shall not lease or provide
  space in its offices in Canada to any person engaged in the placing of
  insurance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the matters referred to in subsection (1) and regulations
  respecting relations between associations and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} entities that undertake the business of insurance;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} insurance agents or insurance brokers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section precludes an association from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring insurance to be placed by a borrower for
    the security of the association; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} obtaining group insurance for its employees or the
    employees of its members or of any bodies corporate in which it has
    a substantial investment pursuant to section 390.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No pressure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No association shall exercise pressure on a borrower
  to place insurance for the security of the association with any
  particular insurance company, but an association may require that an
  insurance company chosen by a borrower meet with its approval, which
  shall not be unreasonably withheld.

- Definition of [[business of insurance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[business of
  insurance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuance of any annuity if the liability in
    respect of the annuity is contingent on the death of a person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issuance of any debt obligation, any of whose
    terms and conditions are established on the basis of mortality
    considerations, under which the issuer is obliged to make periodic
    payments.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 381
- 2012, c. 19, s. 208
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-381-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 381"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on leasing

**[[382]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-382 .sectionLabel}** An association shall
not engage in Canada in any personal property leasing activity in which
a financial leasing entity, within the meaning of subsection 386(1), is
not permitted to engage.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 382
- 2001, c. 9, s. 311
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on residential mortgages

- **[[382.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-382.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not make
  a loan in Canada on the security of residential property in Canada for
  the purpose of purchasing, renovating or improving that property, or
  refinance such a loan, if the amount of the loan, together with the
  amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or prior claim
  against the property, would exceed 80 per cent of the value of the
  property at the time of the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a loan made or guaranteed under the [National
    Housing Act](/eng/acts/N-11) or any other Act of Parliament by or
    under which a different limit on the value of property on the
    security of which the association may make a loan is established;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a loan if repayment of the amount of the loan that
    exceeds the maximum amount set out in subsection (1) is guaranteed
    or insured by a government agency or a private insurer approved by
    the Superintendent;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the association from an entity
    of securities issued or guaranteed by the entity that are secured on
    any residential property, whether in favour of a trustee or
    otherwise, or the making of a loan by the association to the entity
    against the issue of such securities; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a loan secured by a mortgage where

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is taken back by the association on
      a property disposed of by the association, including where the
      disposition is by way of a realization of a security interest, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage secures payment of an amount
      payable to the association for the property.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 311
- 2007, c. 6, s. 161
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-382.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 382.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on charges to borrowers

- **[[382.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-382.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made
  under subsection (2), a retail association that has obtained insurance
  or a guarantee against default on a loan made in Canada on the
  security of residential property shall not charge a borrower an amount
  for the insurance or guarantee that exceeds the actual cost to the
  retail association of the insurance or guarantee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of the actual cost to
    a retail association for the purposes of subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a retail
    association is exempt from the application of subsection (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting, in relation to insurance or a guarantee
    against default on a loan made by a retail association in Canada on
    the security of residential property,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements into which the retail
      association, its representatives and its employees may or may not
      enter, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the payments or benefits that the retail
      association, its representatives and its employees may or may not
      accept from an insurer or the insurer's affiliates; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting any other matters necessary to carry out
    the purposes of subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- disclosure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the disclosure by a retail association of information
  relating to insurance or a guarantee against default on a loan made by
  the retail association in Canada on the security of residential
  property, including regulations respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information that must be disclosed, including
    information relating to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person who benefits from the insurance or
      guarantee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements between the retail association,
      its representatives or its employees and the insurer or the
      insurer's affiliates, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the payments and benefits that the retail
      association, its representatives and its employees accept from an
      insurer or the insurer's affiliates;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which, the form and manner in
    which and the persons to whom information is to be disclosed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances under which a retail association
    is not required to disclose information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 277
- 2012, c. 5, s. 107(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-382.2-20100701.html "Link to previous version of section 382.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policies re security interests

- **[[383]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-383 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of an association shall establish and the association shall
  adhere to policies regarding the creation of security interests in
  property of the association to secure obligations of the association
  and the acquisition by the association of beneficial interests in
  property that is subject to security interests.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to amend policies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, direct an
  association to amend its policies as specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association shall comply with an order made under
  subsection (2) within the time specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 383
- 2001, c. 9, s. 311
- 2007, c. 6, s. 162(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-383-20040101.html "Link to previous version of section 383"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations and guidelines

**[[383.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-383.1 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations and the Superintendent may make guidelines
respecting the creation by an association of security interests in its
property to secure obligations of the association and the acquisition by
the association of beneficial interests in property that is subject to
security interests.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 311
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[383.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-383.2 .sectionLabel}** Sections 383 and
383.1 do not apply in respect of a security interest created by an
association to secure an obligation of the association to the Bank of
Canada or the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 311
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on receivers

**[[384]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-384 .sectionLabel}** An association shall
not grant to a person the right to appoint a receiver or a receiver and
manager of the property or business of the association.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on partnerships

- **[[385]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except with the approval of the
  Superintendent, an association may not be a general partner in a
  limited partnership or a partner in a general partnership.

- Meaning of [[general partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), [[general
  partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any partnership other than a
  limited partnership.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 385
- 2001, c. 9, s. 312
:::

### [Deposit Acceptance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100338 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit acceptance

- **[[385.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association may, without
  the intervention of any other person,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} accept a deposit from any person whether or not the
    person is qualified by law to enter into contracts; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay all or part of the principal of the deposit and
    all or part of the interest on that principal to or to the order of
    that person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply if, before payment,
  the money deposited in the association pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) is
  claimed by some other person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any action or proceeding to which the
    association is a party and in respect of which service of a writ or
    other process originating that action or proceeding has been made on
    the association; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other action or proceeding pursuant to which
    an injunction or order made by the court requiring the association
    not to make payment of that money or to make payment thereof to some
    person other than the depositor has been served on the association.

  If such a claim is made, the money so deposited may be paid to the
  depositor with the consent of the claimant or to the claimant with the
  consent of the depositor.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of trust

- **[[385.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An association is not bound to see
  to the execution of any trust to which any deposit made under the
  authority of this Act is subject.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment when association has notice of
  trust

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies regardless of whether the
  trust is express or arises by the operation of law, and it applies
  even when the association has notice of the trust if it acts on the
  order of or under the authority of the holder or holders of the
  account into which the deposit is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

### [Unclaimed Balances]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100358 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unclaimed balances

- **[[385.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a deposit has been made in Canada that is payable
    in Canada in Canadian currency and in respect of which no
    transaction has taken place and no statement of account has been
    requested or acknowledged by the creditor during a period of ten
    years

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit made for a fixed period,
      from the day on which the fixed period terminated, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other deposit, from the day
      on which the last transaction took place or a statement of account
      was last requested or acknowledged by the creditor, whichever is
      later, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cheque, draft or bill of exchange (including any
    such instrument drawn by one branch of a retail association on
    another branch of the association but not including such an
    instrument issued in payment of a dividend on the capital of an
    association) payable in Canada in Canadian currency has been issued,
    certified or accepted by an association in Canada and no payment has
    been made in respect of the instrument for a period of ten years
    after the date of issue, certification, acceptance or maturity,
    whichever is later,

  the retail association shall pay to the Bank of Canada not later than
  December 31 in each year an amount equal to the principal amount of
  the deposit or instrument, plus interest, if any, calculated in
  accordance with the terms of the deposit or instrument, and payment
  accordingly discharges the association from all liability in respect
  of the deposit or instrument.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall, on making a payment under
  subsection (1), provide the Bank of Canada, for each deposit or
  instrument in respect of which the payment is made, with the following
  information current as of the day the payment is made, in so far as it
  is known to the retail association:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the depositor in whose name the
      deposit is held,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the recorded address of the depositor,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the outstanding amount of the deposit, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the branch of the association at which the last
      transaction took place in respect of the deposit, and the date of
      that last transaction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an instrument,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom or at whose
      request the instrument was issued, certified or accepted;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the recorded address of that person;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the payee of the instrument;

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the amount and date of the instrument;

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name of the place where the instrument was
      payable; and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the branch of the association at which the
      instrument was issued, certified or accepted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of signature cards and signing
  authorities

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall, on written request by
  the Bank of Canada, provide the Bank of Canada with copies of any
  signature cards and signing authorities relating to any deposit or
  instrument in respect of which it has made a payment under subsection
  (1). If it does not have any with respect to a deposit or instrument
  to which the request relates, it shall so inform the Bank of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment to claimant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 22 of the [Bank of Canada
  Act](/eng/acts/B-2), if a payment has been made to the Bank of Canada
  under subsection (1) in respect of any deposit or instrument, and if
  payment is demanded or the instrument is presented at the Bank of
  Canada by the person who, but for that section, would be entitled to
  receive payment of the deposit or instrument, the Bank of Canada is
  liable to pay, at its agency in the province in which the deposit or
  instrument was payable, an amount equal to the amount so paid to it
  together with interest, if interest was payable under the terms of the
  deposit or instrument,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a period not exceeding ten years from the day
    on which the payment was received by the Bank of Canada until the
    date of payment to the claimant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the rate and computed in the manner that the
    Minister determines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcing liability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The liability of the Bank of Canada under
  subsection (3) may be enforced by action against the Bank of Canada in
  the court in the province in which the deposit or instrument was
  payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection (1)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies only in respect of deposits
  made, and cheques, drafts and bills of exchange issued, certified or
  accepted, after the day that subsection comes into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2007, c. 6, s. 163
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.03-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.03"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of unpaid amount

- **[[385.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall mail to
  each person, insofar as is known to the association, to whom a deposit
  referred to in paragraph 385.03(1)(a) is payable, or to whom or at
  whose request an instrument referred to in paragraph 385.03(1)(b) was
  issued, certified or accepted, at the person's recorded address, a
  notice stating that the deposit or instrument remains unpaid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notice to be sent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be sent during the month of January
  next following the end of the first two-year period, during the month
  of January next following the end of the first five-year period and
  also during the month of January next following the end of the first
  nine-year period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit made for a fixed period,
    after the fixed period has terminated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other deposit in respect of
    which no transaction has taken place and no statement of account has
    been requested or acknowledged by the creditor; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cheque, draft or bill of exchange,
    in respect of which the instrument has remained unpaid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of transfer to the Bank of
  Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice to be sent during the month of January
  next following the end of the first nine-year period determined under
  paragraphs (2)(a) to (c), as the case may be, must also

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed before coming into force, 2008, c.
    20, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include the mailing address and websites where
    information can be obtained on how to claim the unpaid deposit or
    instrument.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2007, c. 6, s. 164
- [2008, c. 20, s. 3](#441558-1337466){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="441558-1337466.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.04-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 385.04"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Accounts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100407 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[385.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.05 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this section and sections 385.1 to 385.13, 385.27
and 385.28.

[[member association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[member association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retail
    association that is a member institution as defined in section 2 of
    the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-3). ([association membre]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[personal deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a deposit
    account in the name of one or more natural persons that is kept by
    that person or those persons for a purpose other than that of
    carrying on business. ([compte de dépôt personnel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[retail deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a personal
    deposit account that is opened with a deposit of less than \$150,000
    or any greater amount that may be prescribed. ([compte de dépôt de
    détail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Account charges

**[[385.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.06 .sectionLabel}** A retail
association shall not, directly or indirectly, charge or receive any sum
for the keeping of an account unless the charge is made by express
agreement between the association and a customer or by order of a court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure on opening account

- **[[385.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.07
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not open
  or maintain an interest-bearing deposit account in Canada in the name
  of any natural person unless the association discloses, in accordance
  with the regulations, to the person who requests the association to
  open the account, the rate of interest applicable to the account and
  how the amount of interest to be paid is to be calculated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an
  interest-bearing deposit account that is opened with a deposit in
  excess of \$150,000 or any greater amount that may be prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in advertisements

**[[385.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.08 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
authorize the publication, issue or appearance of any advertisement in
Canada that indicates the rate of interest offered by a retail
association on an interest-bearing deposit or a debt obligation unless
the advertisement discloses, in accordance with the regulations, how the
amount of interest is to be calculated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure regulations

**[[385.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.09 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
  in which disclosure is to be made by a retail association of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} interest rates applicable to debts of the
    association and deposits with the association, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the amount of interest paid is
    to be calculated;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
  in which any charges for the keeping of an account are to be disclosed
  by a retail association to its customers; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other matters or things that may be necessary to
  carry out the requirements of sections 385.06 to 385.08.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 108
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.09-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.09"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure required on opening a deposit
account

- **[[385.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4), a
  retail association shall not open a deposit account in the name of a
  customer unless, at or before the time the account is opened, the
  retail association provides in writing to the individual who requests
  the opening of the account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the account agreement with the
    association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information about all charges applicable to the
    account;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information about how the customer will be notified
    of any increase in those charges and of any new charges applicable
    to the account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} information about the association's procedures
    relating to complaints about the application of any charge
    applicable to the account; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other information that may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a deposit account is not a personal deposit
  account and the amount of a charge applicable to the account cannot be
  established at or before the time the account is opened, the retail
  association shall, as soon as is practicable after the amount is
  established, provide the customer in whose name the account is kept
  with a notice in writing of the amount of the charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association has a deposit account in the
  name of a customer and the customer by telephone requests the opening
  of another deposit account in the name of the customer and the retail
  association has not complied with subsection (1) in respect of the
  opening of that other account, the retail association shall not open
  the account unless it provides the customer orally with any
  information prescribed at or before the time the account is opened.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in writing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association opens an account under
  subsection (3), it shall, not later than seven business days after the
  account is opened, provide to the customer in writing the agreement
  and information referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to close account

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A customer may, within 14 business days after a
  deposit account is opened under subsection (3), close the account
  without charge and in such case is entitled to a refund of any charges
  related to the operation of the account, other than interest charges,
  incurred while the account was open.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), the Governor in
  Council may make regulations prescribing circumstances in which, and
  the time when, the agreement and information will be deemed to have
  been provided to the customer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of charges

**[[385.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.11 .sectionLabel}** A retail
association shall disclose to its customers and to the public, at the
prescribed time and place and in the prescribed form and manner, the
charges applicable to deposit accounts with the association and the
usual amount, if any, charged by the association for services normally
provided by the association to its customers and to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 109
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.11-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No increase or new charges without
disclosure

- **[[385.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not
  increase any charge applicable to a personal deposit account with the
  association or introduce any new charge applicable to a personal
  deposit account with the association unless the association discloses
  the charge at the prescribed time and place and in the prescribed form
  and manner to the customer in whose name the account is kept.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} With respect to prescribed services in relation to
  deposit accounts, other than personal deposit accounts, a retail
  association shall not increase any charge for any such service in
  relation to a deposit account with the association or introduce any
  new charge for any such service in relation to a deposit account with
  the association unless the association discloses the charge at the
  prescribed time and place and in the prescribed form and manner to the
  customer in whose name the account is kept.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 109
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.12-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[385.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.13 .sectionLabel}** Sections 385.1 to
385.12 apply only in respect of charges applicable to deposit accounts
with the retail association in Canada and services provided by it in
Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

### [Registered Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100474 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure required concerning registered
products

- **[[385.131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.131
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a retail
  association shall not open an account that is or forms part of a
  registered product in the name of a customer, or enter into an
  agreement with a customer for a prescribed product or service that is
  or forms part of a registered product, unless the retail association
  provides, in the prescribed manner, to the individual requesting the
  account or the prescribed product or service

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information about all charges applicable to the
    registered product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information about how the customer will be notified
    of any increase in those charges and of any new charges applicable
    to the registered product;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information about the retail association's
    procedures relating to complaints about the application of any
    charge applicable to the registered product; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other information that may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  specifying the circumstances under which a retail association need not
  provide the information.

- Definition of [[registered product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[registered
  product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a product that is defined to be a
  registered product by the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 165
:::

### [Borrowing Costs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100488 .Subheading}

Definition of [[cost of borrowing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[385.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.14 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of this section and sections 385.15 to 385.24, [[cost of
borrowing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a loan made by a
retail association,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the interest or discount applicable to the loan;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any amount charged in connection with the loan that
  is payable by the borrower to the association; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charge prescribed to be included in the cost of
  borrowing.
