For those purposes, however, "cost of borrowing" does not include any
charge prescribed to be excluded from the cost of borrowing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate of borrowing costs

- **[[385.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association makes a loan
  in respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 385.16
  apply and the loan is not secured by a mortgage on real property and
  is required to be repaid either on a fixed future date or by
  instalments, the association shall, if there is a prepayment of the
  loan, rebate to the borrower a portion of the charges included in the
  cost of borrowing in respect of the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charges to be rebated do not include the interest
  or discount applicable to the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  governing the rebate of charges under subsection (1). The rebate shall
  be made in accordance with those regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosing borrowing costs

- **[[385.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not make
  a loan to a natural person that is repayable in Canada unless the cost
  of borrowing, as calculated and expressed in accordance with section
  385.17, and other prescribed information have been disclosed by the
  association to the borrower at the prescribed time and place and in
  the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a loan
  that is of a prescribed class of loans.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 110
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.16-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculating borrowing costs

**[[385.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.17 .sectionLabel}** The cost of
borrowing shall be calculated, in the prescribed manner, on the basis
that all obligations of the borrower are duly fulfilled and shall be
expressed as a rate per annum and, in prescribed circumstances, as an
amount in dollars and cents.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional disclosure

- **[[385.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association makes a loan
  in respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 385.16 are
  applicable and the loan is required to be repaid either on a fixed
  future date or by instalments, the association shall disclose to the
  borrower, in accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the borrower has the right to repay the
    amount borrowed before the maturity of the loan and, if applicable,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any terms and conditions relating to that right,
      including the particulars of the circumstances in which the
      borrower may exercise that right, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whether, in the event that the borrower
      exercises the right, any portion of the cost of borrowing is to be
      rebated, the manner in which any such rebate is to be calculated
      or, if a charge or penalty will be imposed on the borrower, the
      manner in which the charge or penalty is to be calculated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the event that an amount borrowed is not repaid
    at maturity or, if applicable, an instalment is not paid on the day
    the instalment is due to be paid, particulars of the charges or
    penalties to be paid by the borrower because of the failure to repay
    or pay in accordance with the contract governing the loan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing or the loan agreement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} particulars of any other rights and obligations of
    the borrower; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in credit card applications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall, in accordance with the
  regulations, at the prescribed time and place and in the prescribed
  form and manner, provide prescribed information in any application
  forms or related documents that it prepares for the issuance of
  credit, payment or charge cards and provide prescribed information to
  any person applying to it for a credit, payment or charge card.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure re credit cards

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association issues or has issued a
  credit, payment or charge card to a natural person, the association
  shall, in addition to disclosing the costs of borrowing in respect of
  any loan obtained through the use of the card, disclose to the person,
  in accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any charges or penalties described in paragraph
    (1)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the person's rights and obligations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charges for which the person becomes
    responsible by accepting or using the card;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing or the loan agreement; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional disclosure re other loans

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association enters into or has entered
  into an arrangement, including a line of credit, for the making of a
  loan in respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 385.16
  apply and the loan is not a loan in respect of which subsection (1)
  or (3) applies, the association shall, in addition to disclosing the
  costs of borrowing, disclose to the person to whom the loan is made,
  in accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any charges or penalties described in paragraph
    (1)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the person's rights and obligations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charges for which the person is responsible
    under the arrangement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing or the loan agreement; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 111
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.18-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal statement

**[[385.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.19 .sectionLabel}** If a retail
association makes a loan in respect of which the disclosure requirements
of section 385.16 apply and the loan is secured by a mortgage on real
property, the association shall disclose to the borrower, at the
prescribed time and place and in the prescribed form and manner, any
information that is prescribed respecting the renewal of the loan.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 112
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.19-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in advertising

**[[385.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
authorize the publication, issue or appearance of any advertisement in
Canada relating to arrangements referred to in subsection 385.18(4),
loans, credit cards, payment cards or charge cards, offered to natural
persons by a retail association, and purporting to disclose prescribed
information about the cost of borrowing or about any other matter unless
the advertisement discloses prescribed information at the prescribed
time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 112
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re borrowing costs

**[[385.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.21 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time and place at which, and the form
  and manner in which, a retail association is to disclose to a borrower

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cost of borrowing,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any rebate of the cost of borrowing, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other information relating to a loan,
    arrangement, credit card, payment card or charge card referred to in
    section 385.18;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the contents of any statement disclosing
  the cost of borrowing and other information required to be disclosed
  by a retail association to a borrower;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of calculating the cost of
  borrowing;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances under which the cost of
  borrowing is to be expressed as an amount in dollars and cents;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} specifying any class of loans that are not to be
  subject to section 385.15, subsection 385.16(1) or 385.18(1) or (4) or
  section 385.19 or 385.2 or the regulations or any specified provisions
  of the regulations;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time and place at which, and the form
  and manner in which, any rights, obligations, charges or penalties
  referred to in sections 385.15 to 385.2 are to be disclosed;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the imposition of any charge or penalty
  referred to in section 385.18 or providing that the charge or penalty,
  if imposed, will not exceed a prescribed amount;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the nature or amount of any charge or
  penalty referred to in paragraph 385.18(1)(b), (3)(a) or (4)(a) and
  the costs of the retail association that may be included or excluded
  in the determination of the charge or penalty;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting the method of calculating the amount of
  rebate of the cost of borrowing, or the portion of the cost of
  borrowing referred to in subparagraph 385.18(1)(a)(ii);

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} respecting advertisements made by a retail
  association regarding arrangements referred to in subsection
  385.18(4), loans, credit cards, payment cards or charge cards;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} respecting the renewal of loans; and

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting such other matters or things as are
  necessary to carry out the purposes of sections 385.15 to 385.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 113
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.21-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.21"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Complaints]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100572 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedures for dealing with complaints

- **[[385.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establish procedures for dealing with complaints
    made by persons having requested or received products or services in
    Canada from the retail association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} designate an officer or employee of the association
    to be responsible for implementing those procedures; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} designate one or more officers or employees of the
    association to receive and deal with those complaints.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedures to be filed with
  Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall file with the Commissioner
  a copy of its procedures established under paragraph (1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}How procedures to be made available

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall make its procedures
  established under paragraph (1)(a) available

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the form of a brochure, at its branches where
    products or services are offered in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on its websites through which products or services
    are offered in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in written format to be sent to any person who
    requests them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on contacting Agency

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall also make prescribed
  information on how to contact the Agency available whenever it makes
  its procedures established under paragraph (1)(a) available under
  subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2007, c. 6, s. 166
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.22-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to be member

**[[385.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.23 .sectionLabel}** In any province,
if there is no law of the province that makes a retail association
subject to the jurisdiction of an organization that deals with
complaints made by persons having requested or received products or
services in the province from a retail association, the retail
association shall be a member of an organization that is not controlled
by it and that deals with those complaints that have not been resolved
to the satisfaction of the persons under procedures established by
retail associations under paragraph 385.22(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on contacting Agency

- **[[385.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall, in
  accordance with the regulations, at the prescribed time and place and
  in the prescribed form and manner, provide a person requesting or
  receiving a product or service from it with prescribed information on
  how to contact the Agency if the person has a complaint about a
  deposit account, an arrangement referred to in subsection 385.18(4), a
  payment, credit or charge card, the disclosure of or manner of
  calculating the cost of borrowing in respect of a loan or about any
  other obligation of the retail association under a consumer provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall prepare a report, to be
  included in the report referred to in section 34 of the [Financial
  Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1), respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} procedures for dealing with complaints established
    by retail associations pursuant to paragraph 385.22(1)(a); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number and nature of complaints that have been
    brought to the attention of the Agency by persons who have requested
    or received a product or service from a retail association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2012, c. 5, s. 114
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.24-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.24"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Miscellaneous]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100603 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charges for prescribed products or
services

**[[385.241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.241 .sectionLabel}** A retail
association shall not, directly or indirectly, charge or receive any sum
for the provision of any prescribed products or services unless the
charge is made by express agreement between it and a customer or by
order of a court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 167
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepayment protected

- **[[385.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not make
  a loan to a natural person that is repayable in Canada, the terms of
  which prohibit prepayment of the money advanced or any instalment
  thereon before its due date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum balance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except by express agreement between the retail
  association and the borrower, the making in Canada of a loan or
  advance by a retail association to a borrower shall not be subject to
  a condition that the borrower maintain a minimum credit balance with
  the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection (1)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a loan
  that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} secured by a mortgage on real property; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} made for business purposes and the principal amount
    of which is more than \$100,000 or such other amount as may be
    prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government cheques

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A retail association shall not make a charge

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for cashing a cheque or other instrument drawn on
    the Receiver General or on the Receiver General's account in the
    Bank of Canada, in an association or in any other deposit-taking
    Canadian financial institution incorporated by or under an Act of
    Parliament;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for cashing any other instrument issued as
    authority for the payment of money out of the Consolidated Revenue
    Fund; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any cheque or other instrument that
    is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} drawn in favour of the Receiver General, the
      Government of Canada or any department thereof or any public
      officer acting in the capacity of a public officer, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} tendered for deposit to the credit of the
      Receiver General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits of Government of Canada

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (4) precludes any arrangement
  between the Government of Canada and a retail association concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} compensation for services performed by the
    association for the Government of Canada; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest to be paid on any or all deposits of the
    Government of Canada with the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting the holding of
funds

**[[385.251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.251 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting the maximum period during which
a retail association may hold funds in respect of specified classes of
cheques or other instruments that are deposited into an account at a
branch or prescribed point of service in Canada before permitting the
customer in whose name the account is kept to access the funds.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 168
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- activities

**[[385.252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.252 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting any matters involving a retail
association's dealings, or its employees' or representatives' dealings,
with customers or the public, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} what a retail association may or may not do in
  carrying out any of the activities in which it is permitted to engage,
  or in providing any of the services that it may provide, under
  paragraph 375.1(1)(a) and subparagraphs 376(1)(i)(i) to (iii) and any
  ancillary, related or incidental activities or services; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
  in which any of those activities are to be carried out or any of those
  services are to be provided.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 278
- 2012, c. 5, s. 115
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.252-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 385.252"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re customer information

**[[385.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.26 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring a retail association to establish
  procedures regarding the collection, retention, use and disclosure of
  any information about its customers or any class of customers;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} requiring a retail association to establish
  procedures for dealing with complaints made by a customer about the
  collection, retention, use or disclosure of information about the
  customer;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the disclosure by a retail association of
  information relating to the procedures referred to in paragraphs (a)
  and (b);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} requiring a retail association to designate the
  officers and employees of the association who are responsible for

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} implementing the procedures referred to in
    paragraph (b), and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} receiving and dealing with complaints made by a
    customer of the association about the collection, retention, use or
    disclosure of information about the customer;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} requiring a retail association to report information
  relating to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complaints made by customers of the association
    about the collection, retention, use or disclosure of information,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the actions taken by the association to deal with
    the complaints; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} defining "information", "collection" and "retention"
  for the purposes of paragraphs (a) to (e) and the regulations made
  under those paragraphs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of branch closure

- **[[385.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations made under
  subsection (5), a member association with a branch in Canada at which
  it, through a natural person, opens retail deposit accounts and
  disburses cash to customers, shall give notice in accordance with
  those regulations before closing that branch or having it cease to
  carry on either of those activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-closure meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After notice is given but before the branch is closed
  or ceases to carry on the activities, the Commissioner shall, in
  prescribed situations, require the member association to convene and
  hold a meeting between representatives of the member association,
  representatives of the Agency and interested parties in the vicinity
  of the branch in order to exchange views about the closing or
  cessation of activities, including, but not limited to, alternative
  service delivery by the member association and measures to help the
  branch's customers adjust to the closing or cessation of activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting details

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may establish rules for convening a
  meeting referred to in subsection (2) and for its conduct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to rules established under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
    in which notice shall be given under subsection (1), the persons to
    whom it shall be given and the information to be included, the time,
    place, form and manner being permitted to vary according to
    circumstances specified in the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a member association is not
    required to give notice under subsection (1), circumstances in which
    the Commissioner may exempt a member association from the
    requirement to give notice under that subsection, and circumstances
    in which the Commissioner may vary the time and place at which and
    the form and manner in which notice is required to be given under
    any regulation made under paragraph (a); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a meeting may be convened
    under subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2007, c. 6, s. 169
- 2012, c. 5, s. 116
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.27-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 385.27"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re disclosure

**[[385.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.28 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, subject to any other provisions of this Act relating to the
disclosure of information, make regulations respecting the disclosure of
information by retail associations or any prescribed class of retail
associations, including regulations respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information that must be disclosed, including
  information relating to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any product or service or prescribed class of
    products or services offered by them,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any of their policies, procedures or practices
    relating to the offer by them of any product or service or
    prescribed class of products or services,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} anything they are required to do or to refrain
    from doing under a consumer provision, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any other matter that may affect their dealings,
    or their employees' or representatives' dealings, with customers or
    the public;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which, the form and manner in
  which and the persons to whom information is to be disclosed; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the content and form of any advertisement, by retail
  associations or any prescribed class of retail associations, relating
  to any matter referred to in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2007, c. 6, s. 170
- 2012, c. 5, s. 117
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.28-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 385.28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) security

**[[385.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.29 .sectionLabel}** A bank that is
continued as an association under this Act that, immediately before that
continuance, held any outstanding security pursuant to section 426 or
427 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) may continue to hold the
security for the life of the loan to which the security relates and all
the provisions of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) relating to the
security and its enforcement continue to apply to the association as
though it were a bank.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission in case of death

- **[[385.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the transmission of a debt owing
  by a retail association by reason of a deposit, of property held by a
  retail association as security or for safe-keeping or of rights with
  respect to a safety deposit box and property deposited therein takes
  place because of the death of a person, the delivery to the
  association of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an affidavit or declaration in writing in a form
    satisfactory to the association signed by or on behalf of a person
    claiming by virtue of the transmission stating the nature and effect
    of the transmission, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the following documents, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} when the claim is based on a will or other
      testamentary instrument or on a grant of probate thereof or on
      such a grant and letters testamentary or other document of like
      import or on a grant of letters of administration or other
      document of like import, purporting to be issued by any court of
      authority in Canada or elsewhere, an authenticated copy or
      certificate thereof under the seal of the court or authority
      without proof of the authenticity of the seal or other proof, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} when the claim is based on a notarial will, an
      authenticated copy thereof,

  is sufficient justification and authority for giving effect to the
  transmission in accordance with the claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission in case of death

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) shall be construed to
  prevent a retail association from refusing to give effect to a
  transmission until there has been delivered to the association such
  documentary or other evidence of or in connection with the
  transmission as it may deem requisite.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Branch of account with respect to
deposits

- **[[385.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the
  branch of account with respect to a deposit account is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the branch the address or name of which appears on
    the specimen signature card or other signing authority signed by a
    depositor with respect to the deposit account or that is designated
    by an agreement between the retail association and the depositor at
    the time of opening of the deposit account; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no branch has been identified or agreed on as
    provided in paragraph (a), the branch that is designated as the
    branch of account with respect thereto by the retail association by
    notice in writing to the depositor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where debt payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of any debt owing by a retail association
  by reason of a deposit in a deposit account in the association is
  payable to the person entitled thereto only at the branch of account
  and the person entitled thereto is not entitled to demand payment or
  to be paid at any other branch of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where debt payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), a retail association may
  permit, either occasionally or as a regular practice, the person to
  whom the association is indebted by reason of a deposit in a deposit
  account in the association to withdraw moneys owing by reason of that
  deposit at a branch of the association other than the branch of
  account or to draw cheques or other orders for the payment of such
  moneys at a branch other than the branch of account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Situs of indebtedness

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The indebtedness of a retail association by reason of
  a deposit in a deposit account in the association shall be deemed for
  all purposes to be situated at the place where the branch of account
  is situated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of writ, etc.

- **[[385.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4),
  the following documents are binding on property belonging to a person
  and in the possession of an association, or on money owing to a person
  by reason of a deposit account in an association, only if the document
  or a notice of it is served at the branch of the association that has
  possession of the property or that is the branch of account in respect
  of the deposit account, as the case may be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ or process originating a legal proceeding or
    issued in or pursuant to a legal proceeding;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order or injunction made by a court;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an instrument purporting to assign, perfect or
    otherwise dispose of an interest in the property or the deposit
    account; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an enforcement notice in respect of a support order
    or support provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any notification sent to an association with respect
  to a customer of the association, other than a document referred to in
  subsection (1) or (3), constitutes notice to the association and fixes
  the association with knowledge of its contents only if sent to and
  received at the branch of the association that is the branch of
  account of an account held in the name of that customer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notices: Minister of National Revenue

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), a notice, demand,
  order or other document issued with respect to a customer of an
  association constitutes notice to the association and fixes the
  association with knowledge of its contents and, where applicable, is
  binding on property belonging to the customer and in the possession of
  the association or on money owing to the customer by reason of an
  account in the association, if it is sent to the branch of the
  association referred to in subsection (1) or (2), an office of the
  association referred to in paragraph (3)(a) or any other office agreed
  to by the association and the Minister of National Revenue and it
  relates to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the administration of an Act of Parliament by the
    Minister of National Revenue; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the administration of an Act of the legislature of
    a province or legislation made by an aboriginal government, where
    the Minister or the Minister of National Revenue has entered into a
    tax collection agreement under an Act of Parliament with the
    government of the province or the aboriginal government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of an
  enforcement notice in respect of a support order or support provision
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the enforcement notice, accompanied by a written
    statement containing the information required by the regulations, is
    served at an office of an association designated in accordance with
    the regulations in respect of a province; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the order or provision can be enforced under the
    laws of that province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in respect of an
  enforcement notice in respect of a support order or support provision
  until the second business day following the day of service referred to
  in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the designation by an association, for
    the purpose of subsection (3), of a place in any province for the
    service of enforcement notices in respect of support orders and
    support provisions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which an association
    shall publicize the locations of designated offices of the
    association; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information that must accompany
    enforcement notices in respect of support orders and support
    provisions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[designated office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place designated
      in accordance with regulations made for the purpose of subsection
      (3). ([bureau désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[enforcement notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[enforcement notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      support order or support provision, means a garnishee summons or
      other instrument issued under the laws of a province for the
      enforcement of the support order or support provision. ([avis
      d'exécution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[support order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[support order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an order or judgment
      or interim order or judgment for family financial
      support. ([ordonnance alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[support provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[support provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a provision of an
      agreement relating to the payment of maintenance or family
      financial support. ([disposition alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 313
- 2005, c. 19, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385.32-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 385.32"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[Investments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-100742 .Part}

### [Definitions and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100743 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[386]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-386 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity whose
      activities are limited to investing the funds of the entity so as
      to provide investment diversification and professional investment
      management to the holders of its securities, and whose securities
      are

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fixed in number and distributed to the public
        in an offering under a preliminary prospectus, prospectus,
        short-form prospectus or similar document in accordance with the
        laws of a province or a foreign jurisdiction;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} traded on an exchange or an over-the-counter
        market; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} liquidated on a fixed future termination date,
        the proceeds of which are allocated to the holders of the
        securities on a proportional basis. ([fonds d'investissement à
        capital fixe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any loan made or acquired by an association,
        other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a loan to a natural person in an amount of
          two hundred and fifty thousand dollars or less,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a loan to the Government of Canada, the
          government of a province, a municipality, or to any agency
          thereof, or to the government of a foreign country or any
          political subdivision thereof, or any agency thereof, or to a
          prescribed international agency,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is guaranteed by, or fully
          secured by securities issued by, a government, a municipality
          or an agency referred to in subparagraph (ii),

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is secured by a mortgage on real
          property, if

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on residential property and
            the amount of the loan, together with the amount then
            outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or prior claim
            against the property, does not exceed 80% of the value of
            the property at the time the loan is made or acquired, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on real property other than
            residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, does not exceed 80% of
              the value of the property at the time the loan is made or
              acquired, and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} at the time the loan is made or acquired
              the property provides an annual income sufficient to pay
              all annual expenses related to the property, including the
              payments owing under the mortgage and the mortgages having
              an equal or prior claim against the property,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is secured by a mortgage on real
          property, if

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, exceeds 80% of the value
              of the property at the time the loan is made or acquired,
              and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} repayment of the amount of the loan that
              exceeds 80% of the value of the property is guaranteed or
              insured by a government agency or private insurer approved
              by the Superintendent, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on real property other than
            residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, exceeds 80% of the value
              of the property at the time the loan is made or acquired,

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} repayment of the amount of the loan that
              exceeds 80% of the value of the property is guaranteed or
              insured by a government agency or private insurer approved
              by the Superintendent, and

            - [(III)]{.lawlabel} at the time the loan is made or
              acquired, the property provides an annual income
              sufficient to pay all annual expenses related to the
              property, including the payments owing under the mortgage
              and the mortgages having an equal or prior claim against
              the property,

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a loan that

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} consists of a deposit made by the
            association with a financial institution,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by a deposit with any
            financial institution, including the association,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by debt obligations
            guaranteed by any financial institution other than the
            association, or

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by a guarantee of a
            financial institution other than the association,

        - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a loan to another association under
          prescribed terms and conditions, if any are prescribed, or

        - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} a loan to any prescribed entity under
          prescribed terms and conditions, if any are prescribed;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an investment in debt obligations, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} guaranteed by any financial institution
            other than the association,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} fully secured by deposits with any
            financial institution, including the association, or

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} fully secured by debt obligations that are
            guaranteed by any financial institution other than the
            association,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations issued by the Government of
          Canada, the government of a province, a municipality, or by
          any agency thereof, or by the government of a foreign country
          or any political subdivision thereof, or by any agency
          thereof, or by a prescribed international agency,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are guaranteed by, or
          fully secured by securities issued by, a government, a
          municipality or an agency referred to in subparagraph (ii),

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are widely
          distributed, as that expression is defined by the regulations,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are issued by another
          association under prescribed terms and conditions, if any are
          prescribed,

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations of an entity controlled by
          the association, or

        - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations of a prescribed entity
          that are issued under prescribed terms and conditions, if any
          are prescribed; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an investment in shares of a body corporate or
        ownership interests in an unincorporated entity, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that are widely
          distributed, as that expression is defined by the regulations,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests of an entity
          controlled by the association, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} participating shares. ([prêt
          commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[factoring entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[factoring entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a factoring entity
      as defined in the regulations. ([entité s'occupant
      d'affacturage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[finance entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[finance entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a finance entity as
      defined in the regulations. ([entité s'occupant de
      financement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose activities are limited to the financial
        leasing of personal property and such related activities as are
        prescribed and whose activities conform to such restrictions and
        limitations thereon as are prescribed; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that, in conducting the activities referred to
        in paragraph (a) in Canada, does not

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} direct its customers or potential customers
          to particular dealers in the leased property or the property
          to be leased,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} enter into lease agreements with persons in
          respect of any motor vehicle having a gross vehicle weight, as
          that expression is defined by the regulations, of less than
          twenty-one tonnes, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} enter into lease agreements with natural
          persons in respect of personal household property, as that
          expression is defined by the regulations. ([entité s'occupant
          de crédit-bail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an acceptance, endorsement
      or other guarantee, a deposit, a financial lease, a conditional
      sales contract, a repurchase agreement and any other similar
      arrangement for obtaining funds or credit but does not include
      investments in securities. ([prêt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [emprunt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorized vehicle
      designed to be used primarily on a public highway for the
      transportation of persons or things, but does not include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fire-engine, bus, ambulance or utility truck;
        or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other special purpose motorized vehicle
        that contains significant special features that make it suitable
        for a specific purpose. ([véhicule à moteur]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[mutual fund distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mutual fund distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      entity whose principal activity is acting as a selling agent of
      units, shares or other interests in a mutual fund and acting as a
      collecting agent in the collection of payments for any such
      interests if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proceeds of the sales of any such
        interests, less any sales commissions and service fees, are paid
        to the mutual fund; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the existence of a sales commission and service
        fee in respect of the sale of any such interest is disclosed to
        the purchaser of the interest before the purchase of the
        interest. ([courtier de fonds mutuels]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[mutual fund entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mutual fund entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose activities are limited to the investing
        of the funds of the entity so as to provide investment
        diversification and professional investment management to the
        holders of its securities; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose securities entitle their holders to
        receive, on demand, or within a specified period after demand,
        an amount computed by reference to the value of a proportionate
        interest in the whole or in a part of its net assets, including
        a separate fund or trust account of the entity. ([entité
        s'occupant de fonds mutuels]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[participating share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[participating share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share of a
      body corporate that carries the right to participate in the
      earnings of the body corporate to an unlimited degree and to
      participate in a distribution of the remaining property of the
      body corporate on dissolution. ([action
      participante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permitted entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permitted entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity in which
      an association is permitted to acquire a substantial investment
      under section 390. ([entité admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subsidiary
      that is one of a prescribed class of subsidiaries. ([filiale
      réglementaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[real property brokerage entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[real property brokerage entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      entity that is primarily engaged in

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acting as an agent for vendors, purchasers,
        mortgagors, mortgagees, lessors or lessees of real property; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision of consulting or appraisal
        services in respect of real property. ([courtier
        immobilier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specialized financing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specialized financing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      specialized financing entity as defined in the
      regulations. ([entité s'occupant de financement
      spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members of an association's group

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this Part, a member of an
  association's group is any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in paragraph 390(1)(a) that
    controls the association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of the association or of an entity
    referred to in paragraph 390(1)(a) that controls the association;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity in which the association, or an entity
    referred to in paragraph 390(1)(a) that controls the association,
    has a substantial investment; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed entity in relation to the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holding of a security interest in real
    property, unless the security interest is prescribed under paragraph
    403(a) to be an interest in real property; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holding of a security interest in securities of
    an entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 386
- 1993, c. 34, s. 55(F)
- 1997, c. 15, s. 140
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
- 2007, c. 6, s. 171
- 2008, c. 28, s. 151
:::

### [General Constraints on Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100828 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investment standards

**[[387]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-387 .sectionLabel}** The directors of an
association shall establish and the association shall adhere to
investment and lending policies, standards and procedures that a
reasonable and prudent person would apply in respect of a portfolio of
investments and loans to avoid undue risk of loss and obtain a
reasonable return.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 387
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on control and substantial
investments

- **[[388]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-388
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4),
  no association shall acquire control of, or hold, acquire or increase
  a substantial investment in, any entity other than a permitted entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: indirect investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association may, subject to Part XII, acquire
  control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an
  entity other than a permitted entity by way of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of control of an entity referred to
    in any of paragraphs 390(1)(a) to (h), a specialized financing
    entity or a prescribed entity that controls or has a substantial
    investment in the entity; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of shares or ownership interests in
    the entity by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
      390(1)(a) to (h), a specialized financing entity or a prescribed
      entity that is controlled by the association, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an entity controlled by an entity referred to in
      any of paragraphs 390(1)(a) to (h), a specialized financing entity
      or a prescribed entity that is controlled by the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: temporary investments,
  realizations and loan workouts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association may, subject to Part XII, acquire
  control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an
  entity by way of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a temporary investment permitted by section 393;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of shares of a body corporate or of
    ownership interests in an unincorporated entity permitted by section
    394; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a realization of security permitted by section 395.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: specialized financing
  regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A retail association may, subject to Part XII,
  acquire control of, or hold, acquire or increase a substantial
  investment in, an entity other than a permitted entity if it does so
  in accordance with regulations made under paragraph 389(d) concerning
  specialized financing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: uncontrolled event

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An association is deemed not to contravene
  subsection (1) if the association acquires control of, or acquires or
  increases a substantial investment in, an entity solely as the result
  of an event not within the control of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection (2)

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} No association shall, under subsection (2), acquire
  control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an
  entity referred to in paragraph 390(1)(h).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} If an association holds a substantial investment in
  an entity referred to in paragraph 390(1)(h) that it acquired or
  increased under subsection (2) before the coming into force of
  subsection (5.1), the association may continue to hold that
  substantial investment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other provision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite having acquired control of, or a substantial
  investment in, an entity under a particular provision of this Part, an
  association may continue to control the entity or hold the substantial
  investment in the entity as though it had made the acquisition under
  another provision of this Part so long as the conditions of that other
  provision are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of deemed acquisition

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If an association decides to exercise its right under
  subsection (6), the association is deemed to be acquiring the control
  or the substantial investment under the other provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 388
- 1997, c. 15, s. 141
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
- 2007, c. 6, s. 172
- 2013, c. 40, s. 173
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-388-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 388"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re limits

**[[389]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-389 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of the amount or value
  of loans, investments and interests for the purposes of this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the loans and investments, and the maximum
  aggregate amount of all loans and investments, that may be made or
  acquired by an association and its prescribed subsidiaries to or in a
  person and any persons connected with that person;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} specifying the classes of persons who are connected
  with any person for the purposes of paragraph (b); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} concerning specialized financing for the purposes of
  subsection 388(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 389
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
:::

### [Subsidiaries and Equity Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100869 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted investments

- **[[390]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-390
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6)
  and Part XII, an association may acquire control of, or acquire or
  increase a substantial investment in,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an association;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bank or a bank holding company as that expression
    is defined in section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate to which the [Trust and Loan
    Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an insurance company, a fraternal benefit society
    or an insurance holding company incorporated or formed by or under
    the [Insurance Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trust, loan or insurance corporation incorporated
    or formed by or under an Act of the legislature of a province;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative credit society incorporated or
    formed, and regulated, by or under an Act of the legislature of a
    province;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed by or
    under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province and
    that is primarily engaged in dealing in securities; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed, and
    regulated, otherwise than by or under an Act of Parliament or of the
    legislature of a province and that is primarily engaged outside
    Canada in a business that, if carried on in Canada, would be the
    business of banking, the business of a cooperative credit society,
    the business of insurance, the business of providing fiduciary
    services or the business of dealing in securities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (6) and Part XII, an
  association may acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in, an entity, other than an entity referred to
  in any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (h), whose business is limited to one
  or more of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engaging in any financial service activity or in
    any other activity that a retail association is permitted to engage
    in under subsection 375(2) or section 376 or 377;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquiring or holding shares of, or ownership
    interests in, entities in which an association is permitted under
    this Part to hold or acquire;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} engaging in the provision of any services
    exclusively to any or all of the following, so long as the entity is
    providing those services to the association or any member of the
    association's group:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the association,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any member of the association's group,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any entity that is primarily engaged in the
      business of providing financial services,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any permitted entity in which an entity referred
      to in subparagraph (iii) has a substantial investment, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed person, if it is doing so under
      prescribed terms and conditions, if any are prescribed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} engaging in any activity that a retail association
    is permitted to engage in, other than an activity referred to in
    paragraph (a) or (e), that relates to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the promotion, sale, delivery or distribution of
      a financial product or financial service that is provided by the
      association or any member of the association's group, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a significant portion of the business of the
      entity involves an activity referred to in subparagraph (i), the
      promotion, sale, delivery or distribution of a financial product
      or financial service that is provided by any other entity that is
      primarily engaged in the business of providing financial services;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} engaging in the activities referred to in the
    definition [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mutual fund
    distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mutual fund
    entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[real property brokerage
    entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 386(1); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} engaging in prescribed activities, under prescribed
    terms and conditions, if any are prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An association may not acquire control of, or acquire
  or increase a substantial investment in, an entity whose business
  includes any activity referred to in any of paragraphs (2)(a) to (e)
  if the entity engages in the business of accepting deposit liabilities
  or if the activities of the entity include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} activities that an association is not permitted to
    engage in under any of sections 378, 382 and 382.1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dealing in securities, except as may be permitted
    under paragraph (2)(e) or as may be permitted either to an
    association under paragraph 376(1)(f) or to a retail association
    under subparagraph 376(1)(i)(ii);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} activities that an association is not permitted to
    engage in under section 381 if the entity engages in the activities
    of a finance entity or of any other entity as may be prescribed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} acquiring control of or acquiring or holding a
    substantial investment in another entity unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is controlled by
      the association, the association itself would be permitted under
      this Part to acquire a substantial investment in the other entity,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is not controlled
      by the association, the association itself would be permitted to
      acquire a substantial investment in the other entity under
      subsection (1) or (2) or 388(2), paragraph 388(3)(b) or (c) or
      subsection 388(4); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (3)(a), an association may
  acquire control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment
  in, any entity that acts as a trustee for a trust if the entity has
  been permitted under the laws of a province to act as a trustee for a
  trust and the following conditions are satisfied:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity acts as a trustee only with respect to a
    closed-end fund or mutual fund entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the entity engages in other business, that
    business is limited to engaging in one or more of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the activities of a mutual fund distribution
      entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any activity that an association is permitted to
      engage in under subsection 376(2), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the provision of investment counselling
      services and portfolio management services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (8) and the regulations, an
  association may not acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs (1)(a)
    to (h), unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the association controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(e), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the association is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 396(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity whose business includes one or more of
    the activities referred to in paragraph (2)(a) and that engages, as
    part of its business, in any financial intermediary activity that
    exposes the entity to material market or credit risk, including a
    finance entity, a factoring entity and a financial leasing entity,
    unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the association controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(e), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the association is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 396(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity whose business includes an activity
    referred to in paragraph (2)(b), including a specialized financing
    entity, unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the association controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(e), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the association is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 396(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} subject to prescribed terms and conditions, if
      any are prescribed, the activities of the entity do not include
      the acquisition or holding of control of, or the acquisition or
      holding of shares or other ownership interests in, an entity
      referred to in paragraph (a) or (b) or an entity that is not a
      permitted entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, an association may not,
  without the prior written approval of the Minister,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in any of
    paragraphs (1)(e) to (g) from a person who is not a member of the
    association's group;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(h) or (4)(b), other than an entity whose activities
    are limited to the activities of one or more of the following
    entities, if the control is acquired from an entity referred to in
    any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (d) that is not a member of the
    association's group:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a factoring entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a financial leasing entity;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(h) if

    ::: {#100931 .Paragraph}
    A + B \> C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the value of the entity's consolidated assets, as it would
        have been reported in the entity's annual financial statements
        if those statements had been prepared immediately before the
        acquisition,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the aggregate of the values of the consolidated assets of all
        other entities referred to in paragraph (1)(h) that the
        association has acquired control of within the preceding 12
        months, as the value for each entity would have been reported in
        its annual financial statements if those statements had been
        prepared immediately before the acquisition of control of that
        entity, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is 10% of the value of the association's consolidated assets, as
        shown in the association's last annual statement that was
        prepared before its first acquisition of control of an entity
        referred to in paragraph (1)(h) within the preceding 12 months;
    :::

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity whose business includes one or
    more of the activities referred to in paragraph (2)(d);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity that engages in Canada in an
    activity described in paragraph 376(1)(g);

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity that engages in an activity
    described in paragraph 376(1)(h); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity engaging in an activity
    prescribed for the purposes of paragraph (2)(f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or conditions provided
  for in this Act that are relevant to the granting of an approval, the
  Minister may, in considering whether to grant the approval under
  paragraph (5)(b.1), take into account all matters that he or she
  considers relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the stability of the financial system in Canada;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent's approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7) and the regulations, an
  association may not acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in, an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
  (1)(e) to (g) and (4)(b) and (c), unless the association obtains the
  approval of the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply in respect of a
  particular transaction if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association is acquiring control of an entity,
    other than a specialized financing entity, and the only reason for
    which the association would, but for this subsection, require
    approval for the acquisition is that the entity carries on
    activities referred to in paragraph (2)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the association is acquiring control of an entity
    whose activities are limited to the activities of a factoring entity
    or a financial leasing entity; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has approved the transaction under
    subsection (5) or is deemed to have approved it under subsection
    391(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control not required

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} An association need not control an entity referred to
  in paragraph (1)(h), or an entity that is incorporated or formed
  otherwise than by or under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature
  of a province, if the laws or customary business practices of the
  country under the laws of which the entity was incorporated or formed
  do not permit the association to control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on giving up control in
  fact

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An association that, under subsection (4), controls
  an entity may not, without the prior written approval of the Minister,
  give up control, within the meaning of paragraph 3(1)(e), of the
  entity while it continues to control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Giving up control

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} An association that, under subsection (4), controls
  an entity may, with the prior written approval of the Superintendent,
  give up control of the entity while keeping a substantial investment
  in the entity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association is permitted to do so by
    regulations made under paragraph 396(c); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity meets the conditions referred to in
    subparagraph (4)(c)(iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsections do not apply

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If an association controls, within the meaning of
  paragraph 3(1)(a), (b), (c) or (d), an entity, subsections (5) and (6)
  do not apply in respect of any subsequent increases by the association
  of its substantial investment in the entity so long as the association
  continues to control the entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 390
- 1997, c. 15, s. 142
- 1999, c. 28, s. 116
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
- 2007, c. 6, s. 173
- 2012, c. 5, s. 118
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-390-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 390"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval for indirect investments

- **[[391]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-391 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an association obtains the approval of the Minister under subsection
  390(5) to acquire control of, or to acquire or increase a substantial
  investment in, an entity and, through that acquisition or increase,
  the association indirectly acquires control of, or acquires or
  increases a substantial investment in, another entity that would
  require the approval of the Minister under subsection 390(5) or the
  Superintendent under subsection 390(6) and that indirect acquisition
  or increase is disclosed to the Minister in writing before the
  approval is obtained, the association is deemed to have obtained the
  approval of the Minister or the Superintendent for that indirect
  acquisition or increase.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval for indirect investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an association obtains the approval of the
  Superintendent under subsection 390(6) to acquire control of, or to
  acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an entity and,
  through that acquisition or increase the association indirectly
  acquires control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment
  in, another entity that would require the approval of the
  Superintendent under that subsection and that indirect acquisition or
  increase is disclosed to the Superintendent in writing before the
  approval is obtained, the association is deemed to have obtained the
  approval of the Superintendent for that indirect acquisition or
  increase.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 391
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertakings

- **[[392]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-392 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an association controls a permitted entity, other than an entity
  referred to in any of paragraphs 390(1)(a) to (d), the association
  shall provide the Superintendent with any undertakings that the
  Superintendent may require regarding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the activities of the entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} access to information about the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertakings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an association acquires control of an entity
  referred to in any of paragraphs 390(1)(e) to (g), the association
  shall provide the Superintendent with any undertakings concerning the
  entity that the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements with other jurisdictions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may enter into an agreement with
  the appropriate official or public body responsible for the
  supervision of any entity referred to in any of paragraphs 390(1)(e)
  to (g) in each province or in any other jurisdiction concerning any
  matters referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) or any other matter
  the Superintendent considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Part, an
  association shall not control a permitted entity, other than an entity
  referred to in any of paragraphs 390(1)(a) to (d), unless, in the
  course of the acquisition of control or within a reasonable time after
  the control is acquired, the association obtains from the permitted
  entity an undertaking to provide the Superintendent with reasonable
  access to the records of the permitted entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 392
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
:::

### [Exceptions and Exclusions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-100983 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary investments in entity

- **[[393]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-393
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), an
  association may, by way of a temporary investment, acquire control of,
  or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an entity but,
  within two years, or any other period that may be specified or
  approved by the Superintendent, after acquiring control or after
  acquiring or increasing the substantial investment, as the case may
  be, it shall do all things necessary to ensure that it no longer
  controls the entity or has a substantial investment in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if an association that was in
  existence immediately before June 1, 1992 had an investment in an
  entity on September 27, 1990 that is a substantial investment within
  the meaning of section 12 and the association subsequently increases
  that substantial investment by way of a temporary investment, the
  association shall, within two years, or any other period that is
  specified or approved by the Superintendent, after increasing the
  substantial investment, do all things necessary to ensure that its
  substantial investment in the entity is no greater than it was on
  September 27, 1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, in the case of any particular
  association that makes an application under this subsection, extend
  the period of two years, or the other period specified or approved by
  the Superintendent, that is referred to in subsection (1) or (2) for
  any further period or periods, and on any terms and conditions, that
  the Superintendent considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary investment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an association, by way of temporary investment,
  acquires control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment
  in, an entity for which the approval of the Minister would have been
  required under subsection 390(5) if the association had acquired the
  control, or acquired or increased the substantial investment, under
  section 390, the association must, within 90 days after acquiring
  control or after acquiring or increasing the substantial investment,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply to the Minister for approval to retain
    control of the entity or to continue to hold the substantial
    investment in the entity for a period specified by the Minister or
    for an indeterminate period on any terms and conditions that the
    Minister considers appropriate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} do all things necessary to ensure that, on the
    expiry of the 90 days, it no longer controls the entity or does not
    have a substantial investment in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indeterminate extension

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an association, by way of temporary investment,
  acquires control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment
  in, an entity for which the approval of the Superintendent would have
  been required under subsection 390(6) if the association had acquired
  the control, or acquired or increased the substantial investment,
  under section 390, the Superintendent may, on application, permit the
  association to retain control of the entity or to continue to hold the
  substantial investment in the entity for an indeterminate period, on
  any terms and conditions that the Superintendent considers
  appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 393
- 2001, c. 9, s. 314
- 2007, c. 6, s. 174
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-393-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 393"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loan workouts

- **[[394]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-394
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, if an
