  subsection (3) continues to have effect after the expiration of the
  fifteen day period referred to in that subsection if no
  representations are made to the Superintendent within that period or,
  if representations have been made, the Superintendent notifies the
  association or person that the Superintendent is not satisfied that
  there are sufficient grounds for revoking the direction.

**[[440]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-440 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1996, c.
6, s. 58\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court enforcement

- **[[441]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an association or person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is contravening or has failed to comply with a
    prudential agreement entered into under section 438.1 or a direction
    of the Superintendent issued to the association or person pursuant
    to subsection 439(1) or (3),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is contravening this Act, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has omitted to do any thing under this Act that is
    required to be done by or on the part of the association or person,

  the Superintendent may, in addition to any other action that may be
  taken under this Act, apply to a court for an order requiring the
  association or person to comply with the prudential agreement or the
  direction, cease the contravention or do any thing that is required to
  be done, and on such application the court may so order and make any
  other order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal from a decision of a court under
  subsection (1) lies in the same manner, and to the same court, as an
  appeal from any other order of the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 441
- 2001, c. 9, s. 331
:::

#### [Disqualification and Removal of Directors or Senior Officers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-101582 .Subheading}

Meaning of [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[441.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441.01 .sectionLabel}** In sections 441.1
and 441.2, [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the chief
executive officer, secretary, treasurer, controller of an association or
any other officer reporting directly to the association's board of
directors or chief executive officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 332
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[441.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This section applies only in respect
  of an association

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that has been notified by the Superintendent that
    this section applies to it where the association is subject to
    measures designed to maintain or improve its safety and soundness,
    which measures

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} have been specified by the Superintendent by way
      of conditions or limitations in respect of the order approving the
      commencement and carrying on of the association's business, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are contained in a prudential agreement entered
      into under section 438.1 or an undertaking given by the
      association to the Superintendent; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is the subject of a direction made under
    section 439 or an order made under subsection 409(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be provided

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An association shall provide the Superintendent with
  the name of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each person who has been nominated for election or
    appointment as a member of its board of directors, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each person who has been selected by the
    association for appointment as a senior officer,

  together with such other information about the background, business
  record and experience of the person as the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When information to be provided

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information required by subsection (2) shall be
  provided to the Superintendent at least thirty days prior to the date
  or proposed date of the election or appointment or within such shorter
  period as the Superintendent may allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disqualification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is of the opinion that, on the
  basis of the competence, business record, experience, conduct or
  character of a person, he or she is not suitable to hold that
  position, the Superintendent may, by order, disqualify the person from
  being elected or appointed as a director of an association or from
  being appointed as a senior officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of prejudice

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} In forming an opinion under subsection (4), the
  Superintendent must consider whether the interests of the depositors
  and creditors of the association would likely be prejudiced if the
  person were to take office or continue to hold office, as the case may
  be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations may be made

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must in writing notify the person
  concerned and the association of any action that the Superintendent
  proposes to take under subsection (4) and must afford them an
  opportunity within 15 days after the date of the notice, or within any
  longer period that the Superintendent allows, to make representations
  to the Superintendent in relation to the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where an order has been made under subsection (4)
  disqualifying a person from being elected or appointed to a position,
  the person shall not be, and the association shall not permit the
  person to be, elected or appointed to the position.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 59
- 2001, c. 9, s. 333
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of directors or senior officers

- **[[441.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order,
  remove a person from office as a director or senior officer of an
  association if the Superintendent is of the opinion that the person is
  not suitable to hold that office

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the basis of the competence, business record,
    experience, conduct or character of the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} because the person has contravened or, by action or
    negligence, has contributed to the contravention of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} this Act or the regulations made under it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a direction made under section 439,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an order made under subsection 409(3),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a condition or limitation in respect of the
      order approving the commencement and carrying on of the
      association's business, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a prudential agreement entered into under section
      438.1 or an undertaking given by the association to the
      Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In forming an opinion under subsection (1), the
  Superintendent must consider whether the interests of the depositors
  and creditors of the association have been or are likely to be
  prejudiced by the person's holding office as a director or senior
  officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations may be made

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must in writing notify the person
  concerned and the association of any removal order that the
  Superintendent proposes to make under subsection (1) and must afford
  them an opportunity within 15 days after the date of the notice, or
  within any longer period that the Superintendent allows, to make
  representations to the Superintendent in relation to the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is of the opinion that the
  public interest may be prejudiced by the director or senior officer
  continuing to exercise the powers or carry out the duties and
  functions of that office during the period for making representations,
  the Superintendent may make an order suspending the director or senior
  officer. The suspension may not extend beyond 10 days after the
  expiration of that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of order

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall, without delay, notify the
  director or senior officer, as the case may be, and the association of
  a removal order or suspension order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequences of removal order

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The director or senior officer, as the case may be,
  ceases to hold that office as of the date the removal order is made or
  any later date specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The director or senior officer, as the case may be,
  or the association may, within 30 days after the date of receipt of
  notice of the removal order under subsection (5), or within any longer
  period that the Court allows, appeal the matter to the Federal Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Federal Court

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court, in the case of an appeal, may
  dismiss the appeal or set aside the removal order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order not stayed by appeal

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A removal order is not stayed by an appeal.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 334
:::

#### [Supervisory Intervention]{.HTitleText3} {#h-101639 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may take control

- **[[442]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-442
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, where any of
  the circumstances described in subsection (1.1) exist in respect of an
  association, the Superintendent may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period not exceeding sixteen
    days, of the assets of the association and the assets held under the
    administration of the association; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless the Minister advises the Superintendent that
    the Minister is of the opinion that it is not in the public interest
    to do so,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period exceeding sixteen
      days, of the assets of the association and the assets held under
      the administration of the association,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where control of assets has been taken under
      paragraph (a), continue the control beyond the sixteen days
      referred to in that paragraph, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} take control of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances for taking control

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Control by the Superintendent under subsection (1)
  may be taken in respect of an association where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association has failed to pay its liabilities
    or, in the opinion of the Superintendent, will not be able to pay
    its liabilities as they become due and payable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 9, s. 335\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the assets of the association are not, in the
    opinion of the Superintendent, sufficient to give adequate
    protection to its depositors and creditors;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any asset appearing on the books or records of the
    association or held under the administration of the association is
    not, in the opinion of the Superintendent, satisfactorily accounted
    for;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the regulatory capital of the association has, in
    the opinion of the Superintendent, reached a level or is eroding in
    a manner that may detrimentally affect its depositors and creditors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the association has failed to comply with an order
    of the Superintendent under paragraph 409(3)(a);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a retail association, the
    association's deposit insurance has been terminated by the Canada
    Deposit Insurance Corporation; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the opinion of the Superintendent, any other
    state of affairs exists in respect of the association that may be
    materially prejudicial to the interests of the depositors or
    creditors of the association or to those of the owners of any assets
    under the association's administration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of proposed action

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must notify an association of
  any action proposed to be taken in respect of it under paragraph
  (1)(b) and of its right to make written representations to the
  Superintendent within the time specified in the notice, not exceeding
  10 days after it receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives of Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, pursuant to subsection (1), the Superintendent
  has control of the assets of an association referred to in that
  subsection, the Superintendent may do all things necessary or
  expedient to protect the rights and interests of the depositors and
  creditors of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Superintendent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection (1), the Superintendent
  has control of the assets of an association referred to in that
  subsection,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the association shall not make, acquire or transfer
    any loan or make any purchase, sale or exchange of membership shares
    or securities or any disbursement or transfer of cash of any kind
    without the prior approval of the Superintendent or a representative
    designated by the Superintendent; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no director, officer or employee of the association
    shall have access to any cash or securities held by or under the
    administration of the association unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a representative of the Superintendent
      accompanies the director, officer or employee, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the access is previously authorized by the
      Superintendent or the Superintendent's representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 442
- 1996, c. 6, s. 60
- 2001, c. 9, s. 335
- 2014, c. 39, s. 289
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-442-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 442"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[443 to 445]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443_to_445
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1996, c. 6, s. 61\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of directors and officers
suspended

- **[[446]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-446
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent takes
  control of an association pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(iii), the
  powers, duties, functions, rights and privileges of the directors of
  the association and of the officers of the association responsible for
  its management are suspended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent to manage association

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent takes control of an
  association pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(iii), the
  Superintendent shall manage the business and affairs of the
  association and in so doing the Superintendent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may perform any of the duties and functions that
    the persons referred to in subsection (1) were performing prior to
    the taking of control; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has and may exercise any power, right or privilege
    that any such person had or could have exercised prior to the taking
    of control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons to assist

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent takes control of an
  association pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(iii), the
  Superintendent may appoint one or more persons to assist in the
  management of the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 446
- 1996, c. 6, s. 62
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of control

**[[447]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-447 .sectionLabel}** Control by the
Superintendent under subsection 442(1) of an association or of the
assets of an association and the assets held under the administration of
the association expires on the day on which a notice by the
Superintendent is sent to the directors and officers who conducted the
business and affairs of the association stating that the Superintendent
is of the opinion that the circumstances leading to the taking of
control by the Superintendent have been substantially rectified and that
the association can resume control of its business and affairs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 447
- 1996, c. 6, s. 62
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may request winding-up

**[[447.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-447.1 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
may, at any time before the receipt of a request under section 448 to
relinquish control of an association or of the assets of an association
and the assets held under the administration of the association, request
the Attorney General of Canada to apply for a winding-up order under
section 10.1 of the [Winding-up and Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11)
in respect of the association where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assets of the association and the assets held
  under the administration of the association are under the control of
  the Superintendent pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(i) or (ii); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the association is under the control of the
  Superintendent pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(iii).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 62
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to relinquish control

**[[448]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-448 .sectionLabel}** Where no action has
been taken by the Superintendent under subsection 447.1 and, after
thirty days following the taking of control by the Superintendent under
subsection 442(1) of an association or of the assets of an association
and the assets held under the administration of the association, the
Superintendent receives from the board of directors a notice in writing
requesting the Superintendent to relinquish control, the Superintendent
must, not later than twelve days after receipt of the notice,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} comply with the request; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} request the Attorney General of Canada to apply for a
  winding-up order under section 10.1 of the [Winding-up and
  Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11) in respect of the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 448
- 1996, c. 6, s. 62
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advisory committee

**[[449]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-449 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
from among the associations that are subject to an assessment under
section 23 of the [Office of the Superintendent of Financial
Institutions Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7) and required to share in the expenses
resulting from the taking of control of an association pursuant to
subsection 442(1), appoint a committee of not more than six members to
advise the Superintendent in respect of assets, management and all other
matters pertinent to the duties and responsibilities of the
Superintendent in exercising control of the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 449
- 1996, c. 6, s. 62
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenses payable by associations

- **[[450]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-450
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the Superintendent has taken
  control of an association pursuant to subparagraph 442(1)(b)(iii) and
  the control expires or is relinquished pursuant to section 447 or
  paragraph 448(a), the Superintendent may direct that the association
  be liable for repayment of all or part of the expenses resulting from
  the taking of control of the association and assessed against and paid
  by other associations pursuant to section 23 of the [Office of the
  Superintendent of Financial Institutions Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7),
  together with such interest in respect thereof at such rate as is
  specified by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where any direction is made under subsection (1), the
  amount for which the association is liable is a debt due to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada payable on demand and is recoverable in the
  Federal Court or any other court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 450
- 1996, c. 6, s. 63
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority of claim in liquidation

**[[451]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-451 .sectionLabel}** In the case of the
winding-up of an association, the expenses resulting from the taking of
control of the association under subsection 442(1) and assessed against
and paid by other associations pursuant to section 23 of the [Office of
the Superintendent of Financial Institutions Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7), and
interest in respect thereof at such rate as is specified by the
Superintendent, constitute a claim of Her Majesty in right of Canada
against the assets of the association that ranks after all other claims
but prior to any claim in respect of the membership shares or shares of
the association.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 451
- 1996, c. 6, s. 64(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of assessment

**[[452]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452 .sectionLabel}** Any amount recovered
pursuant to section 450 or 451 shall be applied to reduce the total
amount of expenses incurred for or in connection with the administration
of this Act.

## [PART XIII.1]{.HLabel1}[Regulation of Retail Associations --- Commissioner]{.HTitleText1} {#h-101715 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

**[[452.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.1 .sectionLabel}** A retail
association shall provide the Commissioner with the information at the
times and in the form that the Commissioner may require for the purposes
of the administration of the [Financial Consumer Agency of Canada
Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1) and the consumer provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 336
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidential information

- **[[452.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2),
  information regarding the business or affairs of a retail association
  or regarding persons dealing with one that is obtained by the
  Commissioner or by any person acting under the direction of the
  Commissioner, in the course of the exercise or performance of powers,
  duties and functions referred to in subsection 5(1) of the [Financial
  Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1), and any information
  prepared from that information, is confidential and shall be treated
  accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure permitted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner is satisfied that the information
  will be treated as confidential by the agency, body or person to whom
  it is disclosed, subsection (1) does not prevent the Commissioner from
  disclosing it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to any government agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any other agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation, for
    purposes related to its operation; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to the Deputy Minister of Finance or any officer of
    the Department of Finance authorized in writing by the Deputy
    Minister of Finance or to the Governor of the Bank of Canada or any
    officer of the Bank of Canada authorized in writing by the Governor
    of the Bank of Canada, for the purposes of policy analysis related
    to the regulation of financial institutions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 336
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination

- **[[452.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner, from time to time,
  but at least once in each calendar year, shall make or cause to be
  made any examination and inquiry that the Commissioner considers
  necessary for the purposes of satisfying the Commissioner that the
  applicable consumer provisions are being complied with and, after the
  conclusion of each examination and inquiry, shall report on it to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records of retail association

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner or a person acting under the
  Commissioner's direction in carrying out his or her duties under
  subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a right of access to any records, including
    electronic records, of a retail association; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may require the directors or officers of a retail
    association to provide information and explanations, to the extent
    that they are reasonably able to do so, in respect of any matter
    subject to examination or inquiry under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 336
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of Commissioner on inquiry

**[[452.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.4 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner,
in carrying out his or her duties in relation to consumer provisions,
has all the powers of a person appointed as a commissioner under Part II
of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) for the purpose of obtaining
evidence under oath, and may delegate those powers to any person acting
under the Commissioner's direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 336
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance agreement

**[[452.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.5 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner
may enter into an agreement, called a "compliance agreement", with a
retail association for the purposes of implementing any measure designed
to further compliance by it with the consumer provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 336
:::

## [PART XIV]{.HLabel1}[Administration]{.HTitleText1} {#h-101748 .Part}

### [Notices and Other Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101749 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of documents

**[[452.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452.6 .sectionLabel}** Any by-law, notice,
resolution, requisition, statement or other document required or
permitted to be executed or signed by more than one person for the
purposes of this Act may be executed or signed in several documents of
like form, each of which is executed or signed by one or more of the
persons. The documents if duly executed or signed by all persons
required or permitted to sign them are deemed to constitute one document
for the purposes of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 205
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to directors, members and
shareholders

**[[453]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-453 .sectionLabel}** A notice or document
required by this Act or the regulations or by the incorporating
instrument or by-laws of an association to be sent to a director, member
or shareholder of an association may be sent by prepaid mail addressed
to, or may be delivered personally to,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the director at the director's latest address as
  shown in the records of the association or in the latest return made
  under section 432;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the member at the member's latest address as shown in
  the records of the association; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the shareholder at the shareholder's latest address
  as shown in the records of the association or its transfer agent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption from return

**[[454]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-454 .sectionLabel}** A director named in the
latest return sent by an association to the Superintendent under section
432 is presumed for the purposes of this Act to be a director of the
association referred to in the return.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption of receipt

- **[[455]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-455 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice or document sent by mail in accordance with section 453 to a
  director, member or shareholder is deemed to be received by the
  director, member or shareholder at the time it would be delivered in
  the ordinary course of mail unless there are reasonable grounds for
  believing that the director, member or shareholder did not receive the
  notice or document at that time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undelivered notices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an association sends a notice or document to a
  member or shareholder in accordance with section 453 and it is
  returned on two consecutive occasions because the member or
  shareholder cannot be found, the association is not required to send
  any further notices or documents to them until it is informed in
  writing of their new address.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 455
- 2005, c. 54, s. 206
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-455-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 455"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service on an association

**[[456]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-456 .sectionLabel}** A notice or document
required by this Act to be sent to or served on an association may be
sent by registered mail to the head office of the association and, if so
sent, is deemed to be received or served at the time it would be
delivered in the ordinary course of mail unless there are reasonable
grounds for believing that the association did not receive the notice or
document at that time or at all.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of association

- **[[457]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-457 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate issued on behalf of an association stating any fact that
  is set out in the incorporating instrument, the by-laws, the minutes
  of a meeting, or in a contract to which the association is a party,
  may be signed by a director or an officer of the association.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of certain cases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When introduced as evidence in any civil, criminal or
  administrative action or proceeding,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fact stated in a certificate referred to in
    subsection (1),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract from the members register of an
    association,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract from a securities register of
    an association, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of, or an extract from, minutes of
    a meeting

  is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of the facts so
  certified without proof of the signature or official character of the
  person appearing to have signed the certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry in securities register

- **[[458]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-458 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  entry in the securities register of, or on a security certificate
  issued by, an association is evidence that the person in whose name
  the security is registered is the owner of the securities described in
  the register or in the certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry in members register

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An entry in the members register of an association is
  evidence that the person in whose name a membership share is
  registered is a member of that association and the owner of that
  membership share.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 458
- 2005, c. 54, s. 207(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-458-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 458"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of documents or fact

- **[[459]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent may require that a document or a fact stated in a
  document that is required by or under this Act to be sent to the
  Superintendent or to the Minister be verified in accordance with
  subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document or fact required by this Act or by the
  Superintendent to be verified may be verified by affidavit made under
  oath or by statutory declaration under the [Canada Evidence
  Act](/eng/acts/C-5) before any commissioner for oaths or for taking
  affidavits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative means of publication

- **[[459.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is required by a
  provision of this Act to be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or to be published in any other
  way may, instead of being published in that way, be published in any
  manner that may be prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative means of publishing
  summaries

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is required by a provision of this Act
  to be summarized in a publication may instead be summarized and
  published in any manner that may be prescribed for the purpose of that
  provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any condition under a provision of this Act that
  something be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or in any other way is satisfied
  if that thing is published instead in any manner that may be
  prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other consequences

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a provision of this Act provides for
  consequences to follow the publication of something in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or in any other manner, the same
  consequences follow the publication of that thing in any other manner
  that may be prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 159
:::

### [Approvals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101803 .Subheading}

Definition of [[approval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[459.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.2 .sectionLabel}** In sections 459.3
to 459.8, [[approval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any consent,
designation, order, exemption, extension or other permission granted by
the Minister or the Superintendent under this Act, and includes the
issuance of letters patent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 337
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-459.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 459.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters to take into account --- Minister

- **[[459.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or
  conditions provided for in this Act that are relevant to the granting
  of an approval, the Minister may, in considering whether to grant the
  approval, take into account all matters that he or she considers
  relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters to take into account ---
  Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or conditions provided for
  in this Act that are relevant to the granting of an approval and to
  any prudential considerations that the Superintendent considers
  relevant in the circumstances, the Superintendent may, in considering
  whether to grant the approval, take into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister --- terms, conditions and
undertakings

- **[[459.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that
  may be taken under this Act, the Minister may, in granting an
  approval, impose any terms and conditions or require any undertaking
  that the Minister considers appropriate, including any terms,
  conditions or undertakings specified by the Superintendent to maintain
  or improve the safety and soundness of any financial institution
  regulated under an Act of Parliament to which the approval relates or
  that might be affected by it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner --- supervision of terms,
  conditions and undertakings

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister specifies that the Commissioner is
  to supervise an association to determine if it is complying with any
  terms and conditions that are imposed, or undertakings that are
  required, by the Minister for the protection of the association's
  customers, the Commissioner may take the same measures that the
  Commissioner could take if the terms and conditions or undertaking
  were a consumer provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent --- terms, conditions
  and undertakings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that may be taken
  under this Act, the Superintendent may, in granting an approval,
  impose any terms and conditions or require any undertaking that the
  Superintendent considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
- 2010, c. 12, s. 1859
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-459.4-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 459.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment of
approval --- Minister

- **[[459.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke, suspend or
  amend any approval granted by the Minister if he or she considers it
  appropriate to do so. In deciding whether to take any of those
  actions, the Minister may take into account all matters that he or she
  considers relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment of
  approval --- Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may revoke, suspend or amend any
  approval granted by the Superintendent if he or she considers it
  appropriate to do so. In deciding whether to take any of those
  actions, the Superintendent may take into account any prudential
  considerations that he or she considers relevant in the circumstances
  and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before taking any action under this section, the
  Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, shall give the
  person concerned a reasonable opportunity to make representations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of non-compliance on approval

- **[[459.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise expressly provided
  in this Act, a failure to comply with a term, condition or undertaking
  imposed or required under any provision of this Act does not
  invalidate the approval to which the term, condition or undertaking
  relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that may be taken
  under this Act, in the case of non-compliance by a person with a term,
  condition or undertaking imposed or required under any provision of
  this Act, the Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revoke, suspend or amend the approval to which the
    term, condition or undertaking relates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply to a court for an order directing the person
    to comply with the term, condition or undertaking, and on such an
    application the court may make the order and any other order that it
    thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before taking any action under subsection (2), the
  Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, shall give the
  person concerned a reasonable opportunity to make representations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} At the request of the person concerned, the Minister
  or the Superintendent, as the case may be, may revoke, suspend or
  amend any terms or conditions imposed by him or her and may revoke or
  suspend an undertaking given to him or her or approve its amendment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple approval --- other approvals

**[[459.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.7 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or the
Superintendent may grant more than one approval, other than letters
patent, in a single instrument if he or she considers it appropriate to
do so, and if the Minister or Superintendent does so, he or she may
specify different effective dates for each of the approvals.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption in relation to notices of
intention

**[[459.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.8 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
may, on application, exempt an applicant or applicants from the
provisions of this Act respecting the publication of a notice of
intention in respect of applications for approvals and impose any terms
and conditions respecting the publication of the notice of intention
that he or she considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 182
:::

### [Exceptions to Generally Accepted Accounting Principles]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101862 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculations --- generally accepted
accounting principles

- **[[459.81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.81
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, as a result of a change to the
  accounting principles referred to in subsection 292(4) --- whether the
  change is made before or after this section comes into force --- the
  Superintendent considers, given any prudential considerations that he
  or she considers relevant, that any amount, calculation or valuation
  under this Act or the regulations is not appropriate, the
  Superintendent may specify the amount that is to be used or the
  calculation or valuation that is to be performed instead.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause a notice of the
  specification to be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) within 60 days after the day on
  which the specification has effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Five-year limit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The specification ceases to have effect on the day
  indicated in the notice, which may be no later than five years after
  the day on which the specification is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 5, s. 120
:::

### [Orders to Exempt or Adapt]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101872 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

- **[[459.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the
  Minister, the Governor in Council may, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide that any provision of this Act or the
    regulations shall not apply to an association, to Her Majesty in
    right of Canada or an agent or agency of Her Majesty or to any other
    person otherwise subject to the provision; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide that any provision of this Act or the
    regulations applies to an association, to Her Majesty in right of
    Canada or Her Majesty's agent or agency or to any other person
    subject to the provision only in the manner and to the extent
    provided for in the order, and adapt the provision for the purposes
    of that application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's recommendation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make a recommendation under
  subsection (1) only if the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is of the opinion that the order would relate to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition, holding, sale or other
      disposition of, or other dealing with, shares of an association
      by, or the transfer or issue of shares of an association to, Her
      Majesty in right of Canada or Her Majesty's agent or agency, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the management of the business and affairs or
      the regulation and supervision of an association during the time
      that Her Majesty or Her Majesty's agent or agency is acquiring,
      holding, selling or otherwise disposing of, or otherwise dealing
      with, shares of the association, or during the time that shares of
      the association are transferred or issued to Her Majesty or Her
      Majesty's agent or agency; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is of the opinion --- after considering measures
    other than an order under that subsection and after consulting with
    the Superintendent, the Governor of the Bank of Canada and the Chief
    Executive Officer of the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation ---
    that the order will promote the stability of the financial system in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the Minister, the Governor
  in Council may, by order, impose any terms and conditions relating to
  the acquisition of shares of an association by, or transfer or issue
  of shares of an association to, Her Majesty in right of Canada or Her
  Majesty's agent or agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repeal of order under subsection (1)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may recommend the repeal of an order
  made under subsection (1) without regard to subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms, conditions and undertakings

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} From the time that Her Majesty in right of Canada or
  an agent or agency of Her Majesty acquires shares of an association to
  the time that the shares are sold or otherwise disposed of, the
  Minister may, by order, impose any terms and conditions on --- or
  require any undertaking from --- the association that the Minister
  considers appropriate, including any terms and conditions or
  undertakings relating to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the remuneration of the association's senior
    officers, as defined in section 441.01, and directors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the appointment or removal of the association's
    senior officers, as defined in section 441.01, and directors;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment of dividends by the association; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the association's lending policies and practices.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite Part X of the [Financial Administration
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11), the Minister or an agent or agency of Her
  Majesty in right of Canada may, on any terms and conditions imposed
  under subsection (3), acquire and hold shares of an association on
  behalf of or in trust for Her Majesty if an order is made under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment out of C.R.F.

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On the requisition of the Minister, there may be paid
  out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund the amount that the Minister or
  an agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada is required to
  pay for the acquisition of shares under subsection (6) and any costs
  and expenses incurred in connection with the acquisition, holding,
  sale or other disposition of, or other dealing with, the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of shares

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Shares acquired under subsection (6) by the Minister
  or an agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada shall be
  registered in the name of the Minister, agent or agency, as the case
  may be, in the association's securities register if they are capable
  of being registered in it, and the shares shall be held by the
  Minister, agent or agency, as the case may be, on behalf of or in
  trust for Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition by Minister

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at any time, sell or otherwise
  dispose of shares acquired under subsection (6). The [Surplus Crown
  Assets Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of the [Financial
  Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the sale or
  disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition by agent or agency

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} An agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada
  --- at the request of the Minister, which may be made at any time ---
  shall sell or otherwise dispose of shares acquired under subsection
  (6). The [Surplus Crown Assets Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of
  the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the
  sale or disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration by Minister

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister or an agent or agency of Her Majesty
  in right of Canada is holding shares of an association on behalf of or
  in trust for Her Majesty on the day that is two years after the day on
  which the shares were acquired, the Minister shall consider whether
  holding the shares continues to promote the stability of the financial
  system in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory disposition

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister, under subsection (11), considers
  that holding shares acquired under subsection (6) no longer continues
  to promote the stability of the financial system in Canada, the
  Minister --- or, at the request of the Minister, the agent or agency
  of Her Majesty in right of Canada --- shall take the measures that the
  Minister considers practicable in the circumstances to sell or
  otherwise dispose of the shares. The [Surplus Crown Assets
  Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of the [Financial Administration
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the sale or disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to an order made under this section.

- Definition of [[shares]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[shares]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes membership shares and any
  conversion or exchange privilege, option or right to acquire shares or
  membership shares.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 279
- 2016, c. 7, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-459.9-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 459.9"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Orders and Directives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101914 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

**[[460]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-460 .sectionLabel}** An instrument issued or
made under this Act and directed to a single association or person,
other than an order referred to in section 423, is not a statutory
instrument for the purposes of the [Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

**[[461]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
by order, establish the form of any application to be made to the
Minister or the Superintendent under this Act.

### [Applications to Superintendent]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101919 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of applications

- **[[461.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following applications to the
  Superintendent must contain the information, material and evidence
  that the Superintendent may require:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applications for approval under subsection 74(1),
    80(2), 82(4), 86(5), 87(1), 177(1), 221(2), 385(1), 390(6) or (10),
    393(1) or (2) or 406(1), subparagraph 411(2)(a)(iv), section 414 or
    subsection 418(3) or (3.1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applications for consent under subsection 79(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} applications for exemptions under subsection
    166.05(3); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} applications for extensions of time under
    subsection 393(3) or (5), 394(4) or 395(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receipt

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after receiving the application, the
  Superintendent shall send a receipt to the applicant certifying the
  date on which it was received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision to applicant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the Superintendent shall,
  within a period of thirty days after the receipt of the application,
  send to the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the application, subject to any
    terms and conditions that the Superintendent considers appropriate;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Superintendent is not satisfied that it
    should be approved, a notice to that effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is unable to complete the
  consideration of the application within the period referred to in
  subsection (3), the Superintendent shall, within that period, send a
  notice to the applicant informing the applicant that the
  Superintendent has extended the period for a further period set out in
  the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant does not receive the notice required
  by subsection (3) and, where applicable, subsection (4), within the
  required period, the Superintendent is deemed to have approved the
  application and granted the approval, consent, extension or exemption
  to which the application relates, regardless of whether the approval,
  consent, extension or exemption is to be in writing or not.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 338
- 2007, c. 6, s. 183
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-461.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 461.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Applications for Certain Approvals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101939 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for certain approvals

- **[[461.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An application for the prior written
  approval of the Minister in respect of paragraph 376(1)(g) or (h) or
  390(5)(b.1), (c), (d) or (d.1) must be filed with the Superintendent
  and contain the information, material and evidence that the
  Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification of receipt of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, the
  application contains all the required information, the Superintendent
  must refer it to the Minister, together with his or her analysis in
  relation to the application, and send a receipt to the applicant
  certifying the date on which the application was referred to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incomplete application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, the
  application is incomplete, the Superintendent must send a notice to
  the applicant specifying the information required by the
  Superintendent to complete it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the Minister must, within
  30 days after the certified date referred to in subsection (2), send
  to the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the application; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister is not satisfied that the
    application should be approved, a notice to that effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is unable to complete the
  consideration of an application within the 30-day period, the Minister
  must, within that period, send a notice to the applicant informing the
  applicant that the Minister has extended the period for a further
  period set out in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister does not send the notice referred to
  in subsection (4) or, if applicable, subsection (5), within the
  required period, the Minister is deemed to have approved the
  application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 184
- 2012, c. 5, s. 121
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-461.2-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 461.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Appeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101957 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to Federal Court

- **[[462]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-462 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the Federal Court from any direction of the Minister
  made under subsection 368(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court may, in an appeal under subsection
  (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} dismiss the appeal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set aside the direction or decision; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set aside the direction or decision and refer the
    matter back for re-determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of an appeal under subsection (1),
  the Minister shall, at the request of the association or person making
  the appeal, provide the association or person with a certificate in
  writing setting out the direction or decision appealed from and the
  reasons why the direction or decision was made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, ss. 462, 498
- 1996, c. 6, s. 65
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101970 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to make regulations

- **[[463]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-463 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is required or authorized
    by this Act to be prescribed;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the way in which anything that is
    required or authorized by this Act to be prescribed shall be
    determined;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining words and expressions to be defined for
    the purposes of this Act;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} specifying what constitutes predominance for the
    purposes of paragraph 27(g.1) and subsection 50(1.1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring the payment of a fee in respect of the
    filing, examining or issuing of any document or in respect of any
    action that the Superintendent is required or authorized to take
    under this Act, and fixing the amount thereof or the manner of
    determining the amount thereof;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the regulatory capital and total assets
    of an association;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the retention, in Canada, of assets of
    an association;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the value of assets of an association to
    be held in Canada and the manner in which those assets are to be
    held;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the protection and maintenance of assets
    of an association and assets held under the administration of an
    association, including regulations respecting the bonding of
    directors, officers and employees of an association;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the holding of membership shares and
    shares for the purposes of section 78;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting information, in addition to the
    information required by section 434, to be maintained in the
    register referred to in that section;

  - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of the equity of an
    association;

  - [(i.2)]{.lawlabel} respecting persons who are a related party to a
    retail association; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may incorporate any material by
  reference regardless of its source and either as it exists on a
  particular date or as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporated material is not a
  regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Material does not become a regulation for the
  purposes of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) because it
  is incorporated by reference.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 463
- 1997, c. 15, s. 160
- 1999, c. 31, s. 58(F)
- 2001, c. 9, s. 339
- 2005, c. 54, s. 208
- 2014, c. 39, s. 290
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-463-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 463"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Delegation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-101994 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[464]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-464 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
delegate any of the Minister's powers, duties and functions under this
Act to any Minister of State appointed pursuant to the [Ministries and
Ministers of State Act](/eng/acts/M-8) to assist the Minister.

## [PART XV]{.HLabel1}[Sanctions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-101997 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

- **[[465]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-465
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, without reasonable
  cause, contravenes any provision of this Act or the regulations is
  guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False or misleading information

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who knowingly provides false or
  misleading information in relation to any matter under this Act or the
  regulations is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undue preference to creditor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every director, officer or employee of an association
  who wilfully gives or concurs in giving to any creditor of the
  association any fraudulent, undue or unfair preference over other
  creditors, by giving security to the creditor, by changing the nature
  of the creditor's claim or otherwise, is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to provide information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, without reasonable cause, refuses
  or fails to comply with a requirement made under paragraph 437(2)(b)
  is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of name

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except to the extent permitted by the regulations,
  every person who uses the name of an association in a prospectus,
  offering memorandum, takeover bid circular, advertisement for a
  transaction related to securities or in any other document in
  connection with a transaction related to securities is guilty of an
  offence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 465
- 2007, c. 6, s. 185
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-465-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 465"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

- **[[466]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-466
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is guilty of an
  offence under any of subsections 465(1) to (4) is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a natural person, liable

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine not exceeding
      \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding twelve
      months, or to both, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
      exceeding \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding
      five years, or to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, liable

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine not exceeding
      \$500,000, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
      exceeding \$5,000,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to comply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person has been convicted of an offence under
  this Act, the court may, in addition to any punishment it may
  otherwise impose, order the person to comply with the provisions of
  this Act or the regulations in respect of which the person was
  convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional fine

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person has been convicted of an offence under
  this Act, the court may, if it is satisfied that as a result of the
  commission of the offence the convicted person acquired any monetary
  benefits or that monetary benefits accrued to the convicted person or
  their spouse, common-law partner or other dependant, order the
  convicted person to pay, despite the maximum amount of any fine that
  may otherwise be imposed under this Act, an additional fine in an
  amount equal to three times the court's estimation of the amount of
  those monetary benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 466
- 1997, c. 15, s. 161
- 2000, c. 12, s. 85
- 2005, c. 54, s. 209
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-466-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 466"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of officers, directors, etc.

**[[467]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-467 .sectionLabel}** Where an entity commits
an offence under this Act, any officer, director or agent of the entity
who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or participated in
the commission of the offence is a party to and guilty of the offence
and liable on summary conviction or on conviction on indictment to the
punishment provided under paragraph 466(1)(a) for the offence, whether
or not the entity has been prosecuted or convicted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 467
- 1997, c. 15, s. 162
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

- **[[467.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-467.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Proceedings by way of summary
  conviction in respect of an offence under a provision of this Act may
  be commenced at any time within, but not later than, two years after
  the day on which the subject-matter of the proceedings became known,
  in the case of an offence under a consumer provision, to the
  Commissioner and, in any other case, to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of Superintendent or
  Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document appearing to have been issued by the
  Superintendent or Commissioner, as the case may be, certifying the day
  on which the subject-matter of any proceedings became known to the
  Superintendent or Commissioner is admissible in evidence without proof
  of the signature or official character of the person appearing to have
  signed it and is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of
  the matter asserted in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 340
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of offence on contracts

**[[468]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-468 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
expressly provided in this Act, a contravention of any provision of this
Act or the regulations does not invalidate any contract entered into in
contravention of the provision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance or restraining order

- **[[469]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-469 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an association or any director, officer, employee or agent of an
  association does not comply with any provision of this Act or the
  regulations other than a consumer provision, or of the incorporating
  instrument or any by-law of the association, the Superintendent, any
  complainant or any creditor of the association may, in addition to any
  other right that that person has, apply to a court for an order
  directing the association, director, officer, employee or agent to
  comply with --- or restraining the association, director, officer,
  employee or agent from acting in breach of --- the provision and, on
  the application, the court may so order and make any further order it
  thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance or restraining order ---
  consumer provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a retail association or any director, officer,
  employee or agent of a retail association does not comply with any
  applicable consumer provision, the Commissioner or any complainant
  may, in addition to any other right that that person has, apply to a
  court for an order directing the retail association, director,
  officer, employee or agent to comply with --- or restraining the
  retail association, director, officer, employee or agent from acting
  in breach of --- the consumer provision and, on the application, the
  court may so order and make any further order it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 469
- 2001, c. 9, s. 341
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal of final order

- **[[470]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-470 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province from any final order
  made by a court of that province under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal with leave

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province
  from any order, other than a final order made by a court of that
  province, only with leave of the court of appeal in accordance with
  the rules applicable to that court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 48, s. 470
- 2005, c. 54, s. 210
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-470-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 470"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery and application of fines

**[[471]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-471 .sectionLabel}** All fines payable under
this Act are recoverable and enforceable, with costs, at the suit of Her
Majesty in right of Canada, instituted by the Attorney General of
Canada, and, when recovered, belong to Her Majesty in right of Canada.
